  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法评估协议不一致，且过度强调预测性能而忽视可靠性；短期血糖风险预测对主动糖尿病管理至关重要，临床部署前亟需可比的、多维度可靠性评估。

Method: 构建锚点式基准框架，融合纵向血糖序列与多模态患者信息；基于T1DiabetesGranada的736名1型糖尿病患者生成5,503,441个标准化预测锚点，覆盖30/60/120分钟预测时域；在相同患者级验证协议下比较Hybrid LSTM-Transformer、Multimodal Digital Twin与带缺口感知状态更新的State-Updating Digital Twin；评估指标包括预测性能、校准、分类阈值、解释可靠性、跨模型分歧及受控扰动鲁棒性。

Result: 经Holm校正后模型间预测性能无统计学显著差异；Multimodal Digital Twin平均Brier分数最低，State-Updating Digital Twin平均ECE-10最低，Hybrid LSTM-Transformer在其时间归因协议下解释忠实度与稳定性较强，各模型呈互补的可靠性特征。

Conclusion: 数字孪生模型的评价应超越预测精度，纳入校准、解释与鲁棒性等可靠性维度，并在临床部署前开展外部验证；所提锚点式基准框架为可比的、多维度模型评估提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Anticipating short-term hypoglycemic and hyperglycemic risk is essential for proactive diabetes management. However, existing machine learning approaches often use inconsistent evaluation protocols and emphasize predictive performance over reliability. This study introduces an anchor-based benchmarking framework integrating longitudinal glucose sequences and multimodal patient information. Using the T1DiabetesGranada dataset, 5,503,441 standardized prediction anchors were constructed from 736 individuals with type 1 diabetes to evaluate hypoglycemia and hyperglycemia prediction at 30-, 60-, and 120-minute horizons. Three neural architectures, a Hybrid LSTM-Transformer, a Multimodal Digital Twin, and a State-Updating Digital Twin with gap-aware longitudinal state progression, were compared under identical patient-level validation protocols. Beyond predictive performance, the evaluation examined calibration, classification thresholds, explanation reliability, cross-model disagreement, and robustness to controlled perturbations. No statistically significant predictive differences were observed after Holm correction, although the models exhibited complementary reliability characteristics. The Multimodal Digital Twin achieved the lowest mean Brier score, while the State-Updating Digital Twin demonstrated the lowest mean expected calibration error (ECE-10). The Hybrid LSTM-Transformer showed strong explanation faithfulness and stability under its temporal attribution protocol. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating digital-twin models beyond predictive accuracy and emphasize the need for reliability assessment and external validation before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [70] [SpecLST: Spectral-Enhanced Long-Short Term Spatio-Temporal Multimodal Modeling for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36393)
*Kenan Kang,Matthew M. Y. Kuo,Weihua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpecLST——频率增强的长短期时空多模态框架，用FFT频域编码多周历史依赖、注意力融合异构上下文、稀疏MoE骨干与图增强，并以频率增强残差头捕捉高频瞬态异常，在60分钟预测上显著优于八个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市路网多尺度动态建模对智能交通系统至关重要，但受限于长程历史上下文的计算开销与异构模态融合难题；现有时空框架在序列长度与内存开销间存在权衡，且常忽视交通事故、天气等外生因素的瞬态影响。

Method: 1) 基于快速傅里叶变换的频域表示模块，高效编码多周历史依赖，规避原始长序列上标准注意力的内存瓶颈；2) 注意力融合机制整合异构上下文；3) 稀疏混合专家（MoE）骨干结合图增强进行时空表示学习；4) 频率增强残差头分离高频瞬态成分，提升对突发交通异常的敏感性。

Result: 在60分钟预测时域上，跨8个神经预测基线与4个数据集，MAE、RMSE、MAPE平均相对误差分别降低14.10%、10.35%、15.78%；按数据集与指标细分的平均降幅介于5.63%至22.21%。

Conclusion: 频域建模与MoE+图增强及频率残差头的组合可有效缓解长序列内存瓶颈并提升对外生瞬态因素的敏感性，实现长时域交通预测的稳定精度提升。

Abstract: Modeling multi-scale dynamics in urban road networks is essential for intelligent transportation systems but is often hindered by the computational cost of long-range historical context and the challenge of fusing heterogeneous modalities. Existing spatio-temporal frameworks face a trade-off between sequence length and memory overhead, while frequently neglecting the transient effects of exogenous factors such as road incidents and weather. To address these limitations, we propose SpecLST, a frequency-enhanced long-short-term spatio-temporal multimodal framework. SpecLST leverages a fast Fourier transform-based frequency-domain representation module to efficiently encode multi-week historical dependencies, bypassing the memory bottlenecks of standard attention mechanisms over raw long sequences. Heterogeneous context is integrated through attention-based fusion, followed by a sparse Mixture-of-Experts backbone with graph augmentation for spatio-temporal representation learning. Additionally, a frequency-enhanced residual head isolates high-frequency transients, improving sensitivity to abrupt traffic anomalies. At the 60-minute horizon, SpecLST achieves mean relative error reductions of 14.10% in MAE, 10.35% in RMSE, and 15.78% in MAPE across eight neural forecasting baselines and four datasets, with the dataset- and metric-specific average reductions ranging from 5.63% to 22.21%.

</details>


### [71] [When Can a Learned Design Surrogate Be Trusted in a Self-Driving Microfluidics Laboratory? An Offline Closed-Loop Framework Built and Tested on Six Public Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36395)
*Zhiyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可测量的自驱动实验室可信度框架（能力比、预算k*(ε)、到达时间、定价决策），在六个公开液滴微流控/脂质体数据表构成的107个离线"实验室"层级上检验，得到23条发现；暖启动残差提升可在20个目标样本内恢复代理模型能力，闭环中"立即提议+逐次暖启动"策略显著优于参照与随机提议，但无免标签统计量能认证单个候选、无阈值规则优于持续升级策略，且未做湿实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室换到新目标域时必须回答四个问题：何时可信、需要多少目标测量、先测什么、何时停止迁移/切换模型或升级。现有工作缺乏将这些量化为可测量指标的统一框架，也缺乏无湿实验的严格离线评估。

Method: 构建四问框架并给出四个可测度量（competence ratio、预算k*(ε)、time-to-target、priced decision）；以六个公开液滴微流控与脂质体数据表作离线实验室，构造107个留一组（leave-one-group）层级（行标签隐含）；采用密封预注册方案（两次密封负验证＋三表预注册测试）与七个冻结闭环协议各运行两次；按漂移类型分层评估，并设置漂移与标签打乱对照、随机划分模型族排序对照。

Result: 漂移成本按类型分层；无免标签统计量可认证单个候选；随机划分的模型族排序既不能预测密封排序也不能预测闭环排序。暖启动残差提升在94%化学层级（严格噪声底读法下27%）、90%实验室层级、40%器件层级中于20个目标行内使代理模型回到开发参照的1.25×以内；跨表按漂移类型规划的预算在新表上无可测劣化。闭环中仅一项增益在漂移与打乱标签对照下全层级存活，且由稀疏采样层级承载：立即提议并对每次测量暖启动，在10%目标下用2.80次测量（参照3.31），在3%容差下301个困难单元上为5.72对比随机的11.35。无免标签采集规则通过打乱标签对照，区间感知提议及其他模型族均无改进，在升级定价后无阈值规则优于始终继续。

Conclusion: 该框架使四问可测量并可在无湿实验条件下系统检验；暖启动残差提升与"立即提议+逐次更新"是稳健可迁移的增益，尤其利于稀疏采样层级；但个体候选认证、免标签采集与升级阈值决策缺乏可靠信号。作者明确不声称任何湿实验验证。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories couple a learned design surrogate to an instrument, and on every new target domain it must answer four questions: when it can be trusted, how many target measurements it needs, which to make first, and when to stop adapting, switch models or escalate. We state a framework making each measurable (a competence ratio, a budget $k^*(\varepsilon)$, a time-to-target and a priced decision) and instantiate it without wet experiments on six public droplet-microfluidic and liposome tables as offline laboratories: 107 leave-one-group tiers whose rows are hidden labels. A sealed, pre-registered programme (two sealed negative validations and a pre-registered test on three more tables) and seven frozen closed-loop protocols, each run twice, yield 23 findings. Shift cost is stratified by shift type; no label-free statistic certifies individual candidates; random-split model-family rankings predicted neither the sealed nor the loop rankings. Warm-start residual boosting returns a surrogate to $1.25\times$ its development reference within 20 target rows in 94% of chemistry tiers (27% under a strict noise-floor reading), 90% of laboratory and 40% of device tiers, and a budget planned from the shift type on other tables is not measurably worse on a new one. In the loop, one gain survives drift and shuffled-label controls on all tiers, carried by the sparsely sampled ones: proposing at once and warm-starting on every measurement reaches a 10% target in 2.80 measurements against 3.31 for the reference, and in 5.72 against 11.35 for random proposals on 301 hard units at a 3% tolerance. No label-free acquisition rule survives the shuffled-label control, no interval-aware proposal or other model family does better, and no threshold rule on the tested diagnostics beats always continuing once escalation is priced. No wet-lab validation is claimed.

</details>


### [72] [Persistence, Exposure, and Credit Routing in Linear Temporal Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36412)
*Akira Tanase,Julian Gould,Jeffrey Seely*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用细胞层论（cellular sheaf theory）工具分析线性时间预测编码（tPC），揭示时间信用分配是空间位移误差与时间误差之间的“拔河”，其比例由持久算子P与暴露算子Q调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tPC 提供了一种层级局部（layer-local）的、可替代反向传播的机制，但其时间信用分配的内在结构尚缺乏理论刻画，本文旨在从数学结构层面阐明线性 tPC 中信用分配的机制与固有限制。

Method: 将细胞层论应用于线性 tPC，把时间信用分配分解为空间位移误差与时间误差两类分量的竞争，并引入持久算子 P 与暴露算子 Q 刻画其比例调制；推导空间推断下的有效循环转移 F = PA，以及未来信用经 P* 滤波的关系。

Result: 得到三项结论：(1) 空间推断给出有效循环转移 F = PA，未来信用由 P* 滤波；(2) 在非放大不变循环下，空间模态存在持久—误差权衡：长时延上保持影响的模态无法将该影响强烈地表现为时间误差；(3) 通过持久模态与暴露模态之间的路由（routing）可以规避该权衡。

Conclusion: 线性 tPC 的时间信用分配本质上是持久性与可暴露性之间的权衡，并可由循环在两类模态间的路由予以化解，为理解层级局部学习的信用分配提供了结构化的理论框架。

Abstract: Temporal Predictive Coding (tPC) networks offer a layer-local alternative to backpropagation. For linear tPC, we show, by applying tools from cellular sheaf theory, that temporal credit assignment is a tug-of-war between spatial displacement and temporal errors, whose ratios are modulated by persistence and exposure operators $P$ and $Q$. Spatial inference therefore gives us the effective recurrent transition $F=PA$, whilst future credit is filtered by $P^\ast$. This creates a persistence--error tradeoff in spatial modes: under non-amplifying invariant recurrence, modes that preserve influence over long delays simultaneously fail to expose that influence strongly as temporal error. Recurrence can circumvent this via routing between persistent and exposed modes.

</details>


### [73] [Repulsive Population Policy Gradients for Behaviorally Diverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36425)
*Saeed Hedayatian,Harsh Shah,Celine Mneimneh,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RePPO，用种群依赖的动态塑形奖励改造 PPO，一次性联合优化一个紧凑策略种群，在连续控制、像素输入与 LLM 微调上实现可扩展的质量-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QD-RL 方法主要依赖进化算子（黑箱优化、变异、交叉）来促进探索与多样性，随策略规模与行为空间增大，计算开销急剧上升，难以扩展到大规模策略（如像素输入、大语言模型）。

Method: 基于 Soft QD 视角，将 PPO 扩展为对紧凑种群进行联合策略梯度优化：为每个个体构造依赖种群（population-dependent）、动态塑形的奖励，从而在不使用进化算子的情况下同时优化个体质量与集体多样性；并通过消融实验分析各组件对个体质量与集体多样性的影响。

Result: 在标准连续控制任务上取得与 SOTA QD-RL 方法相当的强质量-多样性权衡；在像素任务中，传统 QD-RL 因策略过大而不可行时，RePPO 质量与标准 PPO 持平且多样性显著更高；首次成功应用于 LLM 微调，证明 QD-RL 可扩展到该领域。

Conclusion: 直接用 RePPO 优化紧凑种群是一种可扩展的 QD-RL 方案，可随域复杂度提升（连续控制→像素→LLM）保持质量-多样性权衡，摆脱对昂贵进化算子的依赖。

Abstract: The goal of Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning (QD-RL) is to find a population of high-performing policies that solve a decision-making problem in diverse ways. Despite recent progress, QD-RL methods predominantly rely on evolutionary operators--including black-box optimizers, mutations, and crossovers--to facilitate exploration and induce diversity. Scaling these methods becomes increasingly costly as policies and behavior spaces grow larger. Building on the Soft QD view of quality-diversity optimization, we introduce \textbf{Re}pulsive \textbf{P}opulation \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{RePPO}), which adapts PPO to jointly optimize a compact population of diverse, high-quality policies using a population-dependent, dynamically shaped reward. On standard continuous-control tasks, RePPO achieves a strong quality-diversity tradeoff compared with state-of-the-art QD-RL methods. Ablations further characterize this tradeoff by examining how each component affects individual quality and collective diversity. To assess RePPO's scalability, we extend our evaluation to learning from pixels and fine-tuning large language models. In pixel-based tasks, where large policies make most traditional QD-RL methods infeasible, RePPO maintains quality on par with standard PPO while achieving substantially higher diversity. Its successful application to language model fine-tuning further demonstrates, for the first time, that QD-RL methods can scale to this domain. Together, these results show that directly optimizing compact populations with RePPO provides a scalable approach to QD-RL across increasingly complex domains.

</details>


### [74] [Property-Based Testing for Robust Softmax and Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36427)
*Muhammad Maaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用Hypothesis属性测试复现了Barron等(2026)指出的softmax与交叉熵数值缺陷，发现其修复方案仍存在额外边界失效情形，并提出一种更鲁棒且支持因果注意力掩码的softmax实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习库（PyTorch、JAX等）中的softmax与交叉熵存在已被Barron等(2026)识别的数值问题，但其修复实现是否完备、能否覆盖更多边界情形尚不明确，且缺乏系统性的自动化测试手段。

Method: 采用基于Hypothesis的属性测试（property-based testing）方法，将数值正确性需求表述为通用性质并进行自动化检验；据此复现已知缺陷、发现新的边界失效案例，并设计新的softmax实现，同时支持因果注意力掩码。

Result: 属性测试成功复现了Barron等所述的两类数值问题，并额外识别出一个其实现失效的边界情形；所提实现通过全部属性测试，且在因果注意力掩码场景下可用，与Barron等实现存在若干实现差异。

Conclusion: 属性测试是发现并修复机器学习库数值问题的有效工具；所提softmax实现相较现有方案更为鲁棒且适用面更广，代码与测试已开源。

Abstract: A recent paper of Barron et. al. (2026) identified two numerical issues with the softmax and cross-entropy functions in virtually all standard machine learning libraries (e.g., PyTorch and JAX). We show that these issues can be recovered through simple property-based tests in Hypothesis. However, our tests identify an additional edge case where their implementation fails. We then propose an even more robust softmax implementation which passes these tests and also supports use in causal attention masking. We compare our implementation with theirs and discuss some of the differences. Overall, this work shows that property-based testing can be a powerful tool for discovering and fixing numerical issues in machine learning libraries. Our implementations and property-based tests can be found at https://github.com/mmaaz-git/softmax-fix.

</details>


### [75] [Simulated petrological labels enable probabilistic mineralogy prediction from whole-rock geochemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36429)
*Matthew J. Liu,Aaron D. Kaplan,Michael L. Whittaker,Kristin A. Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用约百万条模拟岩石学标签训练"Transformer自监督编码+模拟标注头+重校准层"三段式模型，实现从全岩化学成分零样本预测模态矿物学，25个标注样本即可恢复大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实测模态矿物学（矿物种类与丰度）测量耗时、通量低；全岩化学数据通量高但矿物学预测是一对多反问题，且带标注矿物学数据集规模过小，无法大规模监督训练。

Method: 构建三段式模型：(1) Transformer编码器在约788,000条全岩成分上自监督预训练；(2) 在约100万条模拟标签上训练预测头，输出矿物组合的分布（模拟标签可表达同一成分的多种成岩条件）；(3) 重校准层将预测与实测矿物学对齐。

Result: 在640块火山岩上零样本测试，模型可判别多数矿物类别的有无、并预测各岩石中哪种矿物更富集；但丰度预测存在系统性偏移。仅用25块标注岩石校准即恢复全量368块校准集76%的似然增益；丰度误差零样本11.0%，全校准集9.5%（下降较慢）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明地球化学机器学习可同时利用不完美模拟器并缓解其系统偏差，为标注稀缺场景下的一对多反问题提供可行范式。

Abstract: Modal mineralogy, the mineral identities and abundances in rocks, guides geological studies and industrial resource extraction. While measuring modal mineralogy is time-intensive, whole-rock chemical measurements furnish elemental concentrations at higher throughput. Predicting mineralogy from elemental data, however, poses a one-to-many inverse problem, and labeled mineralogical datasets are too sparse to train on at scale. We address both challenges by simulating petrological labels that express multiple formation conditions per composition and at a scale unattainable by measurements. Nearly one million simulated labels supervise the middle component of a three-part model: a transformer encoder self-supervised on roughly 788,000 whole-rock compositions, the simulation-trained heads that predict distributions over mineral assemblages, and a recalibration layer that aligns the predictions with measured mineralogy. Zero-shot on 640 volcanic rocks, the model discriminates the presence of most mineral categories and predicts which rocks are richer in each mineral. However, the predicted abundances are systematically offset from measured values. Recalibration fit on as few as 25 labeled rocks recovers 76 percent of the likelihood improvement from fitting on the full 368-rock fitting set, while abundance error responds more slowly (11.0% zero-shot and 9.5% with the full fitting set). This work demonstrates how a machine learning model in the geochemical sciences can both leverage an imperfect simulator and mitigate its systematic biases.

</details>


### [76] [Learning atomic interactions for the thermodynamics of liquid mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36440)
*Marco Hoffmann,Sebastian Schmitt,Hans Hasse,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COGNAC：一种图神经网络，学习原子级几何与相互作用参数并在UNIFAC框架内计算，兼顾高精度与低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活度系数模型是过程设计与优化的基础。UNIFAC等基团贡献法成本低、已集成于流程模拟器但精度有限；HANNA等机器学习模型精度高、适用广，但难以低成本嵌入流程模拟。

Method: 构建图神经网络COGNAC，学习依赖分子邻域的原子级几何参数与相互作用参数，并将其代入UNIFAC框架评估活度系数；使用约82万条实验数据点训练。

Result: 在二元及多组分混合物上精度显著优于UNIFAC与HANNA，相对HANNA误差降低13–63%；可靠预测多相平衡并识别共沸物；参数可解释；严格热力学一致；计算成本与UNIFAC相当。

Conclusion: COGNAC将机器学习的精度与UNIFAC级计算成本结合，释放了ML模型用于过程模拟的潜力。

Abstract: Predictive models for activity coefficients, which describe the non-ideality of liquid mixtures, are foundational for process design and optimization. Group-contribution methods, such as UNIFAC, are computationally cheap and well established in process simulators. Modern machine learning models, such as HANNA, offer higher accuracy and broader applicability, but their integration into process simulation is expensive or even infeasible. In this work, we introduce COGNAC, a graph neural network that learns atom-level geometry and interaction parameters that depend on the molecular neighborhood and evaluates them in the UNIFAC framework. Trained on about 820,000 experimental data points, COGNAC achieves significantly higher accuracy than UNIFAC and HANNA for binary and multi-component mixtures, reduces error relative to HANNA by 13-63%, reliably predicts multi-phase equilibria and identifies azeotropes, yields interpretable parameters, and is strictly thermodynamically consistent. COGNAC evaluations run at a cost comparable to UNIFAC, thereby unlocking the potential of machine-learning models for process simulations.

</details>


### [77] [High-Fidelity Aerostructural Dataset of Transonic Wings in Cruise Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36442)
*Pavankumar Koratikere,Abhijnan Dikshit,Leifur Leifsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个面向跨声速机翼静气动弹性分析的高保真多学科数据集，含2471个样本与3个分布外样本，并提供机器学习基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开机翼设计数据集主要聚焦气动性能，较少考虑实际设计中气动与结构的多学科耦合；高效机翼设计需同时兼顾气动性能与结构完整性，而尚无公开数据集在高保真层面刻画这种耦合的气动弹性行为。

Method: 构建跨声速机翼静气动弹性分析数据集：平面形状由展长、后掠、上反、梢弦、梢扭转参数化，翼型截面采用类别形状变换（CST）方法参数化；分析工况由马赫数、攻角、高度与结构载荷因子参数化；并用先进机器学习模型预测气动表面量、积分气动性能量与翼盒失效分布。

Result: 机器学习模型预测气动量误差最高为0.0309 NRMSE，预测翼盒失效分布误差最高为0.1189 NRMSE。

Conclusion: 该数据集成对多样化跨声速机翼提供耦合的气动与结构响应，为多物理场机翼设计机器学习模型的开发、评估与比较建立基准，并鼓励在更广泛工程领域构建类似高保真多物理场基准数据集。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning (ML) have the potential to substantially accelerate aircraft wing design, a process that has traditionally required extensive high-fidelity simulation and design iteration. Existing public datasets for ML-based wing design have focused primarily on aerodynamic performance, with limited consideration of the multidisciplinary interactions that govern practical aircraft design. In particular, efficient wing design requires simultaneous consideration of aerodynamic performance and structural integrity, yet no publicly available datasets capture this coupled aerostructural behavior at high-fidelity. To address this need, a high-fidelity multidisciplinary dataset for static aerostructural analysis of transonic wings is introduced. The dataset includes 2471 samples, along with three out-of-distribution samples. The wing planform geometry is parameterized by span, sweep, dihedral, tip chord, and tip twist, while the airfoil cross-sections are parameterized using the class-shape transformation method. The analysis is further parameterized by Mach number, angle of attack, altitude, and structural load factor. The utility of the dataset is demonstrated by training state-of-the-art ML models to predict aerodynamic surface quantities, integrated aerodynamic performance quantities, and the failure distribution of the wingbox. ML models achieve at most 0.0309 normalized root mean squared error (NRMSE) in the prediction of aerodynamic quantities and at most 0.1189 NRMSE in the prediction of failure distribution of the wingbox. By providing coupled aerodynamic and structural responses for a diverse set of transonic wings, this dataset establishes a benchmark for developing, evaluating, and comparing multi-physics ML models for wing design. Broadly, it is intended to encourage creation of similar high-fidelity multi-physics benchmark datasets across engineering domains.

</details>


### [78] [Split Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36465)
*Jia Chen,Yecheng Lv,Xinrong Hu,Mingdi Huang,Jun Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统定义分裂联邦遗忘（SFU），涵盖客户端级/类别级移除、反事实重训练与分片特定更新约束；理论上刻画历史影响传播与模式相关的可遗忘性，并提出 SURE 方法，在较小额外开销下逼近模式匹配的重训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂联邦学习（SFL）结合分片模型执行与联邦聚合以支持分布式 Web/边缘协同学习，但由于跨分片耦合与双侧聚合导致的历史影响传播，去除已学习贡献十分困难，且该问题此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 首先形式化定义 SFU 问题（客户端级与类别级遗忘、反事实重训练、分片特定更新约束）；其次从理论上分析历史影响传播机制与依赖模式（更新模式）的可遗忘性可行性，揭示受限模型更新的结构性局限；最后提出 SURE 初始方案，融合双重重要性估计、选择性参数扰动与重要性引导的恢复。

Result: 将现有遗忘方法适配到 SFL 的广泛实验表明，在不同模型架构、数据分布与更新模式下均存在显著的遗忘-效用权衡；SURE 以适度额外成本达到与模式匹配重训练高度一致的效果；进一步分析揭示分片位置与更新约束对遗忘行为的影响。

Conclusion: SFU 应被视为一个独立的研究问题，本文为其奠定了形式化、理论与方法基础，并为分片式联邦系统中的遗忘研究提供参考。

Abstract: Split federated learning (SFL) combines partitioned model execution with federated aggregation to support collaborative learning across distributed Web and edge environments. However, removing learned contributions in SFL is challenging due to cross-partition coupling and historical influence propagation through dual-side aggregation. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first systematic formulation of Split Federated Unlearning (SFU), encompassing client-wise and class-wise removal, counterfactual retraining, and partition-specific update constraints. We theoretically characterize historical influence propagation and mode-dependent unlearning feasibility, revealing structural limitations of restricted model updates. As an initial solution, we propose SURE, integrating dual-importance estimation, selective parameter perturbation, and importance-guided recovery. Extensive experiments with existing unlearning methods adapted to SFL reveal substantial forgetting-utility trade-offs across model architectures, data distributions, and update modes. SURE achieves competitive agreement with mode-matched retraining at modest additional cost, while further analyses highlight the impact of split position and update constraints on unlearning behavior. These findings establish SFU as a distinct research problem and provide a foundation for unlearning in partitioned federated systems.

</details>


### [79] [CoHARL: Co-Evolving Agent Policies and Executable Harnesses with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36469)
*Jianwen Chen,Peng Xia,Kaide Zeng,Siwei Han,Zhen-Yu Zhang,Charles Fleming,Xinyu Ye,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoHARL，一种基于种群的强化学习框架，在策略训练过程中协同进化共享策略与一组可执行 harness，使接口与其生成训练经验同步适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体通过可执行 harness 与环境交互，harness 决定工具调用与反馈方式。固定策略下优化 harness，会忽略策略在学习中行为的变化；而固定 harness 训练策略，则使生成训练经验的接口保持不变。二者割裂，因此需要在训练全程自适应调整 harness。

Method: 采用种群式强化学习：维护一个共享策略和一个由多个可执行 harness 组成的种群共同进化。策略从跨种群的 rollout 中学习；同时一个 proposer 利用执行轨迹在训练中修订 harness 代码。

Result: 在 SWE-rebench 上取得 27.6% 的平均解决率，较仅做 harness 搜索提升 6.4 个百分点；在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，软件工程与终端类任务均有增益。在同等训练预算下，优于"先搜索一次 harness 再做 RL"以及"策略更新与单一活跃 harness 修订交替进行"两种基线。

Conclusion: 策略与 harness 的协同进化优于两者分离或单次优化，验证了将接口纳入训练循环的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with their environments through executable harnesses that govern tool use and feedback. Optimizing a harness for a fixed policy overlooks how its behavior changes during learning, while training a policy under a fixed harness leaves the interface that generates its training experience unchanged. This motivates adapting the harness throughout policy training. We introduce CoHARL, a population-based reinforcement learning framework that co-evolves a shared policy and a population of executable harnesses. The policy learns from rollouts across the population, and a proposer uses its execution traces to revise harness code during training. CoHARL achieves a 27.6% mean resolved rate on SWE-rebench, improving over harness-only search by 6.4 percentage points. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0 show gains in software engineering and terminal-based tasks. Under matched training budgets, CoHARL also outperforms searching for a harness once before RL and alternating policy updates with revisions to a single active harness.

</details>


### [80] [Underdamped Anchored Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36470)
*Qi Feng,Hoang M. Nguyen,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于动量（欠阻尼）锚定Langevin动力学UALD及其离散化算法UALMC，首次为非可微与重尾目标提供无渐近偏置的动能Langevin采样方法，并给出2-Wasserstein距离下的非渐近收敛保证，维度与精度依赖优于过阻尼锚定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ULA等过阻尼Langevin类算法依赖可微对数密度，且对重尾分布采样效率低；已有针对非可微、重尾目标的采样方法均局限于过阻尼框架，缺少动量型（动能）方法。

Method: 构造欠阻尼锚定Langevin动力学UALD：以光滑参考势替换原势（锚定），并在漂移项中引入乘性缩放修正欠阻尼Langevin动力学；基于该动力学的离散化方案设计UALMC算法。

Result: 在2-Wasserstein距离下建立到目标分布的非渐近收敛保证，所导出的迭代复杂度在维度和精度上的依赖优于基于过阻尼的锚定Langevin算法；数值实验验证理论与实际表现。

Conclusion: 将锚定思想从过阻尼推广到欠阻尼（动量）框架，首个对非可微与重尾目标无渐近偏置的动能Langevin算法，兼具理论复杂度优势与实用性能。

Abstract: Classical Langevin Monte Carlo algorithms, such as the unadjusted Langevin algorithm (ULA), are based on discretization schemes of the overdamped Langevin dynamics and are widely used for sampling in machine learning because they scale to high dimensions and large datasets. However, they require differentiable log-densities, and are less efficient for sampling heavy-tailed distributions. Although various approaches have been proposed in the literature for sampling non-differentiable and heavy-tailed targets, all of them are developed in the overdamped setting only. We propose a novel momentum-based underdamped anchored Langevin dynamics (UALD), and underdamped anchored Langevin Monte Carlo (UALMC) algorithm based on a discretization scheme of UALD. Our paper is the first to provide a kinetic (momentum-based) Langevin algorithm for non-differentiable and heavy-tailed targets that is free of asymptotic bias. The method replaces the original potential with a smooth reference potential and modifies the underdamped Langevin dynamics via multiplicative scaling in the drift term. We establish non-asymptotic guarantees in the 2-Wasserstein distance to the target distribution and show that the resulting iteration complexity has better dependence on dimension and accuracy level compared to overdamped-based anchored Langevin algorithms. We provide numerical experiments to illustrate the theory and practical performance of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [81] [Proposing Better Rollouts for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36477)
*Sirui Chen,Yunzhe Qi,Haobo Xu,Jiaru Zou,Mengting Ai,Zihao Li,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPD中固定用学生自身采样的rollout策略会造成教师-学生不匹配的复合放大，作者将rollout策略本身作为优化对象，提出MPOPD：由局部信赖域目标导出学生锚定、教师引导的几何混合分布并用以近似该混合的proposal policy生成轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPD通常将rollout policy视为固定，即每轮直接从当前学生采样。作者指出该做法会让教师被迫对任何学生采样轨迹（包括教师自身难以到达的轨迹）提供监督，导致监督噪声甚至误导，引发重复等不良模式；且更新后的学生又定义下一轮rollout策略，使问题在训练中持续并可能放大。因此仅改蒸馏目标不够，需把rollout策略本身当作优化目标。

Method: 基于相对教师支持度（relative teacher support）构造局部信赖域目标，推导出学生锚定、教师引导的rollout分布，其解为学生策略与教师策略的几何混合；进而引入一个proposal policy来近似该混合分布，使rollout可在单次前向传播中完成生成。

Result: 在16个数学、知识及医学推理基准上，方法稳定优于OPD及近期变体；在Qwen3-4B-Base的数学推理上，平均准确率较OPD提升6.91，同时平均生成长度下降41%。

Conclusion: rollout策略设计是提升OPD的有效途径；将rollout分布作为优化对象可缓解教师-学生不匹配及由此引发的重复等问题，在精度与生成效率上同时受益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model post-training by letting the student learn from its own sampled trajectories under teacher supervision, aligning the training process with inference-time behavior. However, the rollout policy in OPD is usually treated as fixed: each round samples directly from the current student. We show that this choice can create a compounding teacher--student mismatch, because it requires the teacher to provide effective supervision on whichever trajectories the student samples, including ones less reachable by the teacher itself. On such trajectories, teacher supervision can become noisy or even misleading, leading to undesirable student patterns such as repetition. Moreover, since the updated student in turn defines the rollout policy of subsequent rounds, these patterns persist and potentially amplify over training. This failure mode suggests that improving OPD requires treating the rollout policy itself as an optimization target, rather than only modifying the distillation objective. We propose Mixture Proposal On-Policy Distillation (MPOPD), which derives a student-anchored, teacher-guided rollout distribution from a local trust-region objective based on relative teacher support. The resulting target is a geometric mixture of the student and teacher policies, and we introduce a proposal policy that approximates this mixture, enabling rollout generation in a single forward pass. Experiments on 16 math, knowledge, and medical reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over OPD and recent variants. On math reasoning, our method improves average accuracy by 6.91 over OPD on Qwen3-4B-Base while reducing average generation length by 41%, suggesting that rollout policy design provides an effective way to improve OPD.

</details>


### [82] [PRISM: Pathology Reliability In Scarce-label Medicine. A Tile-level Benchmark of Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36517)
*Alicem Koyun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PRISM：一个瓦片级病理基础模型可靠性基准，联合评估模型选择、标签预算、校准与跨机构分布外迁移四个维度，共 864 次线性探针实验与 288 个校准单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型评测通常只在全标签、同分布测试集上看分类性能，缺少对校准性、低标签预算行为与跨机构分布偏移鲁棒性的联合评估，难以反映真实部署中的可靠性。

Method: 瓦片级基准：8 个基础模型（CLIP、PLIP、CONCH、UNI、Virchow2、GigaPath、H-Optimus-0、Midnight）× 6 个瓦片数据集 × 6 个标签比例（1%–100%）× 3 个随机种子，共 864 次线性探针；并扩展至 Camelyon17 五家医院、20 个有向迁移对；以 AUROC、ECE、温度缩放等指标做秩相关与异质性（I²）分析。

Result: (i) 全监督下 AUROC 排名可稳定预测温度缩放后校准排名（ρ=0.52，I²=0%），1% 标签时关系变为数据集特异（ρ=-0.24，I²=67%）；(ii) 较难数据集在低于某数据集特定标签比例时线性探针坍缩为单一预测类别；(iii) 迁移 AUROC 低于约 0.85 时，增加源域标签在 86–88% 组合中反而恶化目标域 ECE；(iv) 288 个单元中有 49 个温度缩放未能降低 ECE，误校准以欠置信为主，迁移失败时用源域拟合温度有害。

Conclusion: 病理基础模型的分类精度排名不能可靠外推为校准可靠性；低标签与跨机构迁移条件下校准行为显著退化，需将校准与迁移联合纳入模型选择。PRISM 以 pip 可安装包形式发布，并提供全部 288 个单元的参考结果。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are typically benchmarked on classification performance at full label availability and on in-distribution test sets, while calibration, behavior at low label budgets, and robustness under cross-institutional shift are seldom evaluated jointly. We introduce PRISM, a tile-level benchmark that measures the reliability of pathology foundation models along four axes: model choice, label fraction, calibration, and out-of-distribution transfer. PRISM evaluates eight foundation models (CLIP, PLIP, CONCH, UNI, Virchow2, GigaPath, H-Optimus-0, Midnight) on six tile-level datasets at six label fractions (1% to 100%) with three seeds (864 linear probe runs), extended with a five-hospital Camelyon17 study of 20 directed transfer pairs. Four phenomena emerge. (i) Under full supervision, AUROC rank predicts post-temperature calibration rank consistently across datasets (Spearman rho = 0.52, I^2 = 0%), whereas at 1% labels the relationship becomes dataset-specific (rho = -0.24, I^2 = 67%). (ii) On harder datasets the linear probe collapses to a single predicted class below a dataset-specific label fraction. (iii) Whether additional source-domain labels help or harm target calibration tracks how well the probe transfers: below a transfer AUROC of roughly 0.85, more labels worsen target ECE in 86-88% of combinations. (iv) Temperature scaling fails to reduce ECE in 49 of 288 cells, miscalibration is predominantly under-confidence, and a source-fitted temperature is harmful under failing transfer. PRISM is released as a pip-installable package with reference results for all 288 cells. Code and data: https://github.com/alicemkyn/prism-benchmark

</details>


### [83] [Folding Attention Biases into Keys Makes Compacted KV Caches Run on Standard Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36520)
*Utkarsh Kanwat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将Attention Matching的逐键logit偏置折叠为每个head一个闭式方向并入key，得到可直接由FlashAttention/vLLM服务的普通KV cache，精度与AM相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attention Matching（AM）通过保留每head部分key、重拟合value并给每个保留key的logit加偏置来压缩KV cache，但FlashAttention与vLLM的paged cache无法接收这种额外输入，导致工程上难以部署。

Method: 把偏置沿每个head的一条闭式方向折叠进key（该方向在上下文query上拟合），使cache恢复为普通格式：尺寸与head维度同AM，查询路径仍用模型自身的query；输出误差由每个query在该方向上的投影偏离1的程度界定。

Result: 等per-head预算下，未修改的vLLM经KV connector服务折叠cache：Qwen3-4B全部51题与Hugging Face一致，Qwen3-14B LongBench 200样本中180个贪心输出一致；QuALITY与LongHealth共14个设置中13个与AM精度无显著差异，12个可对比设置中KL均低于丢弃偏置；在公开per-head预算下10个设置中9个与AM无显著差异，KL不再优于丢弃偏置。

Conclusion: 折叠使AM类压缩可直接部署于现有注意力内核，精度基本保持，但在更紧/公开预算下其相对简单丢弃偏置的优势减弱。

Abstract: Attention Matching (AM) compacts a KV cache by keeping some keys per head, refitting their values and adding a bias to each kept key's logit. FlashAttention and vLLM's paged caches have no input for it. We fold the bias into the keys along one closed-form direction per head, fitted on context queries, giving a plain cache with AM's size and head dimension and the model's own query path; the output error is bounded by how far each query's projection on that direction is from 1. With equal per-head budgets, unmodified vLLM serves folded caches through a KV connector, matching Hugging Face's answers on all 51 Qwen3-4B questions and its greedy output on 180 of 200 Qwen3-14B LongBench samples. With equal budgets on QuALITY (Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, 8B, Llama-3.1-8B) and LongHealth (three Qwen3 models), the fold is within noise of AM's accuracy in 13 of 14 settings, with lower KL to the full cache than dropping the bias in all 12 where both ran. Under the published per-head budgets, on Qwen3-4B, its Instruct-2507 variant and Llama-3.1-8B, it is within noise of AM in 9 of 10, no longer beats dropping the bias in KL, and the two each win once in accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [NextEval: An Agent-Based Solver and Benchmark for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36553)
*Shuo Chen,Li Cui,Cunxin Huang,Ya-Feng Liu,Minghang Xie,Meng Xu,Zaikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM智能体的无导数优化求解器NextEval，通过"LLM智能体+数值环境"协同，在有限评估预算下自适应选择并配置数值方法以高效选点，并配套基准协议NextEval Bench，在低维DFO问题上函数评估效率优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFO场景中导数不可得或代价高，黑箱目标评估本身昂贵，核心难题是如何在有限预算下充分利用已积累的观测来选择有信息量的评估点。

Method: 构建LLM智能体与数值环境的耦合系统：环境提供历史评估分析工具与数值方法执行工具，并维护共享评估历史与方法级状态以支持方法切换后的延续；LLM智能体依据观测与工具反馈选择工具、配置参数、分配评估预算，候选点由数值方法计算与评估。同时定义NextEval Bench基准协议，在匹配评估预算与信息约束下用相同数值工具对比不同智能体配置。

Result: 在低维DFO问题上，NextEval在函数评估效率上优于当前最先进的DFO基线。

Conclusion: 将LLM智能体作为元决策层调度传统数值方法，可有效提升预算受限下的DFO评估效率，并提供可复现、匹配预算与信息条件的基准评估范式。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses problems where derivatives are unavailable or costly to obtain. This setting often arises in simulation-based and experimental applications, where evaluating the black-box objective can itself be costly. Thus, it remains a central challenge in DFO to effectively exploit accumulated observations to select informative evaluation points under a limited budget. In this light, we introduce the NextEval solver, an agent-based DFO solver, which integrates the capabilities of a large language model (LLM) to guide the sequential selection of evaluation points. Specifically, we establish a system that combines an LLM agent with a numerical environment. The environment provides tools for analyzing past evaluations and executing numerical methods, while maintaining a shared evaluation history and method-specific state to support continuation across method switches. Using observations and tool feedback, the LLM agent selects tools, configures their parameters, and allocates the evaluation budget, while numerical methods compute and evaluate candidate points. Building on this system, we also introduce NextEval Bench, a benchmark protocol for comparing agent configurations on shared DFO tasks using the same numerical tools under matched evaluation budgets and information constraints. Numerical results on low-dimensional DFO problems demonstrate that the NextEval solver outperforms the state-of-the-art DFO baselines in function-evaluation efficiency.

</details>


### [85] [Learning from Points and Patches: A Pre-trained Hybrid Attention Transformer for Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36572)
*Peiwang Tang,Cheng HE,Yupeng Ye,Weitai Zhang,Qinghua Sun,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TimeHAT通用时间序列预训练框架，从"学什么"（统一点-片段掩码策略UPPMS）与"怎么学"（混合注意力编码器HAE）两方面设计，在分类、长期预测、插补、异常检测四类任务上取得有竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web服务与互联系统产生的时间序列支撑用户活动分析、服务监控与资源管理，但异构时间序列的可迁移表征学习困难：时间模式多样、序列长度可变、高质量标注稀缺。NLP与CV中预训练虽成功，时间序列预训练仍需针对时序数据定制的策略。

Method: TimeHAT由两部分组成：(1) UPPMS（统一点-片段掩码策略）在统一框架内融合点级与片段级监督，同时进行上下文重建与因果外推，兼顾细粒度时序变化与宏观时序模式；(2) HAE（混合注意力编码器）在浅层使用前缀约束的双向注意力，在深层使用方向性混合注意力，在保留有效上下文交互的同时缓解秩退化（rank degeneration）。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上评测四类下游任务（分类、长期预测、插补、异常检测），覆盖微调、少样本与零样本迁移设置，各任务均表现出有竞争力的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 针对时序数据特性的定制化预训练（掩码监督设计与注意力结构设计）是提升跨任务、跨场景可迁移表征的有效途径；TimeHAT作为通用框架在多种时序分析任务与迁移设定下具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Time-series data generated by Web services and connected systems support applications such as user activity analysis, service monitoring, and resource management. However, learning transferable representations from heterogeneous time series remains challenging due to diverse temporal patterns, variable sequence lengths, and limited high-quality annotations. Despite the success of pre-training in natural language processing and computer vision, effective time-series pre-training requires strategies tailored to temporal data.
  We propose TimeHAT (Hybrid Attention Transformer), a general-purpose pre-training framework for time-series analysis that addresses two complementary questions: what to learn and how to learn. For what to learn, we introduce a Unified Point-Patch Masking Strategy (UPPMS) that integrates point-level and patch-level supervision within a unified framework for contextual reconstruction and causal extrapolation, enabling the model to capture both fine-grained temporal variations and broader temporal patterns. For how to learn, we develop a Hybrid Attention Encoder (HAE) that combines prefix-constrained bidirectional attention in initial layers with directional hybrid attention in deeper layers, aiming to preserve effective contextual interactions while mitigating rank degeneration.
  We evaluate TimeHAT across real-world datasets and four downstream tasks: classification, long-term forecasting, imputation, and anomaly detection. Experiments cover fine-tuning, few-shot, and zero-shot transfer settings, demonstrating competitive performance across diverse time-series analysis tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/TangPeiwang/TimeHAT.

</details>


### [86] [Less is more: learning what to profile for GPU kernel optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36576)
*Yihan Wang,Peiyu Liu,Runyu Chen,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoProfiler，用优化反馈学习一棵确定性的 profiling 树，为每个 kernel 自适应选择最有用的 profiling 信息，在有限 profiling 预算下提升 LLM 的 GPU kernel 优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的 GPU kernel 优化系统依赖预定义 profiling 策略，把"profiling 什么"排除在优化过程之外。作者观察到：profiling 指标数量增加并不单调提升 kernel 优化效果，且最有用的 profiling 信息因 kernel 而异（异构 profiling 效用），因此固定配置存在次优性。

Method: 理论上证明在异构 profiling 效用下，kernel 条件化的 profiling 选择可优于任何单一固定配置。据此提出 AutoProfiler：从优化反馈中学习一棵确定性 profiling 树，通过 REWRITE 与 SPLIT 操作迭代精炼，由 LLM 生成候选修改、经验评估决定是否接受；树构建完成后对任意 kernel 快速采集所需 profiling 信息，LLM rewriter 仅需调用一次。

Result: 在 KernelBench 上，于受限 profiling 预算条件下，该方法相比固定 profiling 配置提升了 kernel 优化效果，验证了 kernel 条件化 profiling 选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 硬件 profiling 应从静态指标采集转向"profiling 推理"，使 profiling 本身成为提升 kernel 优化性能的一个新维度。

Abstract: Large language models have recently shown strong potential for automatically optimizing GPU kernels, yet their effectiveness depends critically on the hardware information available to the kernel optimization model. Existing systems typically rely on predefined profiling strategies, leaving what to profile largely outside the optimization process. We show that effective profiling is not simply about collecting more information: increasing the number of profiling metrics does not monotonically improve kernel optimization, while the most useful profiling information varies across kernels. Our theoretical analysis further shows that, under such heterogeneous profiling utility, kernel-conditioned selection can outperform any single fixed profiling configuration. Motivated by this observation, we propose AutoProfiler, a feedback-driven framework for learning a deterministic profiling tree from optimization feedback. The tree is iteratively refined through REWRITE and SPLIT operations, with an LLM generating candidate modifications and empirical evaluation determining their acceptance. Once constructed, the tree quickly gathers the required profiling information for any kernel, allowing the LLM rewriter to be invoked only once. Experiments on KernelBench show that our approach improves kernel optimization over fixed profiling configurations under constrained profiling budgets. These results suggest that hardware profiling should evolve from static metric collection toward profiling reasoning, making profiling itself a new dimension for improving kernel optimization. The codes are available at https://github.com/Satissss/AutoProfiler.

</details>


### [87] [MM-Net: A Multimodal Multi-Task Network for Sleep Staging and Sleep Apnea Assessment After Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36583)
*Md Wahiduzzaman Suva,Esm E Moula Chowdhury Abha,Md Imtiaj Alam Sajin,Md Iftekharul Mobin,Jia Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MM-Net多模态多任务框架，通过cross-fitting与ridge嵌入对齐解决预训练EEG编码器的表征不一致问题，同时实现睡眠分期与呼吸事件检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺血性卒中后的自动多导睡眠图分析困难：睡眠相关神经活动改变且并发呼吸紊乱；现有卒中专用方法多只做睡眠分期，缺乏睡眠结构与呼吸事件的联合分析；此外适配预训练EEG编码器后，下游训练与评估患者不一致会引入表征不一致问题。

Method: MM-Net融合睡眠适配的EEG-EOG表征与工程化神经描述符，并与学习到的呼吸特征通过cross-attention及共享时序建模结合；采用cross-fitting和基于ridge的嵌入对齐来消除表征不一致。

Result: iSLEEPS（99例亚急性缺血性卒中）十折患者独立评估：分期准确率76.3%（κ=0.656），呼吸事件AUC 0.825，优于匹配协议下的基线。对齐后睡眠适配编码器使分期准确率较冻结编码器提升1.4个百分点（p=0.020），未对齐仅0.2点。Sleep-EDF上分期准确率86.4%，优于所有重实现的单EEG架构；ISRUC-I上80.3%分期准确率与0.889呼吸AUC。iSLEEPS训练模型无需重训即在ISRUC-I与UCD上分别保持0.833和0.841的呼吸AUC。

Conclusion: 结果支持表征一致的神经适配与跨卒中、健康睡眠及睡眠障碍队列的联合生理建模的有效性。

Abstract: Automated polysomnography after ischemic stroke is challenging because of altered sleep-related neural activity and concurrent respiratory disturbances. Existing stroke-specific approaches mainly address sleep-stage classification, leaving joint analysis of sleep architecture and respiratory events underexplored. Moreover, adapting pretrained EEG encoders introduces a representation inconsistency when downstream models are trained on embeddings from previously seen patients but evaluated on unseen individuals. We propose MM-Net, a multimodal multi-task framework that resolves this inconsistency through cross-fitting and ridge-based embedding alignment. Sleep-adapted EEG-EOG representations and engineered neural descriptors are fused with learned cardiorespiratory features through cross-attention and shared temporal modeling for simultaneous sleep staging and respiratory-event detection. On iSLEEPS (99 patients with subacute ischemic stroke), ten-fold patient-independent evaluation achieved 76.3% staging accuracy (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.656) and 0.825 respiratory-event AUC, outperforming the evaluated baselines under matched protocols. The sleep-adapted encoder improved the downstream model when its embeddings were aligned, raising staging accuracy by 1.4 percentage points over the frozen encoder ($p$ = 0.020) versus 0.2 points without alignment. MM-Net also achieved 86.4% staging accuracy on Sleep-EDF, above every single-EEG architecture reimplemented on the same recordings, and 80.3% staging accuracy with 0.889 respiratory AUC on ISRUC-I, exceeding its retrained staging baselines. Without retraining, the iSLEEPS-trained model retained respiratory AUCs of 0.833 on ISRUC-I and 0.841 on UCD. These findings support representation-consistent neural adaptation and joint physiological modeling across stroke, healthy-sleep, and sleep-disorder cohorts.

</details>


### [88] [Efficient Quantization Training with Stochastic Rounding via Structured Subspace Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36587)
*Zeyu Wang,Yiheng Duan,Tao Chen,Donghyun Koo,Sihan Liu,Wenbo Zhang,Chuan He,Liang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出结构化子空间随机舍入（S3R）及其误差校正版本S3R-EC，通过在每个token的隐藏维度上共享单个随机阈值，解决了逐元素独立随机舍入导致向量单元吞吐瓶颈的问题，并在LLM预训练中实现与独立SR相当的收敛和显著的INT8训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度训练依赖随机舍入（SR）保持量化梯度无偏并防止小更新被舍入为零，但标准SR为每个梯度元素独立采样阈值。加速器仅能在吞吐远低于矩阵单元的向量单元上生成随机数，逐元素采样会饱和向量单元，使INT8+SR训练甚至慢于BF16训练。而简单地在张量元素间共享阈值虽消除该开销，却迫使这些元素同向舍入并引入共同偏移的舍入误差，损害训练。

Method: 作者首先诊断出共享阈值带来的公共偏移主要通过激活梯度反向传播到浅层，且浅层无法用自身局部舍入误差抵消该偏移，因此必须在产生偏移的层内消除。据此提出S3R：对上游梯度只量化一次，同时用于激活梯度与权重梯度两次矩阵乘法，并在每个token内沿隐藏维度共享单个随机阈值。其误差校正S3R-EC从激活梯度和权重梯度中减去结构化的平均舍入误差，同时保持优化器不变。

Result: 在LLM预训练实验中，未校正的阈值共享会导致收敛退化；引入S3R-EC后该退化被消除，验证损失与独立SR相匹配。此外，在TPU v5p上，S3R-EC使稠密LLM的完整INT8训练步骤较BF16加速1.24倍至1.32倍，较独立SR加速1.11倍。

Conclusion: 通过将随机阈值的共享结构化到token级并配合针对性误差校正，可在几乎不损失收敛质量的前提下大幅降低随机数生成开销，使INT8训练真正优于BF16，为低精度训练中随机性与硬件效率的矛盾提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low-precision training often relies on stochastic rounding (SR) to keep quantized gradients unbiased and prevent small updates from rounding to zero. Standard SR draws an independent random threshold for every gradient element. However, accelerators generate random numbers on vector units whose throughput lags far behind their matrix units. Drawing one threshold per element saturates the vector unit and can make INT8 training with SR slower than BF16 training. Sharing random thresholds across tensor elements removes this cost but forces those elements to round in the same direction and shifts their rounding errors by a common offset. We find that this offset degrades training primarily through the activation gradient, which propagates the error backward into earlier layers. Because earlier layers cannot cancel an incoming offset using their own local rounding errors, the offset must be removed at the layer where it originates. We propose Structured Subspace Stochastic Rounding (S3R), which quantizes each upstream gradient once for both the activation and weight gradient matrix multiplications and shares a single threshold per token across the hidden dimension. Its error correction, S3R-EC, subtracts the structured mean rounding error from the activation and weight gradients while leaving the optimizer unchanged. In LLM pre-training experiments, uncorrected threshold sharing degrades convergence, whereas S3R-EC eliminates this degradation and matches the validation loss of independent SR. Furthermore, S3R-EC accelerates full INT8 training steps of dense LLMs on TPU v5p by 1.24x to 1.32x over BF16 and by 1.11x over independent SR.

</details>


### [89] [Normalized Cuts and Data Clustering via Matrix Fractional Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36616)
*Yannan Chen,Beichen Huang,Licheng Zhao,Chris Ding,Kaiming Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多维二次变换（multidimensional quadratic transform），将多类归一化割（NCut）问题从分式规划（FP）视角重新刻画为二分匹配问题并用线性搜索求解，同时建立其与MM理论的联系并分析收敛性，在聚类与图像分割任务上验证优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCut是数据聚类的核心问题，具有分式结构，但现有文献很少从分式规划（FP）角度处理。原因在于传统FP方法不适用：经典Dinkelbach变换只能处理单个分式比值，仅限两类聚类，而多类聚类涉及多个分式项。

Method: 提出多维二次变换，用于处理多比值（multi-ratio）情形，将NCut问题重构为二分匹配问题，该问题可直接通过线性搜索求解；并进一步探讨该FP方法与minorization-maximization（MM）理论之间的联系，据此刻画算法的收敛行为。

Result: 在多种数据聚类与图像分割任务上开展实验，数值上验证了所提基于FP的算法的优势。

Conclusion: 多维二次变换突破了Dinkelbach变换仅限单比值、两类的局限，为多类NCut提供了统一的FP求解框架；其求解可归结为二分匹配与线性搜索，且可从MM理论角度获得收敛性保证，实验表明该算法具有实际优越性。

Abstract: The normalized cut (NCut) problem is a fundamental one in the area of data clustering. Despite its fractional structure, the NCut problem is seldom considered from a fractional programming (FP) perspective in the existing literature. The main reason is that the conventional FP method is inadequate for this problem case: the classical Dinkelbach's transform can only handle a single ratio and hence is limited to the two-class clustering, whereas the multi-class clustering involves multiple ratio terms. This work proposes a novel so-called multidimensional quadratic transform that is capable of dealing with the multi-ratio case; it recasts the NCut problem into a bipartite matching problem---which can be immediately solved by linear search. Furthermore, we explore the connection between the proposed FP method and the minorization-maximization (MM) theory, and in turn characterize the convergence behavior of our algorithm. Finally, we conduct experiments to numerically demonstrate the advantages of the proposed FP-based algorithm in a variety of data clustering and image segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [90] [Balanced State Subspaces for Mamba-2 State Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36617)
*Shang Tingyu,Cao Wenfei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mamba-2 的状态压缩应针对"写—读子空间"而非独立坐标：作者提出基于平衡截断的后训练变换方法，并配合按层分配秩的 HARP 策略，在 25% 保留率等苛刻条件下显著优于 GHOST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba-2 将所有上下文压缩进固定大小的循环状态，该状态主导解码显存与带宽。现有压缩器（量化状态值或剪枝状态坐标）在原生基下逐通道独立打分，但作者发现：训练后的状态能量近似秩一，却集中在由循环累积产生的偏置式方向上，而输出实际读取的内容位于一个低维子空间，且与状态坐标方向不对齐；即便采用输出感知打分，逐坐标计算也会遗漏决定输入—输出映射的关键方向。

Method: 提出一种后训练变换方法：以完整可控性 Gramian 与可观测性 Gramian 实例化平衡截断，并借助 Mamba-2 的标量—恒等转移结构精确施加旋转；同时引入 HARP，依据 Hankel 谱为每层分配秩，并保护对检索敏感的层。标定仅需一次前向传播。

Result: 标定速度比 GHOST 公开协议快一个数量级，峰值显存更低。在 130M–2.7B 规模上，配合 HARP 将状态维度减半时，WikiText-2 困惑度平均仅上升 0.3 点（1.8%），而 GHOST 为 1.6 点（9.3%）；在 25% 保留率下差距进一步扩大；在困惑度与检索指标上均优于均匀预算分配。

Conclusion: 状态压缩的有效目标应是写—读（可控性—可观测性）子空间，而非单个坐标；平衡截断结合层级秩分配可在低标定成本下实现高压缩率与低性能损失，且优势在激进压缩下更为明显。

Abstract: Mamba-2 compresses all context into a fixed-size recurrent state that dominates decoding memory and bandwidth. Existing compressors quantize state values or prune state coordinates, scoring each channel independently in the native basis. We examine trained Mamba-2 states and find that energy is almost rank-one, but the output does not read the energetic directions: energy concentrates in a bias-like direction accumulated by the recurrence, while the content the output reads lives in a low-dimensional subspace that is not aligned with the state's coordinates. Even output-aware scores are computed coordinate by coordinate, so they miss the directions that determine the input--output map. Compression should therefore target the write--read subspace rather than individual coordinates. We propose \method{}, a post-training transform that instantiates balanced truncation with full controllability and observability Gramians and applies the rotation exactly via Mamba-2's scalar-identity transition. Because the useful rank varies widely across layers, we further introduce HARP, which allocates ranks per layer from the Hankel spectrum and protects retrieval-sensitive layers, improving both perplexity and retrieval over uniform budgets. Calibration takes a single forward pass, an order of magnitude faster than GHOST's released protocol, with lower peak memory. With HARP, \method{} halves the state dimension at an average WikiText-2 perplexity increase of $0.3$ points ($1.8\%$) across 130M--2.7B, versus $1.6$ ($9.3\%$) for GHOST, and the gap widens at 25\% retention.

</details>


### [91] [Dream-COP: Guiding Algorithm Discovery for Combinatorial Optimization Problems in an Experience World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36618)
*Kuan Xu,Jianming Wang,Ni Zhang,Yaoxin Wu,Yong Wang,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dream-COP 提出用"经验世界"中的回放（dreaming）让策略智能体学习可执行的探索策略，用以指导发现智能体生成与精化组合优化程序，在五种 COP 上以更少搜索开销发现更优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的算法发现依赖迭代式程序生成与评估，核心难题是在有限预算下如何组织搜索以发现更好的算法。作者观察到以往搜索轨迹及其记录结果构成了可复用的"经验世界"，但固定探索策略并未利用这一信息。

Method: 构建 Dream-COP 框架：将历史搜索轨迹与评估结果组织为经验世界；在该经验世界中通过"dreaming"/策略回放训练一个策略智能体（policy agent），学习可执行的探索策略；策略回放对已生成程序的质量与所耗搜索步数提供反馈，从而更有效地进行策略选择；学到的策略用于指导发现智能体（discovery agent）生成与精化 COP 程序。

Result: 在五类 COP 上，Dream 在启发式组件设计与完整求解器生成两方面均优于固定探索；在 KP 上达到最优值，在 OBPP 上将差距由 2.112% 降至 1.199%，同时减少 42.5% 的搜索请求；所学探索策略借助经验世界可有效跨不同 COP 迁移。

Conclusion: 将搜索历史显式建模为可回放的经验世界，并以策略学习指导 LLM 算法发现，能够提升搜索效率与解质量，且具备跨问题迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable algorithm discovery for combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) through iterative program generation and evaluation. A central challenge is how to organize search to discover better algorithms within a limited budget. Our key insight is that past search trajectories and their recorded outcomes form an experience world, which can be used to facilitate the COP solving through replay. We introduce Dream-COP, in which a \emph{policy agent} learns executable policies through \emph{dreaming} in this experience world to guide a \emph{discovery agent} in generating and refining COP programs. Specifically, policy replay provides feedback on the quality of the programs revealed and the search steps used, to guide policy selection more effectively. Across five COPs, \textsc{Dream} discovers better algorithms than fixed exploration in both heuristic component design and complete solver generation. Particularly, it matches the optimal values on KP and reduces the gap on OBPP from 2.112\% to 1.199\% while using 42.5\% fewer search requests. Moreover, the learned exploration policies transfer effectively across different COPs with the assitance of the experience world.

</details>


### [92] [The Batch Size Can Be One: Test-Time Adaptation from Classifier Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36628)
*Jaeho Hwang,Yujin Oh,Juhyeon Shin,Juhye Lee,Sungroh Yoon,Uiwon Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OriTTA从源模型构造固定的几何目标（新原点、类原型轴与理想点），在测试时将选中特征向预测类轴对齐并惩罚半径过大，配合按预测类保留样本的记忆与崩溃重置机制，在batch size=1的连续测试流上取得最优平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线测试流逐样本到达且类序不定，单样本难以估计分布级统计；按置信度选样会纳入"自信但错误"的预测。而数据损坏只平移特征、不改变冻结分类器的决策区域，因此可用源模型提供的固定几何结构作为稳定监督信号。

Method: 1) 以每个类一个合成原型构造原型集，选取使各类得分更接近、且靠近这些原型特征均值的新原点；2) 从新原点出发，各类原型方向分布更均匀，为每类预先确定一条类轴与一个理想点（测试前离线完成）；3) 适配时将被选样本特征与其预测类轴对齐，并对径向距离施加惩罚；4) 按预测类各保留一个输入样本作为记忆，约束流中未出现类的特征漂移；5) 当记忆样本重分类结果显示崩溃时重置模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C上，无需逐设置调参，于四种骨干网络×两种协议×两种流顺序共16种组合中均取得batch size=1下最高平均精度；ImageNet-C fog上，近期方法在100次运行中最多有三分之二发生崩溃，而OriTTA从未崩溃。

Conclusion: 利用源模型固定决策区域所隐含的原点几何结构，可为单样本在线适配提供稳定、免调参的几何监督，显著提升精度并避免模型崩溃。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation updates a deployed model on an unlabeled test stream. In practice this stream often arrives one sample at a time, with the classes in no particular order. A single sample then provides limited information for estimating distribution-level statistics, and selecting samples by confidence can admit predictions that are wrong yet confident. A corruption, however, shifts the features while leaving the decision regions of the frozen classifier unchanged. We therefore propose Origin-recentered Test-Time Adaptation (OriTTA), which constructs fixed geometric targets from the source model and adapts selected features toward them. Seen from the origin of the feature space, the classes lean toward a common direction. We choose a new origin to bring class scores closer together while keeping it near the mean feature of one synthetic prototype per class. From this origin, prototype directions spread more evenly, and each class obtains an axis and an ideal point before any test data arrives. During adaptation, OriTTA aligns selected features with their predicted class axis and penalizes excessive radius, keeps one input per predicted class in memory to limit feature changes for classes absent from the stream, and resets the model when re-classifying this memory reveals collapse. Without tuning for each setting, OriTTA achieves the highest mean accuracy at batch size 1 on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C and ImageNet-C in all sixteen combinations of four backbones, two protocols and two stream orders. On ImageNet-C fog, recent methods collapse in up to two thirds of one hundred runs, while OriTTA never does.

</details>


### [93] [Deep Bayesian Approaches for Interpretable Nonlinear Structural System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36633)
*Wei Liu,Konstantinos Vlachas,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该教程系统介绍面向非线性结构系统识别的深度贝叶斯滤波，以Deep Markov Model为数据驱动基准，对比PgDMM与Neural EKF两种互补范式，并在含Bouc-Wen滞回的3层剪切框架基准上考察状态估计与状态-参数联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯滤波依赖预设的状态转移与观测模型，难以刻画未知非线性动力学；而纯数据驱动方法又丧失物理结构与不确定性量化能力。因此需要系统梳理"学习到的动力学—物理归纳偏置—后验推断"三者如何交互，为工程诊断、预测与决策提供可用的识别框架。

Method: 采用教程式综述与基准实验相结合的方式：以Deep Markov Model作为数据驱动参照，以基于摊销变分推断的Physics-guided Deep Markov Model和基于可微Kalman滤波的Neural Extended Kalman Filter作为互补方法，在具有滞回Bouc-Wen连接和稀疏加速度测量的三维剪切框架基准上开展动态状态估计及状态与参数联合估计。

Result: 结果揭示了若干关键区别：测量条件下的重构与开环预测性能存在显著差异；引入物理结构可提升隐状态可解释性，但并不必然保证预测保真度；响应估计与参数估计在鲁棒性上表现不同。

Conclusion: 论文给出模型构建、训练、评估与部署的实践指南，并指出不确定性校准、可辨识性及真实世界验证仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: System identification, namely the inference of mathematical representations of dynamical systems from measured data, is fundamental to engineering diagnostics, prognostics, and decision-making. Bayesian filtering offers a principled framework for probabilistic state estimation but traditionally relies on prescribed transition and observation models. Recent advances in deep Bayesian filtering address this limitation by embedding learnable representations within probabilistic state-space formulations, enabling data-driven identification of unknown dynamics while retaining physical structure and uncertainty-aware inference.
  This tutorial provides a systematic introduction to deep Bayesian filtering for nonlinear structural system identification, focusing on the interplay between learned dynamics, physical inductive biases, and posterior inference. The Deep Markov Model serves as a data-driven reference, while the Physics-guided Deep Markov Model (PgDMM) and Neural Extended Kalman Filter (Neural EKF) illustrate complementary approaches based on amortized variational inference and differentiable Kalman filtering, respectively. Their capabilities are examined through dynamic state estimation and joint dynamic state and parameter estimation on a three-dimensional shear-frame benchmark with hysteretic Bouc-Wen connections and sparse acceleration measurements. The results highlight important distinctions between measurement-conditioned reconstruction and open-loop forecasting, demonstrate how physical structure can improve latent-state interpretability without necessarily ensuring predictive fidelity, and reveal differences in the robustness of response and parameter estimation. Practical guidelines for model construction, training, evaluation, and deployment are provided, together with an outlook on remaining challenges in uncertainty calibration, identifiability, and real-world validation.

</details>


### [94] [When Does Perturbation Consistency Add Information Beyond Softmax Confidence? Theory and Evidence for the Decision Consistency Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36640)
*Amit Kalitaa,Moirangthem Tiken Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扰动一致性框架DCF（标签稳定性LS+置信度偏差CD→一致性分数CS→CAG），系统检验一致性分数相对干净softmax输出（MSP）何时提供额外信息：总体上CS判错能力不如MSP，但在噪声与对抗扰动下具有独特价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扰动一致性分数常被用来判断预测是否稳健，但缺乏对其相对干净softmax输出是否带来增量信息的严格检验。作者希望界定一致性分数"有信息"与"无信息"的边界条件。

Method: 构建Decision Consistency Framework：在六种轻度扰动算子下计算标签稳定性（LS）与置信度偏差（CD），合成一致性分数CS，并用准确率与平均一致性之差CAG刻画模型。开展201次评估（每个配置3个随机种子），跨多种模型族比较CS与MSP的误分类检测AUROC；理论上推导标签翻转所需的logit margin不等式，并据此构造无标签的split-conformal“不翻转”证书；在CIFAR-10-C噪声腐蚀与PGD对抗攻击下做预注册验证，并分析OCTMNIST上的CAG。

Result: 所有模型族中CS检测干净数据误分类的能力均弱于MSP；冻结CNN上AUROC差0.057（95%CI 0.043–0.073），计算量高8–15倍。理论表明标签翻转要求logit margin≤√2倍logit位移，固定margin下位移预测错误的AUROC仅0.515；由此给出的无标签split-conformal不翻转证书在84次运行中从未被违反。在置信度被误导的场景CS有增益：CIFAR-10-C噪声腐蚀下优于margin；PGD攻击（预算2/255）下区分干净与受攻击输入的AUROC为0.83，MSP仅0.37（符合预注册）。但噪声下的增益无法迁移到未见过的分布偏移族。OCTMNIST上大幅负CAG在全微调与域内训练下依然存在，部分源于类别先验效应，先验校正可恢复约40%的差距。

Conclusion: 一致性分数并非干净置信度的替代品：在干净数据上其判错能力系统性弱于MSP且代价更高，理论上由margin–位移不等式限制了其信息量。其价值仅限于置信度失效的特定扰动（噪声腐蚀、对抗攻击），且增益不跨偏移族泛化；异常CAG部分由类别先验解释而非稳健性缺失。

Abstract: Perturbation consistency scores judge whether a prediction survives small input changes. The Decision Consistency Framework (DCF) scores each image by label stability (LS) and confidence deviation (CD) under six mild operators, combines them into a consistency score (CS), and summarises a model by the gap between accuracy and mean consistency (CAG). We ask when these scores carry information that the clean softmax output lacks. Across 201 evaluations with three seeds per configuration, CS detected misclassifications on clean data less well than the maximum softmax probability (MSP) in every model family. For frozen CNNs, the deficit was 0.057 in area under the ROC curve (AUROC; 95% CI 0.043 to 0.073), at 8 to 15 times the compute. A label flip requires the logit margin to be at most the square root of two times the logit displacement, and at a fixed margin, the displacement predicted errors at AUROC 0.515. The same inequality gives a label-free split-conformal no-flip certificate, never violated in 84 runs. Consistency did add information where confidence is misled: under noise corruptions of CIFAR-10-C, it beat the margin, and under projected gradient attacks, it separated clean from attacked inputs with AUROC 0.83 against 0.37 for MSP at a budget of 2/255, as pre-specified. The gain under noise did not transfer to unseen shift families. The large negative CAG on OCTMNIST persists under full fine-tuning and in-domain training and is partly a class-prior effect: correcting the prior recovers about 40% of the gap.

</details>


### [95] [When Is a Cheap Fairness Edit Safe? Screening Post-Hoc Edits in Two-Sided Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36649)
*Moirangthem Tiken Singha,Rabinder Kumar Prasada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在两方匹配市场（如招生）中通过闭式线性投影器在推理时降低受保护属性的影响，并对保持垂直有序的市场给出平均福利变化的界，配套提出包含垂直性检验与福利扫描的部署前筛查流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低受保护属性对匹配结果的影响通常需要重训练编码器，成本高；同时缺乏判断后处理编辑是否可采纳的可靠性保证。

Method: 提出闭式线性投影器在推理时编辑嵌入；对编辑前后仍保持垂直有序的市场，用编辑引起的吸引力偏移加显式余项界定平均福利变化，并用数据依赖的偏差项给出概率化界；提出PASS/KILL/OUT-OF-SCOPE三级筛查（垂直性检验+福利扫描），分精确层级与分级层级。

Result: 30个留出合成市场上筛查与全部预设判定一致且无误报PASS；60个精确垂直市场上无界被违反，概率界在69%–87%配置中有信息量；3个真实市场的2700个配置中无插件式界被违反；闭式因式分解在90%–100%实例中落在分级范围，而两塔与图神经网络编码器全部超出。

Conclusion: 架构选择影响后处理编辑是否可采纳：闭式因式分解更易满足可采纳条件，可作为部署前的可靠性筛查依据。

Abstract: Two-sided matching systems, such as admissions pipelines, allocate opportunities jointly. Reducing a protected at tribute's influence usually requires encoder retraining. We study a closed-form linear projector that does this at inference time. For markets that stay vertically ordered before and after the edit, we bound the mean welfare change by the edit-induced appeal shift and an explicit remainder. A probabilistic refinement replaces worst-case constants with a data-dependent deviation term. A pre-deployment screen combines a verticality test with a welfare sweep and returns PASS, KILL, or OUT-OF-SCOPE. An exact tier requires every opportunity to rank applicants by appeal, and a graded tier admits near-vertical markets. On 30 held-out synthetic markets, the screen agrees with every pre-specified regime verdict, with no observed false PASS. On 60 exactly vertical markets, no bound is violated, and the probabilistic bound is informative in 69% to 87% of configurations. On 2700 configurations from three realistic markets, which do not reach the exact tier, no plug-in bound expression is violated. A closed-form factorization stays within graded scope in 90% to 100% of instances, whereas two-tower and graph neural network encoders fall outside it in every instance. Architecture choice thus appears to affect whether a post-hoc edit is admissible.

</details>


### [96] [TACO-Graph: Automated Construction of Text-Atributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36650)
*Amiri Hayes,Kristina Wicke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TACO-Graph，一种基于决策树的框架，用于学习在给定数据集和任务下哪些文本属性图（TAG）构图选择最有效，并以可解释的方式预测构图质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构文本表示在机器学习中应用广泛，但如何从给定数据集中系统性地为特定任务构建图仍不明确。节点、边、节点文本的选择会显著影响下游性能，而寻找最优构图通常依赖专家知识或人工试错。

Method: 针对由多属性表格数据构建的文本属性图，TACO-Graph枚举有效的TAG表示，在小型代表性子集上进行快速代理评估打分，再基于这些分数训练可解释决策树。在五个数据集（引文网络、产品评论、文献关系）上验证，覆盖节点分类与GraphRAG检索任务。

Result: 在独立抽取的评估样本上，决策树预测构图质量的平均Spearman ρ在节点分类任务上超过0.9，在GraphRAG检索任务上超过0.8；决策树能系统性识别在GNN与GraphRAG任务上表现更优的构图方式。完整流程已开源。

Conclusion: TACO-Graph为将表格数据转换为面向下游任务的文本属性图提供了系统且可解释的指导，并作为开源库支持新任务与新数据集的评估扩展。

Abstract: Graph-structured representations of text data have become foundational across machine learning, yet it remains unclear how to systematically construct a graph from a given dataset for a target task. Choices about which entities become nodes, which relationships become edges, and what text each node contains can substantially affect downstream performance. In practice, finding optimal constructions often requires expert knowledge or manual trial and error. We introduce TACO-Graph, a decision tree framework for learning which graph construction choices are most effective for a given dataset and task. Specifically, we focus on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) constructed from multi-attribute tabular data. TACO-Graph enumerates valid TAG representations, scores each using fast proxy evaluation on small representative subsets, and trains an interpretable decision tree on those scores. Across five datasets spanning citation networks, product reviews, and bibliographic relationships, our decision trees predict construction quality on separately drawn evaluation samples with mean Spearman $ρ$ above $0.9$ for node classification and above $0.8$ for GraphRAG retrieval. Our trees systematically identify which constructions perform better across both GNN and GraphRAG tasks, and the full pipeline is released as an open-source library to support evaluation on new tasks and datasets at https://github.com/AmiriHayes/text_attributed_graphs. TACO-Graph provides a systematic and interpretable guide for determining how to convert tabular data to text-attributed graphs for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Log-Derivative Trick for Parametric Markov Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36655)
*Arthur Gall,Ichiro Hasuo,Thomas Seiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于测度论推广的对数导数技巧的蒙特卡洛梯度估计器，沿采样轨迹纯黑盒计算局部转移概率的导数，无偏估计参数化马尔可夫链的期望奖励与可达概率梯度，避免全局状态空间物化和线性系统求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化马尔可夫链（pMC）中期望奖励与可达概率优化是含环概率系统分析与综合的核心。传统符号方法（parameter lifting、有理函数生成）存在可扩展性瓶颈；现有梯度方法仍需在全局状态空间上做符号梯度计算（如求解大型线性系统），同样受限；有限时域近似则引入不可控偏差。

Method: 将经典的 log-derivative trick 推广到测度论框架，构造蒙特卡洛梯度估计器：仅沿采样轨迹运行，对局部转移概率（或密度）及其导数做黑盒评估，完全规避状态空间物化与线性方程求解；框架天然适用于连续及一般 σ-有限状态空间。

Result: 在几乎必然终止与标准正则条件下证明估计器无偏；在合成离散 pMC 与连续状态案例上实验表明，方法可扩展至远超现有求解器能力的状态空间规模，显著缩短执行时间且不引入偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一种可扩展、无偏、仅需局部黑盒信息的 pMC 梯度估计途径，突破了现有技术对有限离散系统与全局符号计算的根本限制，并自然覆盖连续状态空间。

Abstract: Optimizing expected rewards and reachability probabilities in parametric Markov chains (pMCs) is central to the analysis and synthesis of probabilistic systems with loops. While traditional symbolic synthesis approaches--such as parameter lifting and rational function generation--suffer from scalability bottlenecks, gradient-based optimization emerges as an effective alternative. However, existing gradient-based techniques still rely on symbolic gradient computation--such as solving large linear systems--over the global state space, inheriting scalability limits. Finite-horizon approximation is possible but introduces uncontrollable bias.
  In this paper, we introduce a scalable Monte Carlo gradient estimator based on a measure-theoretic generalization of the log-derivative trick. We prove that our estimator provides unbiased gradient estimates under almost-sure termination and standard regularity conditions. Crucially, our method avoids global state-space materialization and linear system solving entirely: it operates purely along sampled trajectories, requiring only black-box evaluations of local transition probabilities (or densities) and their derivatives. Unlike existing pMC techniques that are fundamentally restricted to finite discrete systems, our measure-theoretic framework naturally extends to continuous and general $σ$-finite state spaces. Empirical evaluations on synthetic discrete pMCs and continuous-state case studies demonstrate that our method scales to state spaces far beyond the reach of existing solvers, significantly reducing execution time without introducing bias.

</details>


### [98] [HeteGCD: Statistics-Aware Dual-Prototype Learning for Generalized Category Discovery on Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36696)
*Xuanzhi Xi,Tongze Zhang,Zhili Qin,Yulin Tao,Hongliang Wang,Zhong Zhang,Qinli Yang,Junming Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HeteGCD，从"统计感知的双原型学习"视角解决异配图上的广义类别发现（GCD）问题：用门控双流编码器融合节点属性与局部属性差异统计，再用双原型分类器结合特征相似性与分布一致性发现新类，在五个异配图上优于24种基线组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异配图在现实中普遍存在（相连节点属性不相似或类别不同），而现有图GCD方法依赖同配假设的归纳偏置，难以适用；现有异配图学习方法虽利用多样邻域信号，但其选择与融合依赖覆盖全部类别（含无标签新类）的监督，在GCD设定下不可用。核心问题：在无新类监督下如何有效利用异构邻域信息进行类别发现。

Method: 提出 HeteGCD：(1) 同时考察局部属性差异统计与邻域类别分布，在特征空间和概率空间提取判别信息；(2) 门控双流编码器整合节点属性与局部属性差异统计，学习有信息量的节点表示；(3) 双原型分类器将基于特征的相似性与基于分布的一致性相结合以支持类别发现。

Result: 在五个异配图上，HeteGCD 相对于24种基线组合取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 无需新类监督，即可通过统计感知的双空间（特征+概率）双原型学习有效利用异配邻域信息完成广义类别发现，为异配图GCD提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Heterophilous graphs are prevalent in real-world applications, where connected nodes often exhibit dissimilar attributes or belong to different classes, posing significant challenges to conventional graph learning methods based on homophily. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in generalized category discovery (GCD), which requires models to recognize known classes and discover novel ones using labels from known classes only. However, existing graph GCD methods typically rely on aforementioned homophily-based inductive biases, which are ill-suited to heterophilous graphs. Meanwhile, although current heterophilous graph learning methods exploit diverse neighborhood signals, their selection and fusion are often guided by supervision across all classes, including novel classes whose labels are unavailable in GCD. These limitations raise a key question: how can heterogeneous neighborhood information be effectively leveraged for category discovery without any novel-class supervision? To answer this question, in this study we propose HeteGCD, a new statistics-aware dual-prototype learning perspective for generalized category discovery on heterophilous graphs. The key idea is to examine both statistics of local attribute differences and neighborhood class distribution to extract discriminative information in feature and probability spaces simultaneously. Specifically, a gated dual-stream encoder integrates both node attributes and local attribute-difference statistics to learn informative node representations. Afterwards, a dual-prototype classifier combines feature-based similarity with distribution-based consistency to support category discovery. Experiments on five heterophilous graphs show that HeteGCD achieves competitive performance against 24 baseline combinations.

</details>


### [99] [RankFormer: Rethinking Time Series Transformers through Representation Capacity Analysis and Progressive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36703)
*Peiwang Tang,Cheng HE,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RankFormer编码器-解码器架构，用秩（rank）分析框架解释时间序列Transformer的表征能力，并据此刻画patchification与编码设计的作用，设计Hourglass Attention秩增强编码器与交互式渐进解码器（IPD），在长期预测基准上取得与SOTA相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在多元时间序列预测中效果强，但其表征容量的内在机理缺乏理论解释；同时现有模型多采用通道独立建模与直接投影头等简化解码策略，难以刻画复杂的多元动态。

Method: 构建基于秩的分析框架，考察tokenization、embedding与注意力设计对表征容量的影响；据此设计秩增强编码器（含Hourglass Attention以提升信息交互中的秩稳定性），并引入交互式渐进解码器IPD，通过全局引导的跨通道交互与渐进式细化建模未来轨迹。

Result: 分析表明patchification通过将多个时间观测聚合到单个token而提升潜在表征容量，后续编码操作决定该容量的实际兑现程度；在真实世界长期预测基准上，RankFormer与SOTA方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 秩视角可为时间序列Transformer的容量设计提供可解释指导，秩增强编码与跨通道渐进解码能有效提升多元预测性能；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting plays a critical role in Web and cyber-physical systems, where large-scale services and IoT infrastructures generate complex temporal data. Transformer-based models have achieved strong empirical performance in this domain, yet the principles governing the expressive capacity of their learned representations remain insufficiently understood. Moreover, many existing models rely on simplified decoding strategies, including channel-independent modeling and direct projection heads, which limit their ability to capture complex multivariate dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose RankFormer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture for multivariate time series forecasting. We establish a rank-based analysis framework to investigate how tokenization, embedding, and attention designs affect the representation capacity of time series Transformers. Our analysis reveals that patchification increases the potential representation capacity by aggregating multiple temporal observations into each token, while subsequent encoding operations determine how much of this capacity is realized. Guided by these insights, we develop a rank-enhancing encoder with Hourglass Attention to improve rank stability during information interaction. Furthermore, we introduce the Interactive Progressive Decoder (IPD), which improves future trajectory modeling through global-guided cross-channel interaction and progressive refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world long-term forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that RankFormer achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. Code repository: https://github.com/TangPeiwang/Rankformer.

</details>


### [100] [Conservative Dueling Bandits: Preference Learning under Baseline Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36708)
*Rohan Deb,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究保守情境对决赌博机（conservative contextual dueling bandits），提出两种算法 MaxInP-BC 与 BTIGW-BC，在每轮保证优于可信基线的 (1−α) 分数比例的安全约束下，实现无遗憾量级的后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对决赌博机算法探索激进，可能向用户展示两个差的选项；且已有安全测试方法（如 CLUCB）无法直接迁移——论文指出直接迁移会带来线性后悔，即使基线效用已知。

Method: 引入基于效用差分的分数定义（击败当前最优物品的概率），使约束无需绝对奖励；对线性效用提出 MaxInP-BC，通过效用间隙认证分数，并在回退时以基线搭配信息量大的对手使回退轮仍促进学习；对一般效用类提出 BTIGW-BC，将在线逻辑回归 oracle 的保证转化为累积安全证书。

Result: MaxInP-BC 以高概率全程安全，后悔为 Õ(d√T/κ) 加与时间跨度无关的附加保守代价；BTIGW-BC 全程安全、回退次数有限，且当 oracle 的逻辑后悔为次线性时具有次线性后悔。实验表明两者满足约束，而对应无约束算法常违反约束，代价为中等程度的后悔增加。

Conclusion: 将安全约束建立在效用差分上并设计"有信息回退"机制是可行的；线性与一般效用类均可获得万无一失的安全性与强后悔保证，安全性以适度后悔为代价。

Abstract: Contextual dueling bandits learn from pairwise comparisons, the same feedback used to fine-tune language models from human preferences. Standard algorithms explore aggressively and can show users two poor options. We study conservative contextual dueling bandits, which require that, at every round, the cumulative score of the better displayed item stay above a $(1-α)$ fraction of a trusted baseline's. An item's score is its probability of beating the best available item, so the constraint depends only on utility differences and needs no absolute rewards. This also changes how safety can be certified: we show that transferring the safety test of the conservative bandit algorithm CLUCB unchanged can incur linear regret, even when the baseline's utilities are known exactly. For linear utilities, we develop MaxInP-BC, which certifies scores through utility gaps and, when it must fall back, pairs the baseline with an informative partner so that fallback rounds still drive learning. It is safe with high probability at all times, with regret $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{T}/κ)$ plus an additive cost of conservatism that does not grow with the horizon. For general utility classes, we develop BTIGW-BC, which turns the guarantee of an online logistic-regression oracle into cumulative safety certificates. It is safe at all times, makes finitely many fallbacks, and has sublinear regret whenever the oracle's logistic regret is sublinear. In experiments, both satisfy the constraint, which their unconstrained counterparts often violate, at a moderate cost in regret.

</details>


### [101] [Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment for Scalable Backpropagation-Free Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36718)
*Wu Lin,Yan Fan,Chenxiang Ma,Yinglan Feng,Jia Wang,Ka-Chun Wong,Qiuzhen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NDFA用轻量非线性替身子网络构造各隐藏层的反馈，并设计学习策略更新该子网络，从而缩小DFA与BP的性能差距，同时保持并行训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP生物不可信且存在反向锁定问题，限制层间并行；DFA虽可直接将全局输出误差通过固定反馈矩阵传至各隐藏层，但固定矩阵难以刻画隐藏表示到输出的非线性变换，导致与BP存在显著性能差距。

Method: 提出Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment (NDFA)：为每个隐藏层使用轻量级非线性替身子网络构造反馈；并提出学习策略在训练过程中更新替身子网络，以更好地逼近底层非线性变换。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、ImageNet上，针对残差网络与视觉Transformer的大量实验表明，NDFA持续优于现有DFA类方法，显著缩小与BP的差距，并保持高效并行训练。

Conclusion: 非线性替身反馈可有效缓解DFA固定反馈矩阵的表达局限，在保持并行优势的同时提升性能，为BP替代方案提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) remains the predominant algorithm for training deep neural networks, but it is biologically implausible and suffers from the backward locking problem, constraining parallelization across layers. Direct Feedback Alignment~(DFA), which propagates the global output error directly to each hidden layer via fixed feedback matrices, has emerged as a promising alternative for addressing these limitations. However, a significant performance gap persists between DFA and BP, which we attribute to the limited ability of fixed feedback matrices to capture the nonlinear transformation from hidden representations to the network output. To address this limitation, we propose Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment (NDFA), which constructs each hidden-layer feedback using a lightweight nonlinear surrogate subnetwork. To enhance the effectiveness of the feedback throughout training, we further propose a learning strategy to update the surrogate subnetwork, thereby better approximating the underlying nonlinear transformation. Extensive experiments across residual networks and vision transformers on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, and ImageNet datasets demonstrate that NDFA consistently outperforms existing DFA-based methods and substantially narrows the performance gap to BP, while maintaining efficient parallel training. The code is available at https://github.com/YanFan321/NDFA.

</details>


### [102] [Generative Refinement Learning for Temporal Interpolation in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36751)
*Pu Ren,Michael W. Mahoney,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iFLEX将"学习校正简单插值残差"用于科学场时间下采样重建：以加权平均插值为基线，用条件扩散模型生成残差并回加，在湍流、雷达降水、海温数据上误差低于光流与视频插帧扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学仿真与观测常以低于下游任务需求的时间分辨率提供数据；在固定网格点上平均会引入误差，而仅靠运动（光流）估计并不能确定物理场数值如何变化，因此需要能直接重建中间时刻场值的方法。

Method: iFLEX先按时间距离加权平均相邻两个观测得到基础估计；训练时用真实中间场减去该估计得到残差，由条件扩散模型学习生成。条件包括两个观测、目标时间、观测间隔及可获得的物理参数；推理时将生成的残差加回加权平均以重建未观测场。

Result: 在Navier-Stokes Kraichnan湍流、上海雷达降水与海表面温度三个数据集上，重建误差低于所评估基线（含计算机视觉视频插帧的光流法与扩散模型）。在Re=36,000的受控湍流对比中，固定扩散骨干与训练设置，学习残差将相对Frobenius范数误差由直接生成场的约13.7降至10.5。

Conclusion: 学习校正简单数值插值（残差建模）优于直接生成中间场，是一种有效且可迁移的科学场时间超分辨/插值范式；作者公开了代码。

Abstract: Computationally expensive simulations and real-world observations often provide data at a lower temporal resolution than downstream tasks require. Averaging observations at fixed grid points can introduce errors, and motion estimates alone do not determine how the physical field values change. In this work, we study whether learning to correct a simple numerical interpolation improves the reconstruction of intermediate scientific fields. Our model, interpolative FLow EXpert (iFLEX), first estimates the missing field by taking a weighted average of the two observations at each grid point, giving more weight to the observation closer to the requested time.
