We prove that both \textsc{MuSAM} and \textsc{MoMuSAM} attain the same asymptotic first-order stationarity order, and further show that \textsc{MoMuSAM} yields a more stable stochastic linearization than \textsc{MuSAM}. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that sharpness awareness consistently improves Muon.

</details>


### [139] [When Is a Relation Worth Trusting? Manipulation Costs, Exposure, and the Trust Trap in Multi-Relational Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37239)
*Vincess Dongmo,Astride Melvin Fokam Ninyim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多关系图中"是否使用某条关系"的取舍：在多行为攻击成本给定的前提下，证明度门控在特定条件下严格优于任何关系选择/重加权，并提出按"单位成本暴露度"优选需加固关系的准则，配以精确最坏情形与认证界工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多关系图检测器从干净数据学习对各关系的信任度，但攻击者对不同关系、不同行为付出的代价不同；作者要回答"在成本给定下，使用某条关系是否反而不如弃用"，并给出平台该加固哪条关系的可行判据。

Method: (1) 度门控（degree gating）机制及锐利性分析；(2) 每节点暴露度上界（可扩展到消息传递）；(3) 支撑工具：线性关系模型的精确最坏情形、R-GCN与树集成的认证上下界、对抗验证的有限样本保证；(4) 基于上述结果的选择协议与成本感知提议。

Result: 理论：当部分节点在该关系上被孤立且攻击者无力开启门控时，度门控严格优于任意选择/重加权，且该窗口是锐利的；真实图上验证性门控仅在这些条件下获益。加固选择：单位成本暴露度（无需攻击信息）在16例中15例选出使精确陷阱阈值提升最大的关系，对比信息量/廉价性/等编辑成本分析至多7例。实证：干净训练的图检测器在6图90种设置中58种低于无图对照；选择协议从不显著劣于弃图，≤4条关系时多弃权，关系多时成本感知提议有效（目前仅在合成图）。

Conclusion: 在多关系图中，是否使用某关系的决定取决于攻击成本与节点暴露结构；度门控在可证明的窗口内优于替代方案，单位成本暴露度是有效的加固指引。缺乏成本信息时，安全选择是弃权（不使用该关系）。

Abstract: Multi-relational graph detectors learn from clean data how much to trust each relation, but an adversary pays a different price to manipulate each relation and each action; taking such costs as given, we ask when using a relation is worse than ignoring it. Our first main result proves that degree gating strictly beats every selection or reweighting of a relation when part of the population is isolated on it and the attacker cannot afford to open the gate, and that this window is sharp; on real graphs, validated gating gains exactly on the relations that meet these conditions and nowhere else. Our second shows that a per-node exposure bound, which extends to message passing, tells a platform which relation to harden: exposure per unit cost, computed without any attack, picks the relation whose hardening raises the exact trap threshold most in 15 of 16 cases, against at most 7 for informativeness, cheapness, or an analysis with equal edit costs. Supporting tools give exact worst cases for linear relational models, certified brackets for R-GCN and tree ensembles, and a finite-sample guarantee for adversarial validation, under which clean-trained graph detectors fall below their graph-free counterparts in 58 of 90 settings on six graphs. A selection protocol built on these results is never significantly worse than ignoring the graph; with at most four relations it mostly abstains, as validation alone would, and its cost-aware proposals help when relations are many, so far shown on synthetic graphs. Without cost information, the safe choice is to abstain.

</details>


### [140] [Your Policy Is Secretly a Harness: The Decision Geometry of Agent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37255)
*Jian Mu,Ruibo Shen,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出把语言模型智能体的部署回报分解为"harness 上限（ceiling）"减去"策略失配（mismatch）"，并用 KL 正则化控制下的 log-odds 分析证明策略携带的是 harness 的偏好，据此重新界定 harness 评估与改进的正确方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: harness（提示、工具与控制逻辑）常被反复修改，并用"已有模型在其下的表现"来评判，这隐含假设模型在某一 harness 下学到的内容能迁移到另一 harness。作者质疑该假设，追问：策略从 harness 学到了什么？harness 改变后什么会保留？

Method: 在 KL 正则化控制框架下推导最优策略的 log-odds 与 harness 各动作偏好强度之间的关系；据此把部署回报形式化为 ceiling − mismatch。在 MiniWoB++ 上做 harness 顺序反转实验（不改变最优决策，只改变字段顺序），并以"强模型替代最优"估计 ceiling 与 mismatch，在 Terminal-Bench Lite 的编码智能体 harness 上验证该分解能复现准确率排序。

Result: MiniWoB++ 中，仅反转观测字段顺序即令训练后模型成功率从 85.0% 降至 19.2%，说明策略偏好高度 harness 特定；固定模型比较中"被拒绝"但提升 ceiling 的新 harness，在继续训练后均优于原智能体，且事前测得的 ceiling 与 mismatch 可预测增益。Terminal-Bench Lite 上，ceiling − mismatch 复现准确率排序，而单看任一项都不行。

Conclusion: harness 评估不能只看固定模型的表现，因为它混淆了"harness 提供多少"与"模型用得多好"。训练只降低 mismatch，换 harness 则同时移动 ceiling 与 mismatch；应以 ceiling 和 mismatch 联合判定 harness 潜力，并为每个 harness 重新适配策略。

Abstract: Language model agents are post-trained and deployed through a harness, the prompts, tools, and control logic that present a task to the model and execute its outputs. Harnesses are often revised and judged by how an already trained model performs under them, which assumes that what the model learned under one harness carries over to the next. We ask what a policy learns from its harness and what survives when the harness changes. Under KL-regularized control, we show that the log odds of an optimal policy recover how strongly its harness favors each action over the others, so a trained policy carries the preferences of its harness. Deployed return then equals the ceiling of the harness, the best return it offers, minus the mismatch of the policy, the return lost to its own suboptimal decisions. Training changes only the mismatch, while changing the harness moves both terms. Comparing harnesses with a fixed model therefore mixes what each harness offers with how well that model uses it. In MiniWoB++, post-training makes a model's preferences specific to its training harness. Reversing the order of the observation fields leaves every optimal decision unchanged yet lowers a trained model's success from 85.0% to 19.2%. Conversely, every new harness that a fixed-model comparison rejects but that raises the ceiling outperforms the original agent after further training under it, and ceiling and mismatch measured beforehand predict these gains. For coding-agent harnesses on Terminal-Bench Lite, ceiling minus mismatch estimated with a stronger model as a stand-in for the optimum reproduces the accuracy ranking, while either term alone does not. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/HRP.

</details>


### [141] [Where Should the Evaluation Budget Go in LLM-Driven Agent Scaffold Evolution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37276)
*Sichun Luo,Yi Huang,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Guanzhi Deng,Haibo Wang,Jian Xu,Zefa Hu,Junlan Feng,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出评估预算分配框架，将稀疏搜索期反馈与定向的最终确认相结合，在约一半评估量下达到或超过全量评估的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的agent scaffold演化需对每个候选在完整任务集上评估，以同时指导后续搜索和决定最终scaffold，执行成本高昂；因此需研究评估预算在这两种角色间如何分配。

Method: 实验分析表明稀疏反馈足以维持有效演化、额外定向评估利于最终选择，且共享信息性题目与共同评分基准支撑相近候选的可比性。据此提出评估分配框架：短期全量评估预热，建立共享信息性核心并校准题目参数在搜索期固定的响应模型；搜索后重拟合模型并采用配对逐次减半（paired successive halving），复用已有观测、仅补做缺失评估。

Result: 在四个基准与两个演化框架上，多数设置下平均性能优于全量评估，且评估单元用量约为其一半。

Conclusion: 评估预算可解耦为搜索期稀疏反馈与最终定向确认；稀疏反馈不损害演化生产力，定向确认提升选择质量，配合固定题目参数的响应模型与配对逐次减半可实现评估效率与性能的双赢。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven agent scaffold evolution improves agent workflows through iterative candidate generation and evaluation. Evaluation both guides subsequent search and determines the final scaffold, but evaluating every candidate on the full task set incurs substantial execution costs. We investigate how evaluation budgets can be allocated across these two roles. Our experiments show that sparse feedback can sustain productive evolution, while additional targeted evaluation improves final selection. Replay analyses further show that shared informative items and a common scoring reference support comparisons among closely matched candidates. Motivated by these findings, we propose \method, an evaluation allocation framework that combines sparse search-time feedback with targeted final confirmation. A short fully evaluated warm-up establishes a shared informative core and calibrates a response model whose item parameters remain fixed during search. After search, \method refits the model and applies paired successive halving, reusing accumulated observations and executing only missing evaluations. Across four benchmarks and two evolution frameworks, \method achieves higher mean performance than full evaluation in most settings while using approximately half as many evaluation cells.

</details>


### [142] [Bayesian Inference of Expensive Simulators: Survey and Comparison of Design Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37284)
*Šimon Soldát,Václav Šmídl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了针对昂贵模拟器的贝叶斯推断方法，将现有贝叶斯优化方法统一为一个通用框架，系统比较设计选择，并提出以自定义"代理变量"（proxy variables）替代标量对数似然作为代理模型响应变量，从而显著提升推断效率，并发布Julia工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模拟器前向模型极其昂贵时，直接进行贝叶斯推断不可行；该领域方法分散在不同文献中，缺乏统一框架，且不清楚各设计选择对推断效率的影响。作者希望整合零散工作并提供可操作的实践指导。

Method: 将基于贝叶斯优化的推断方法归纳为通用框架，识别主要设计选择；在统一仿真预算度量下进行系统性的实证比较；分析代理模型响应变量的选择对推断效率的影响，并提出对模拟器输出做变换、构造更多信息且更光滑响应面的自定义代理变量的概念。

Result: 推断效率通常由代理模型响应变量的选择主导，因为它直接决定被建模函数的复杂度；常用的标量对数似然是较差的响应变量，会丢失关键信息；自定义代理变量在相同模拟预算下相比标准响应变量大幅提升推断效率。

Conclusion: 提供了该领域的统一框架与可操作指南，并发布数值稳健的Julia软件包，以促进贝叶斯优化在模拟器逆问题中的实际应用。

Abstract: We present a survey of approaches to Bayesian inference with simulator forward models, focusing on settings with prohibitively expensive simulators. In this domain, Bayesian optimization-based methods have emerged as the most promising approach. We unify existing methods into a general framework, enabling consistent reasoning about shared algorithmic structure across the fragmented literature. Within this framework, we identify the primary design choices and perform a systematic empirical comparison of their effects on inference efficiency in terms of required simulation budget.
  A key result of our study is that inference efficiency is often dominated by the choice of surrogate-model response variable, as it directly governs the complexity of the modeled function. Specifically, we demonstrate that the prevalent scalar log-likelihood is a poor response variable, as it discards crucial information. Based on this insight, we introduce the concept of custom \emph{proxy variables}: transformations of simulator outputs engineered to retain more information and provide smoother response surfaces, substantially improving inference efficiency over standard response variables.
  We provide actionable guidance and a numerically robust Julia package to facilitate practical adoption of Bayesian optimization for simulator inverse problems.

</details>


### [143] [Structured Representation Probing for Risk Anticipation in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37299)
*Jiawei Liang,Tianrui Lou,Le Yang,Xianghao Jiao,Wei Wang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构化表示探针（SRP），一种轻量级监控器，从冻结的端到端驾驶策略内部表示中读取风险，实现失效预警，无需修改策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略隐含结构化的交通参与者、自车行为与道路约束表示，可作为失效预警的监控接口，补充轨迹、控制量与人工特征风险信号的不足。

Method: SRP 从冻结策略中读取 actor、ego、map 三类表示，为每个来源学习独立的线性风险方向；通过基数归一化的 log-mean-exp 池化聚合元素分数，兼顾显著证据敏感性并降低对 token 数量的依赖；采用事件窗口监督将读数与即将发生的硬失效关联，并以辅助事件类型头联合训练各来源方向；在线仅用单一通用分数告警。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 上对 DriveTransformer、UniAD、VAD 实验，帧级 PR-AUC 与 ROC-AUC 均优于运行时基线；DriveTransformer 2 秒horizon 下 ROC-AUC 达 0.833（时序一致性基线 0.715），读出头运行时开销仅 2.67%；探针在新路线上仍保持失效判别能力。

Conclusion: 内部结构化表示可作为有效的失效预警接口；SRP 以低成本、策略无关的方式实现可靠在线监控，并具备跨路线泛化性。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies form structured internal representations of traffic, ego behavior, and road constraints. These representations offer a monitoring interface for anticipating failures, complementing risk signals from trajectories, controls, and manually designed features. We introduce the Structured Representation Probe (SRP), a lightweight monitor that reads actor, ego, and map representations from a frozen policy. SRP learns a separate linear risk direction for each source and aggregates element scores through cardinality-normalized log-mean-exp pooling, retaining sensitivity to salient evidence while reducing dependence on token count. Event-window supervision links the readout to upcoming hard failures, and auxiliary event type heads jointly train the source directions across failure modes. Online alerting uses a single generic score without modifying the driving policy. Bench2Drive experiments on DriveTransformer, UniAD, and VAD show higher frame-level PR-AUC and ROC-AUC than the evaluated runtime baselines. On DriveTransformer at a horizon of 2 seconds, ROC-AUC reaches 0.833 versus 0.715 for temporal consistency, with 2.67% readout runtime overhead. The trained probes also retain the ability to distinguish impending failures on new routes.

</details>


### [144] [WCC: A Learning-Based Framework for Deciding When to Apply Cooperative Coevolution in Large-Scale Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37329)
*Wenjie Qiu,Hepei Li,Jianfeng Wang,Jun Zhang,Yue-Jiao Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WCC：用强化学习动态决定大规模全局优化中何时部署协同进化，实现“一次训练、重复使用”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CC与NDA（非分解算法）是LSGO的两大主流路径，但两者在不同问题类型上表现差异显著且各有固有局限，"何时使用CC"这一问题长期未解。

Method: 提出学习型框架WCC，将CC/NDA切换建模为马尔可夫决策过程，设计针对性特征集、鲁棒奖励与定制网络结构；采用自回归策略实现细粒度动态切换与条件式计算预算分配，无需专家设定。

Result: 在多种问题、优化时域与基线优化器上展现强竞争力与优异泛化性；低预算下实现“一次训练、重复使用”，显著降低LSGO开销。

Conclusion: 给出既有理论未能覆盖的洞见，为实际中何时及如何有效部署CC提供指导。

Abstract: Cooperative Coevolution (CC) and Non-Decomposition Algorithms (NDAs) are two primary approaches for large-scale global optimization (LSGO). Although both have achieved success, they exhibit markedly different performance across problem types and each faces approach-specific limitations, leaving the question of "when to use CC" unresolved. We address this gap with WCC, a novel learning-based framework that dynamically decides when to deploy CC in LSGO. WCC employs a streamlined design for efficient adaptive learning. We formulate the decision as a Markov decision process, designing a targeted feature set, a robust reward, and a tailored network architecture to provide the agent with rich information about the problem state and optimization progress. Our auto-regressive policy enables WCC to perform fine-grained, dynamic switching between CC and NDAs while conditionally allocating the computational budget without expert specifications. WCC shows strong competitiveness and superior generalization across problems, optimization horizons, and baseline optimizers. It realizes a "train-once and reuse" paradigm under a low budget, significantly curbing LSGO cost. Additionally, in-depth analyses yield insights not captured by existing theory, offering practical guidance on when and how to effectively deploy CC.

</details>


### [145] [CoS-VQ: Coordinated Shared-Scale Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37336)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Zhe Jiang,Xin Si,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoS-VQ，在保持低维码本的前提下联合优化硬索引与跨位置共享尺度，用切线可分离上界与下包络避免穷举码字组合，在Qwen3-27B上优于QTIP与LLVQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署受内存制约，低比特权重量化是有效手段。已有向量量化(VQ)方法多通过增大有效编码维度提升精度，但会使独立查表和局部码字更新变复杂，因此希望在不提高码字维度的条件下取得同样收益。

Method: 核心难点是尺度诱导耦合：最优尺度依赖整个码字组合，又反过来改变各位置的候选排序，导致即便重新优化尺度，逐点更新仍会停滞。CoS-VQ在多探测尺度下收集候选，针对残差感知二次目标构造切线可分离上界；将候选比较化为正尺度上直线的下包络，从而得到有限个代表区间以完成索引—尺度联合更新，无需穷举码字组合。

Result: 在Qwen3-27B上，CoS-VQ在语言建模、代码与推理基准上均优于QTIP和LLVQ；同一初始化下的消融显示，联合更新的增益大于仅尺度精修，说明联合选择可作为增大码字维度的替代方案。

Conclusion: 保持低维码字、通过索引与共享尺度的协调优化即可获得精度提升，为低比特VQ提供了一条不依赖维度扩张的可行路径；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require substantial memory, motivating low-bit weight quantization for efficient deployment. Existing vector quantization (VQ) methods often improve accuracy by increasing the effective coding dimension, which can complicate independent lookup and local codeword updates. We propose CoS-VQ (Coordinated Shared-Scale Vector Quantization), which preserves low-dimensional codewords while jointly optimizing hard indices and their shared scale across positions. The key challenge is scale-induced coupling: the optimal scale depends on the entire codeword combination and, in turn, changes candidate rankings across positions, so pointwise updates can stall even after scale re-optimization. CoS-VQ collects candidates at multiple probe scales and constructs a tangent separable upper bound for the residual-aware quadratic objective. By reducing candidate comparisons to lower envelopes of lines over positive scales, it identifies finitely many representative intervals for joint index--scale updates without exhaustively enumerating codeword combinations. On Qwen3.8-27B, CoS-VQ outperforms QTIP and LLVQ on the evaluated language modeling, coding, and reasoning benchmarks. Ablations from the same initialization further show larger gains from joint updates than from scale-only refinement, highlighting joint selection as an alternative to increasing codeword dimensionality. The code is available at https://github.com/xzktx003/Cos_VQ.

</details>


### [146] [Normative Deviation Modeling for EEG-based Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37338)
*Wan Jiang,Jingqi Cheng,Hui Zhang,Xu Zhao,Xiaojing Chen,Shengeng Tang,Jingjing Wu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用健康人群的正常参考模型（normative reference）提升跨异质数据源的EEG抑郁症检测性能，提出标量级（S-NDD）与特征级（F-NDD）两种规范性偏差引导诊断方法，并在两个公开数据集上验证其在受试者独立与双向跨源场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的抑郁症（MDD）检测在异构数据源间面临挑战：个体差异与采集条件差异会削弱疾病相关表征。作者希望借助健康对照（HC）数据构建正常参考，通过刻画"偏离健康模式的程度"来获得更稳健、跨源可迁移的诊断线索。

Method: 仅用健康对照记录分别独立训练结构性与分布性两类参考模型，用于刻画EEG偏离健康模式的程度；提出两种整合方式：S-NDD将结构与分布偏差汇总为标量分数并与深度EEG表征融合；F-NDD保留逐维残差，通过注意力聚合与辅助监督与深度表征结合；跨源评估时采用目标域HC辅助的分数校准，且不更新已训练模型。

Result: 在两个公开EEG数据集上，于受试者独立的同源评估与双向跨源评估两种设置下，两种规范性偏差引导方法均表现出有效性，说明该范式在不同评估场景下均具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以健康规范参考为核心的偏差建模可作为EEG抑郁症检测的有效补充表征，特征级残差保留与注意力聚合较标量汇总提供更细粒度信息；配合免训练的目标域健康样本校准，可缓解跨源分布偏移。

Abstract: Web-enabled digital health services are expanding access to physiological monitoring and mental health screening. However, electroencephalography (EEG)-based major depressive disorder (MDD) detection remains challenging across heterogeneous data sources, where individual variability and acquisition differences may affect disease-related representations. In this work, we investigate the use of healthy normative references for EEG-based MDD detection. Specifically, structural and distributional reference models are independently trained using healthy control (HC) recordings to characterize departures from healthy EEG patterns. We develop two diagnostic approaches that incorporate normative deviations at different representation levels. Scalar-level Normative Deviation-guided Diagnosis (S-NDD) summarizes structural and distributional deviations into scalar scores and integrates them with deep EEG representations. Feature-level Normative Deviation-guided Diagnosis (F-NDD) retains dimension-wise residuals and combines them with deep EEG representations through attention-based aggregation and auxiliary supervision. We also employ target HC-assisted score calibration for cross-source evaluation without updating the trained models. Experiments on two public EEG datasets examine both approaches under subject-independent within-source and bidirectional cross-source settings. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of normative deviation-guided diagnosis across different evaluation settings.

</details>


### [147] [EmbeddingGemma 2: Unified and Efficient Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37364)
*Henrique Schechter Vera,Sahil Dua,Jovan Andonov,Sara Smoot,Sebastian Russo,Ivan Llanos,Max Matveev,Jae Hyeon Yoo,Kat Black,Shih-Cheng Huang,Josiah Aklilu,Maarten Grootendorst,Omar Sanseviero,Chandramouli Amarnath,Ian Ballantyne,Zhe Li,Daniel Salz,Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin,Ilya Philippov,Kristen Quan,Glenn Cameron,Edouard Yvinec,Luciano Martins,Doug Reid,Sindhu Raghuram Panyam,Weiyi Wang,Hengxuan Ying,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Tanmaya Dabral,Shanfeng Zhang,Juyeong Ji,Renjie Wu,Joe Zou,Xu Chen,Yunan Zhang,Yu-Hui Chen,Matthew Soulanille,Vinod Mamillapalli,Yi-Chun Kuo,Wai Hon Law,Matt Kreileder,Alice Zheng,Suril Shah,Mogan Shieh,Pooja Consul,Alberto Montes,Andreas Hess,Andres Sevilla,Babak Samari,Brendan McCloskey,Brian Potetz,Daniel Cer,Georgios Kathareios,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Hesen Zhang,Ji Ma,Jiaxing Wu,Keegan Mosley,Paul Suganthan,Rui Meng,Shen Gao,Shengcao Cao,Yi-Ting Chen,Yichang Chen,Zhongli Ding,Kendra Byrne,Cormac Brick,Olivier Lacombe,Qin Yin,Tris Warkentin,Armand Joulin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源原生多模态嵌入模型 EmbeddingGemma 2，在统一空间中表征文本、图像、音频、视频及其组合，并在全模态基准上刷新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型多为单模态专用或模态拼接式方案，缺少在文本、图像、音频、视频及混合模态上同时保持高质量的原生统一表征模型，且难以适配资源受限部署场景。

Method: 采用简化的逐层嵌入（simplified per-layer embeddings）结构，并设计多阶段课程学习（multi-stage curriculum），按模态与训练目标逐步推进；同时提供选择性模态编码器加载、可调视觉 token 预算、可截断嵌入维度与量化权重等部署灵活性，并辅以消融实验分析关键设计决策。

Result: 在全模态综合嵌入基准上建立新的帕累托前沿，性能同时超越专用单模态模型与规模为其 2–12 倍的更大模型；在单模态、跨模态与混合模态评测中均表现稳健。

Conclusion: EmbeddingGemma 2 验证了原生统一多模态嵌入的可行性，兼顾质量与部署效率，并以开源形式发布以推动后续研究。

Abstract: We present EmbeddingGemma 2, an open multimodal embedding model which natively represents text, images, audio, video, and combinations thereof in a unified space. Our approach introduces improvements such as simplified per-layer embeddings and a multi-stage curriculum with modality and objective progression which ensure strong quality across unimodal, cross-modal, and mixed-modal evaluations. Notably, in comprehensive embedding benchmarks across all modalities, EmbeddingGemma 2 establishes a new Pareto frontier, outperforming both specialist models and models two to twelve times larger. Furthermore, our design offers flexibility for resource-constrained environments, supporting selective modality encoder loading, adjustable vision token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantized weights. We present ablations analyzing key decisions and release EmbeddingGemma 2 to promote further research.

</details>


### [148] [TRIDENT-Seq: Profile-Matched Prefix-Future Contrast for Label-Efficient Payment Risk Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37365)
*Weiyu Han,Haoyu Jiang,Jie Sheng,Ruobing Zuo,Huilin He,Ze Zhao,Xiao Tan,Nairong Zhang,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIDENT-Seq：以Prefix-Future Contrast（PFC）预训练的交易基础模型，通过"活跃度画像匹配"的候选构造迫使编码器学习支付资源使用行为而非粗粒度活跃度差异，在三个生产支付业务上全面超越架构匹配的从头训练与XGBoost系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ant International拥有大量跨用户与支付业务的交易历史，但确认欺诈与拒付标签稀缺、滞后且业务局部化。理想方案是用单一编码器在无标签历史上预训练以服务所有业务，前提是预训练能捕捉区分风险的行为。然而交易日志上的对比预训练存在"过于容易"的问题：交易量、金额、时间等粗粒度活跃度特征即可把某段历史的真实延续与任意候选区分开，模型无需建模"支付资源如何被使用"这一真正风险信号。

Method: 提出TRIDENT-Seq交易基础模型，预训练目标为Prefix-Future Contrast（PFC）：将每个前缀与其后续行为摘要进行匹配，但候选样本限定为活跃度画像相似的样本，从而排除粗粒度活跃度捷径；同时对编码器输出施加活跃度预测任务与离散度正则化。评估覆盖三个生产支付业务，对比架构匹配的从头训练（含全标签与少标签设定）、跨业务迁移、随机候选消融，并与线上XGBoost系统在同一干预预算下比较。

Result: 在6个"业务×预算"设定（3业务×2预算）中，预训练编码器在0.5%干预预算下的AP与召回均优于架构匹配从头训练。固定验证集下，仅用10%训练标签（Wallet）与25%（Shopping、SME）即超过全标签从头训练的平均AP与召回。仅用另一业务历史预训练仍有效。将画像匹配候选替换为随机候选，召回从24.62%降至5.64%，验证了PFC设计的关键作用。部署评估中，同一干预预算下基于金额的拒付率与总体损失率分别比原XGBoost系统低37.7%与37.9%。

Conclusion: 粗粒度活跃度差异会使交易日志对比预训练失去风险判别价值；通过画像匹配候选的Prefix-Future Contrast可有效迫使编码器学习支付资源使用模式，得到可跨业务复用、标签效率高、且在生产环境中显著降低拒付与损失的交易基础模型。

Abstract: Ant International accumulates large volumes of transaction history across users and payment businesses. Confirmed fraud and chargeback outcomes, by contrast, are scarce, delayed, and specific to each business. A single encoder pretrained on unlabeled histories could serve all businesses, but only if pretraining captures the behavior that separates risk. Coarse activity differences can make contrastive pretraining on transaction logs too easy: transaction volume, amount, and timing can distinguish a history's continuation from arbitrary candidates without modeling how payment resources are used. We present TRIDENT-Seq, a transaction foundation model trained with Prefix-Future Contrast (PFC). PFC matches each prefix to a summary of its subsequent behavior among candidates with similar activity profiles; activity prediction and dispersion regularization act on the encoder output. Across three production payment businesses, the pretrained encoder improves average precision (AP) and recall at a 0.5% intervention budget over architecture-matched scratch training in all six business-budget settings. With a fixed validation set, it exceeds full-label scratch training in mean AP and recall using 10% of the training labels on Wallet and 25% on Shopping and SME. Pretraining only on another business's histories still helps. Replacing profile-matched candidates with random ones cuts recall from 24.62% to 5.64%. In Ant International's deployment evaluation, recorded amount-based chargeback and overall loss rates are 37.7% and 37.9% lower than those of the previous XGBoost-based system under the same intervention budget.

</details>


### [149] [Euclidean Crowding-Driven Adaptive Euclidean-Hyperbolic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37373)
*Hao Chen,Jian Sun,Chuan Shi,Hongying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从几何与泛化理论角度解释双曲空间在层次数据上的优势，并提出自适应欧氏-双曲融合框架EH-Fusion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲图表示学习在层次数据上经验表现优异，但其理论机制尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对"欧氏拥挤"现象的量化刻画及基于泛化理论的分析。

Method: 1）通过对节点嵌入的逐层最小间隔进行分析，建立欧氏拥挤的几何量化，证明固定维欧氏嵌入树状图必然出现逐层拥挤，导致基于间隔的泛化界更弱；2）给出双曲几何在层次数据上优越性的泛化界；3）提出EH-Fusion：通过指数映射将欧氏GNN表示提升到双曲空间，并基于节点级邻域扩展模式自适应融合两种几何。

Result: 理论证明欧氏嵌入的逐层拥挤导致更弱的margin泛化界，并给出双曲几何优越性的泛化界；大量实验验证了理论结论并表明EH-Fusion在基准数据集上的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究从几何量化与泛化理论层面揭示了双曲空间处理层次数据的优势机制，所提出的自适应融合框架在理论与实践间建立了桥梁并取得良好效果。

Abstract: Hyperbolic graph representation learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for modeling hierarchical data. Unlike Euclidean space, which exhibits polynomial volume growth, hyperbolic space grows exponentially with radius, naturally accommodating tree-like structures without the geometric distortion known as Euclidean crowding. Despite strong empirical performance, the theoretical mechanisms underlying hyperbolic embeddings remain poorly understood. In this work, we provide a geometric quantifcation research for Euclidean crowding, a generalization-theoretic explanation for the superiority of hyperbolic space on hierarchical data, and further develop an adaptive Euclidean-hyperbolic fusion learning framework toward bridging this gap. Specifically, we \textbf{first} establish a geometric quantification of Euclidean crowding by analyzing layer-wise minimum separation in node embeddings, proving that fixed-dimensional Euclidean embeddings of tree-like graphs inevitably exhibit layer-wise crowding that leads to weaker margin-based generalization bounds. \textbf{Second}, we provide a generalization-bound for the superiority of hyperbolic geometry on hierarchical data. \textbf{Third}, we propose EH-Fusion, a geometry-adaptive framework that lifts Euclidean GNN representations into hyperbolic space via exponential mapping and adaptively fuses both geometries based on node-level neighborhood expansion patterns. Extensive experiments validate our theoretical insights and demonstrate the effectiveness of EH-Fusion on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [150] [Channel Selection for Mixture-of-Experts Decoding Needs No Calibration Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37386)
*Geunhyoung Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出MoE通道选择器的校准阈值既不能保证读取预算、也不能稳定提升精度，提出无需校准的top-k选择，并给出完全由模型权重构建的高效reader，在保留98.1%精度下仅需17.8%专家读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小批量交互式解码时，MoE模型的瓶颈在于从内存加载被路由专家的权重。由于单token下多数中间通道贡献很小，通道选择器只读取重要通道的权重，但现有做法依赖离线数据校准阈值，该阈值既不稳定也未必带来收益。

Method: 1) 实证检验校准阈值缺陷：阈值附近通过通道数随输入波动，Prox在18%预算校准下实测平均仅读12.9%；2) 采用top-k选择器，每token取分数最高的k个通道，无需校准数据；3) 提出由模型权重直接构建的reader：用三元sketch筛选通道、低秩映射恢复被跳过通道，并实现Triton内核。

Result: 在13%和18%预算下，top-k版本在43组比较中40组不劣于/优于同分数的阈值版本，生成任务23组全部胜出；避免Gemma 4上阈值导致的重复循环崩溃。新reader在三个后训练MoE模型上保留98.1%全模型精度，仅读17.8%路由专家权重；Triton内核在batch=1时比优化vLLM快1.60–1.90倍，更大batch最高2.69倍。

Conclusion: 校准阈值对选择器承诺的两件事——已知读取预算与稳定精度增益——均无法兑现，应改用top-k；基于权重构建的reader在精度与推理速度上都表现优异，可直接加速MoE解码。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are bottlenecked by loading routed-expert weights from memory during interactive decoding at small batch sizes. Because most intermediate channels in a routed expert contribute little to any single token, channel selectors predict which channels matter and read only their weights, typically keeping channels above a threshold calibrated on offline data. We show that channel selection needs no calibration data. First, a calibrated threshold does not fix how much a selector reads, because the number of channels passing it varies across inputs; Prox with thresholds calibrated for an 18% read budget actually reads only 12.9% on average across our benchmarks. Second, top-k keeps each token's k highest-scoring channels and needs no calibration data. At the calibrated budget, a selector's top-k version matches or beats its threshold version on the same score in 40 of 43 comparisons at 13% and 18% budgets, including all 23 on generation tasks. The top-k versions also avoid the repetition-loop collapse that calibrated thresholds cause on Gemma 4. Calibrating a threshold thus provides neither of the two things selectors use it for: a known read budget and a consistent accuracy gain at that budget. We furthermore introduce a reader built entirely from the model's weights, which screens channels with a ternary sketch and recovers the skipped ones with a low-rank map. Across three post-trained MoE models, our reader retains 98.1% of full-model accuracy at 17.8% of routed-expert reads, with Triton kernels running MoE blocks 1.60 to 1.90x faster than optimized vLLM at batch one and up to 2.69x at larger batches on the same three models.

</details>


### [151] [Para-Gaussian Approximations of Random Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37388)
*Mihai Nica,Janosch Ortmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"para-Gaussian分布"——一种基于Hermite多项式构造的轻微非高斯近似，用以更准确地刻画有限宽度深层神经网络的逐层分布，并给出实用的递归近似方法，在线性网络与ReLU网络上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机初始化神经网络在宽度 n→∞ 时呈高斯行为，但该近似在有限 n 下不准确，且随网络加深误差累积恶化。因此需要一种比高斯更精确、又保持可解析处理的近似。

Method: 从Hermite多项式出发构造"para-Gaussian分布"，作为对高斯分布的轻微非高斯修正；据此建立递归方法逐层近似网络更深的层分布；在可精确求解的线性神经网络和需近似处理的ReLU网络上应用该理论。

Result: 该框架能递归地近似深层网络分布；在线性网络上给出精确解的一致性验证，在ReLU网络上给出近似结果。

Conclusion: para-Gaussian近似在有限宽度、深层网络场景下优于纯高斯近似，为分析深层随机初始化网络的信号传播提供了更精细且可计算的理论工具。

Abstract: It is known that randomly initialized neural networks have Gaussian behaviour in the $n\to \infty$ limit. Unfortunately, this approximation can be poor for finite $n$ and gets worse as one goes deeper into the network. In this work, we develop a more accurate approximation that is \emph{slightly} non-Gaussian, which we call the para-Gaussian distribution and is constructed from the Hermite polynomials. We show how this para-Gaussian approximation can lead to a practical recursive method to approximate the deeper layers of the network. Finally, we apply this theory to linear neural networks (where things can be solved exactly) and to ReLU neural networks (where things can be approximated).

</details>


### [152] [How Many and How Much? Contrastive Learning with Negative Samples for Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37389)
*Sooho Moon,Yunyong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RADAR框架，通过风险感知的负样本预算分配与分布校准的负样本加权两个策略，回答了"每个正样本应采多少负样本"和"每个负样本应贡献多少"两个问题，在3个真实KG与4个KGE模型上平均取得最佳性能，并将假负样本减少90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGE通常采用带负样本的对比学习来学习判别性表示，虽在实践中成功，但两个关键问题未被充分探索：(Q1) 每个正样本应使用多少负样本；(Q2) 每个负样本应对学习贡献多少。现有方法普遍采用固定预算与均等权重，导致开放世界KG中假负样本风险高、训练效率低。

Method: RADAR包含两个策略：(1) 风险感知负预算分配——将开放世界KG建模为闭世界对应体的随机部分观测，量化假负样本采样风险，并据此为每个正样本自适应分配负样本预算；(2) 分布校准负加权——基于负样本分数分布的两个互补特征（离散度与集中度）校准每个正样本的加权强度，从而确定各负样本的权重。

Result: 在3个真实世界KG与4个KGE模型的组合上开展大量实验，RADAR在所有模型—KG组合上平均取得最佳KGE性能，且相比传统固定预算方法将采样到的假负样本减少90%以上。

Conclusion: 负样本的"数量"与"权重"应随正样本风险与负样本分数分布自适应调整，而非固定预算与均等权重；该思路能同时提升KGE表示质量并显著降低假负样本，为KGE对比学习中的负采样设计提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE), a fundamental technique for knowledge-centric Web and KG-augmented AI applications, is commonly trained through contrastive learning with negative samples to learn discriminative representations. Despite its empirical success, two crucial questions regarding negative samples remain underexplored: (Q1) how many negative samples should be used for each positive sample and (Q2) how much should each negative sample contribute to learning? To answer these questions, we propose RADAR, a novel contrastive learning framework for KGE models with two effective strategies, respectively: (1) Risk-aware negative budget allocation quantifies false-negative sampling risk by modeling an open-world KG as a stochastic partial observation of its closed-world counterpart and adaptively assigns a negative budget to each positive sample based on its risk; (2) Distribution-calibrated negative weighting assigns negative weights by calibrating the weighting strength for each positive based on two complementary characteristics of the negative-score distribution: dispersion and concentration. Extensive experiments on 3 real-world KGs with 4 KGE models reveal that RADAR achieves the best KGE performance on average across all model--KG combinations and reduces sampled false negatives by over 90% compared with conventional fixed-budget methods.

</details>


### [153] [LinMix: Data Mixing as Linear Domain Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37390)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Martin Pawelczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将预训练数据域权重选择视为"域归因问题"，指出常用Dirichlet采样的代理数据混合回归设计较差，提出无超参的顶点采样方法LinMix，在capped simplex顶点处采样混合使各域效应解耦，得到可解释线性模型并将系数作为域归因，理论证明其最优性且Dirichlet采样会放大归因误差，实验在OLMo2与Qwen3.5上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型性能强烈依赖预训练语料中各域混合比例。主流数据混合方法需训练大量随机混合的小代理模型再拟合回归预测性能，但Dirichlet采样存在需调参、无法支持上百个域的线性拟合等问题，缺乏对回归设计的审视。

Method: 将数据混合识别为域归因问题（回归系数即各域对目标指标的贡献）。基于经典最优实验设计，提出LinMix：在capped simplex的顶点处采样数据混合（每个域取到上限或缺失），无需超参数；顶点采样使域效应去相关，从而产生可解释的线性模型；给定归因值，LinMix的最优混合可证明仅保留正归因域，无需在候选混合上搜索。此外从理论上建立顶点采样的最优性，并证明当Dirichlet混合集中于先验数据混合时会放大归因误差。

Result: 1B参数OLMo2与4B Qwen3.5预训练实验中，LinMix在推理、代码、数学基准上优于Dirichlet数据混合，MBPP相对提升46%、GSM8K提升14%；在288个细粒度域上仅顶点采样支持线性拟合，LinMix较最佳非线性基线平均提升11%基准准确率。

Conclusion: 无超参顶点采样是一种理论上最优、实践上有效的域归因与数据混合设计，可扩展到大量细粒度域并给出可解释的域贡献与免搜索最优混合。

Abstract: Language model performance depends strongly on the mixture of domains in the pretraining data corpus. Popular data mixing methods select these domain weights by training many small proxy models on random mixes and then fitting a regression model to predict performance from the mix. We recognize this practice as a domain attribution problem, where the regression measures each domain's contribution to the target metric. Through this lens, the standard Dirichlet sampling of proxy data mixes is a poor regression design that requires tuning and cannot support a linear fit for hundreds of domains. Building on classical optimal experimental design, we introduce LinMix, which samples mixes at the vertices of the capped simplex so that each domain is at its cap or absent. This hyperparameter-free vertex sampling decorrelates domain effects, enabling an interpretable linear model whose coefficients are the domain attributions. The optimal mix given by LinMix provably keeps only domains with positive attribution values and requires no search over candidate mixes. We further establish the optimality of vertex sampling, and prove that Dirichlet sampling inflates the attribution error when its mixes concentrate around the prior data mix. Pretraining experiments with 1B-parameter OLMo2 and 4B Qwen3.5 language models show that LinMix outperforms Dirichlet-based data mixing across reasoning, code, and math benchmarks, with relative gains of 46% on MBPP and 14% on GSM8K. At 288 fine-grained domains, only vertex sampling supports a linear fit, and LinMix improves benchmark accuracy by 11% on average over the best nonlinear baseline.

</details>


### [154] [Displacement Moments and Path-Terminal Separation in Brownian Interpolant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37413)
*Zhong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为布朗随机插值（Brownian stochastic interpolants）在任意 Borel 耦合下的标准 Euler 采样建立了早停、目标到输出的 KL 保证，刻画了采样误差与位移矩的关系，并区分了桥路径 KL、比较路径 KL 与终端 KL 的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布朗随机插值连接成对端点样本，但其采样误差可能依赖于位移矩，即使在两个端点都没有一阶矩时也是如此。作者希望给出不依赖端点矩假设的、面向任意 Borel 耦合的显式采样误差刻画。

Method: 从真实源分布出发的标准 Euler 采样；证明早停的目标到输出 KL 界；分析路径 KL 分解以识别马尔可夫投影所丢弃的源信息；构造具有期望输出 KL 一致性的当前状态学习器（current-state learner）；并配合数值诊断。

Result: 四阶位移矩给出显式 O(N^{-1}) 界；二阶矩足以在漂移误差消失时保证固定律收敛。对某个无高于二阶位移矩的固定耦合：原始桥路径 KL 保持为一阶量级，比较路径 KL 为 O(1/log N)，同一网格上终端 KL 至多 C/N^2。当原桥为马尔可夫时，当前状态 Euler 采样具有二阶矩 O((M_2+d)/N) 界；包含概率布朗参考下的有限熵 Schrödinger 桥。

Conclusion: 路径分解揭示了马尔可夫投影所丢弃的源信息；数值诊断展示了各误差项的区别；条件扩散恒等式给出一个复原接口，而图像对照实验未发现相对廉价概率预测器的迭代优势。一般二阶矩耦合下的一致终端速率仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Brownian stochastic interpolants connect paired endpoint samples, yet their sampling error can depend on displacement moments even when neither endpoint has a first moment. For arbitrary Borel couplings, we prove early-stopped, target-to-output KL guarantees for standard Euler sampling from the true source. A fourth displacement moment gives an explicit $O(N^{-1})$ bound; a second moment suffices for fixed-law convergence as drift error vanishes. For one fixed coupling with no displacement moment above two, original bridge-path KL stays of order one, comparison-path KL is of order $1/\log N$, and terminal KL is at most $C/N^2$ on the same grid. The path decomposition identifies the information about the source discarded by Markov projection. When the original bridge is Markov, current-state Euler sampling instead has a second-moment $O((M_2+d)/N)$ bound; finite-entropy Schroedinger bridges for the probability Brownian reference are included. We also construct a current-state learner with expected output KL consistency from iid endpoint pairs. Numerical diagnostics illustrate the distinct errors. Conditional diffusion identities specify a restoration interface, while image controls find no iterative advantage over cheap probabilistic predictors. Uniform terminal rates for general second-moment couplings remain open.

</details>


### [155] [Bayes-Adaptive MDPs for Rollout Allocation to Enhance GRPO Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37429)
*Faeze Moradi Kalarde,Armin Toroghi,Nam Ho Koh,Doo Hwan Hwang,Seungjin Kim,Kee-Eung Kim,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO对所有训练问题分配统一rollout预算、忽略题目难度差异的问题，将rollout预算分配建模为难度感知的资源分配问题，提出AdaRoll-GRPO，用贝叶斯自适应MDP学习自适应分配策略，在总预算不变下提升GRPO训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在LLM后训练中表现优异，但对每个训练问题采用均匀的rollout预算，忽视问题难度差异：高难度问题因rollout过少难以产生有信息量的正奖励信号，对训练贡献受限；中等难度问题则常获得超出所需的rollout，造成预算浪费。

Method: 将难度感知的rollout预算分配形式化为在相同总rollout预算下最大化训练效用的问题；提出AdaRoll-GRPO，把rollout分配重构为序贯决策问题，基于贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（Bayes-adaptive MDP）学习自适应分配策略。该策略离线计算，在GRPO训练中直接应用，不引入额外在线优化开销。

Result: 在多个推理数据集与多种LLM架构上的大量实验表明，在固定rollout预算下，AdaRoll-GRPO能持续提升GRPO的训练性能。

Conclusion: 自适应rollout分配对LLM后训练中的GRPO是有效且低开销的改进方向，可在不增加总预算的前提下按难度重分配计算资源以提升训练效用。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has demonstrated strong performance in LLM post-training. However, it allocates a uniform rollout budget to every training question, ignoring variations in question difficulty. As a result, highly difficult questions may receive too few rollouts to generate informative positive reward signals, limiting their contribution to training, while moderately difficult questions often receive more rollouts than necessary. In this work, we formulate the problem of difficulty-aware rollout budget allocation to maximize training utility under the same total rollout budget as standard GRPO. We propose AdaRoll-GRPO, which reformulates rollout allocation as a sequential decision-making problem and leverages a Bayes-adaptive Markov decision process (MDP) to learn an adaptive allocation policy. The learned policy is computed offline and applied during GRPO training without introducing additional online optimization overhead. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning datasets and LLM architectures demonstrate that AdaRoll-GRPO consistently improves GRPO training performance under a fixed rollout budget, highlighting the effectiveness of adaptive rollout allocation for GRPO in LLM post-training.

</details>


### [156] [When Visual Evidence Is Insufficient: How Instructions, Post-Training, and Internal Activations Shape Refusal in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37430)
*Ananth K Suresh,Aveen Dayal,Gouthaman KV,Aditya Pola,Vineeth N Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比推理时指令、后训练与内部激活干预三种控制来源如何塑造多模态大模型对视觉上不可回答问题的拒答行为，指出拒答≠识别到证据不足，需区分"证据识别"与"学习到的拒答倾向"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的MLLM部署要求模型能识别图像证据不足以支撑问题时拒绝作答，但拒答本身并不能证明模型进行了这种识别——模型可能因无关的安全约束而拒答，或未定位缺失证据就拒答。因此需厘清拒答行为由哪些因素驱动。

Method: 在MoHoBench基准上对三个MLLM系统性地操纵拒答指令的语义与位置，比较监督式与偏好式后训练，并基于自然作答与自然拒答样本提取拒答相关激活方向，对其施加内部激活干预。

Result: 拒答对指令的语义与位置高度敏感，样本层面呈模型特异性变化，被总体拒答率掩盖；证据感知指令与通用拒答指令效果强于刻意错配的安全指令；后训练带来的拒答提升更大，但会产生"拒答语句+尝试作答"混合回答；激活干预效果较小但可跨提示条件迁移，且在测试的可回答样本上未见无差别拒答。

Conclusion: 拒答由外部指令、后训练与内部表征共同塑造，需将证据识别与模型习得的拒答倾向解耦。

Abstract: For reliable deployment, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should recognize when an image provides insufficient evidence to answer a question and withhold unsupported answers. Yet refusal alone is not necessarily evidence of such recognition: models may refuse because of unrelated safety constraints or without identifying the missing evidence. We study how refusal on visually unanswerable questions is shaped by three sources of control: inference-time instructions, post-training, and internal activation interventions. Across three MLLMs on the MoHoBench benchmark, we systematically manipulate the meaning and placement of refusal instructions, compare supervised and preference-based post-training, and intervene on a refusal-related activation direction derived from naturally answered and refused examples. We find that refusal is highly sensitive to both the meaning and placement of instructions, with model-specific changes at the sample level that are obscured by aggregate refusal rates. Evidence-aware and general refusal instructions have stronger effects than an intentionally mismatched safety instruction. Post-training produces larger increases in refusal, but can also induce responses that combine refusal language with attempted answers. In contrast, activation intervention produces smaller yet transferable changes across prompt conditions, without evidence of indiscriminate refusal on tested visually answerable samples. These findings show that refusal is jointly shaped by external instructions, post-training, and internal representations, highlighting the need to disentangle evidence recognition from a model's learned propensity to refuse.

</details>


### [157] [CRISP: Co-designing Pruning and Scheduling for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37442)
*Congwei Zhang,Youming Tao,Yifei Zou,Yu Yang,Ruirui Zhang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Falko Dressler,Xiuzhen Cheng,Dongxiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP通过组合感知的剪枝与运行时调度协同设计，在内存受限边缘平台高效服务MoE模型，吞吐最高提升2.1×，精度损失仅3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台上的MoE推理瓶颈在于专家权重的内存移动而非计算。尽管每个token仅激活少量专家，但路由依赖输入，所有专家必须保持可访问，导致内存搬运主导延迟。现有均匀剪枝与专家丢弃方法忽略了MoE执行的两个特性：专家组合频率高度倾斜、组合内专家贡献不对称。

Method: 提出CRISP组合感知框架：（1）按重要性重排神经元，使一份存储的专家支持多种执行宽度；（2）依据频率与专家角色，对热、温、冷组合分配差异化执行；（3）利用异构宽度实现专家加载与计算的重叠。

Result: 在Mixtral-8x7B与DeepSeek-MoE-16B上，相同内存预算下吞吐最高提升2.1×，相对全宽推理精度损失限制在3.6%。

Conclusion: 高效的边缘MoE服务需联合优化两方面：保留哪些专家容量，以及如何调度专家执行。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference on memory-constrained edge platforms is often dominated by expert-weight movement rather than computation. Although each token activates only a small subset of experts, routing is input-dependent, so all experts must remain accessible and memory movement dominates latency. Existing approaches such as uniform pruning and expert dropping overlook two properties of MoE execution: expert combinations are highly skewed in frequency, and experts contribute asymmetrically within each combination. We present CRISP, a combination-aware framework that co-designs pruning and runtime scheduling for efficient MoE serving on memory-constrained edge platforms. CRISP reorders neurons by importance so one stored expert can support multiple execution widths, assigns differentiated execution to hot, warm, and cold combinations based on frequency and expert role, and exploits heterogeneous widths to overlap expert loading with computation. Across Mixtral-8x7B and DeepSeek-MoE-16B, CRISP improves throughput by up to $2.1\times$ at the same memory budget while limiting accuracy loss to 3.6\% relative to full-width inference. Our results highlight that efficient edge MoE serving requires jointly optimizing which expert capacity is preserved and how expert execution is scheduled.

</details>


### [158] [Interpretable Meta-Policies for Neural Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37451)
*Raban Emunds,Jannis Blüml,Quentin Delfosse,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出层次化强化学习框架NEXUS，将神经技能与符号元策略结合，把透明推理从原子动作上移到高层技能层面，从而在保持环境性能的同时大幅降低符号复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习因神经网络黑箱特性难以解释和与目标对齐；(神经)符号方法虽提升透明度，但直接作用于低层动作会产生过于复杂、不可读的策略。

Method: NEXUS采用层次化RL架构：低层为神经技能（neural skills），高层为符号元策略（symbolic meta-policies）。通过将透明推理移至高层技能而非原子动作来降低符号复杂度；利用以物体为中心的观测（object-centric observations）支持高层推理，并借助LLM交互设计奖励函数与元策略；同时对比神经、符号、神经符号三类元策略架构。

Result: NEXUS智能体学到解耦技能，在高层目标上具有可解释性；相比深度RL，与游戏手册所述目标对齐更好、更不易出现奖励黑客、对游戏简化更鲁棒；并隔离评估了不同元策略架构对环境回报和对齐任务性能的影响。

Conclusion: 把透明性放在技能层面而非原子动作层面，可在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可解释、可对齐的层次化强化学习，且元策略架构选择会显著影响回报与对齐性能。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning remains difficult to interpret and align due to the black-box nature of neural networks. While (neuro-)symbolic approaches improve transparency by incorporating symbolic reasoning, applying them directly to low-level actions results in overly complex, unreadable policies. We introduce NEXUS, a hierarchical RL framework that resolves this tension by combining neural skills with symbolic meta-policies. By shifting transparent reasoning to high-level skills rather than atomic actions, NEXUS drastically reduces symbolic complexity without sacrificing environment performance. Object-centric observations enable high-level reasoning and the design of reward functions and meta-policies by interaction with LLMs. We demonstrate that NEXUS agents learn disentangled skills, are interpretable regarding high-level goals, are better aligned to goals described in the game manual, less prone to reward hacking and more robust to game simplifications than deep RL. Finally, we isolate and evaluate the impact of different meta-policy architectures (neural, symbolic, neuro-symbolic) on environmental return and aligned task performance.

</details>


### [159] [An Input Skip Can Reparameterize a VAE Without Improving the Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37456)
*Kevin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 输入跳连可在不改变模型新样本分布的情况下降低重构误差；但它会按b²缩放重构MSE，而优化结果依赖于参数坐标，故生成质量不能仅由重构误差评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重构误差常被用来评价生成模型，但作者想澄清：降低重构误差并不等于改善生成分布，需检验输入跳连对生成分布、评分与密度估计的实际影响。

Method: 对常数输入跳连0≤α<1的高斯VAE做解析推导：无跳连模型可写成跳连形式（解码输出乘b=1-α、噪声方差乘b²）而不改变观测与隐变量分布及精确生成评分，仅使重构MSE乘b²；并在已知密度的高斯实验与7条DART年度记录上比较能量评分、重要性采样负对数密度估计，另以PPCA为基线，考察由训练重构误差估计观测方差的影响。

Result: 高斯实验中同时调整跳连与观测方差可降低MSE，但能量评分与重要性采样密度估计变差；DART上同一观测方差下能量评分差异在4条原始记录中正负号兼有、在3条2018年记录中偏向跳连；PPCA的平均能量评分低于所有神经DART拟合；保持编码器解码器不变、由重构误差估计观测方差也可能恶化能量评分。

Conclusion: 重构误差与生成分布可解耦，单独训练因参数坐标不同产生不同生成器；评估生成能力需要超越重构误差的指标（如能量评分、密度估计）。

Abstract: An input skip can reduce reconstruction error while leaving a model's distribution of new samples unchanged. For a Gaussian variational autoencoder (VAE) with constant input skip $0\leqα<1$, a model without a skip can be written in skip form by multiplying its decoder output by $b=1-α$ and its noise variance by $b^2$. The distribution of observations and latent variables and its exact generative scores stay the same, while reconstruction mean squared error (MSE) is multiplied by $b^2$. Separate training can produce different generators because optimization depends on the parameter coordinates. In a Gaussian experiment with known density, changing skip and observation variance together reduces MSE but worsens energy score and the estimate of negative log density from importance sampling. On seven annual Deep-ocean Assessment and Reporting of Tsunamis (DART) records, energy score differences at the same observation variance have both signs on four original records and favor the skip on three records from 2018. Probabilistic principal component analysis (PPCA) has lower mean energy scores than every neural DART fit. With the encoder and decoder kept the same, estimating observation variance from training reconstruction error can also worsen energy score. Assessing generation requires more than reconstruction error.

</details>


### [160] [Follow the Gradient: Tracing Safety Degradation Back to Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37481)
*Saket Reddy,Amro Abbas,Anshuman Suri,Ari Morcos,David Schwab,Pratyush Maini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SimpleLoRIF：一种可扩展的梯度嵌入方法，通过简化低秩归因方法并将反向传播限制在最后四个 Transformer 块，实现百万级样本的梯度归因，用于过滤微调数据中"看起来无害"但会侵蚀 LLM 安全性的样本，并构建 SafeTulu。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调数据即使没有任何有害样本，也可能悄然削弱 LLM 的安全性；由于这类降安全性数据表面良性，基于内容的标准过滤器难以识别，因此需要一种能在大规模训练数据上运作的、基于行为对齐的过滤手段。

Method: 1）将低秩梯度归因方法（LoRIF 类）简化，仅对最后四个 transformer 块做反向传播，得到可扩展的梯度嵌入；2）利用梯度与不安全行为的对齐程度识别高风险微调样本；3）在"涌现性错位"上验证梯度可把不安全代码训练数据与微调后的广泛有害行为联系起来；4）基于梯度对齐度剔除 20% 最对齐不安全响应的样本，构建 SafeTulu（Tulu 3 的过滤版本）。

Result: 在错位模型的梯度空间中 AUC 最高达 0.865；在 Qwen、Llama、Olmo 上使用 SafeTulu 训练使 GCG 攻击成功率最多下降 22%，优于 LLM-as-a-judge 与既有梯度过滤方法，且基本保持甚至提升模型能力；对被剔数据的分析发现工具执行失败、叙事算术等被忽视的安全退化来源。

Conclusion: 梯度归因过滤可从"小规模分析工具"升级为面向完整后训练数据集的可扩展安全过滤手段；发布 SafeTulu 及 Hugging Face Space，支持研究者自定义移除比例或仅过滤特定子集以构建定制版本。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on instruction data can quietly erode their safety, even when no training example looks harmful. Because this safety-degrading data looks benign, standard content filters struggle to catch it. We tackle this problem by developing SimpleLoRIF, a scalable gradient embedding method that identifies fine-tuning examples whose gradients align with unsafe behavior. By simplifying a low-rank attribution method and restricting backpropagation to the final four transformer blocks, SimpleLoRIF can embed datasets on the order of a million examples, boosting gradient attribution from a small-scale analysis tool into a practical filter for full post-training datasets. We first validate it on emergent misalignment, where we find that gradients can connect insecure-code training data to the broadly harmful behaviors that appear after fine-tuning, with AUC as high as 0.865 in the gradient space of misaligned models. We then use SimpleLoRIF to build SafeTulu, a version of Tulu 3 with the 20% of examples most gradient-aligned with unsafe responses removed. Training on SafeTulu reduces GCG attack success rate by up to 22% across Qwen, Llama, and Olmo, outperforming both LLM-as-a-judge and prior gradient-based filtering, while largely preserving or even improving capability. Finally, we analyzed the removed data and identify possibly under-studied sources of safety degradation, such as tool-execution failures and narrative arithmetic. We release SafeTulu along with a Hugging Face Space that lets researchers build their own customized versions of SafeTulu to fine-tune on, such as by changing the removal fraction or filtering only certain subsets.

</details>


### [161] [Orthogonal and Parametrized Multi-Attribute Steering of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37517)
*Remy Ogasawara,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Foad Namjoo,Amir Abdullah,Jens Kristian Refsgaard Schou,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可解释的“参数化引导”方法：先定位每个引导向量最易操控的区间并重标定幅度，再以直接设定目标特质强度替代方向推动，并通过向量正交化实现多特质同时引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导（activation steering）在推理阶段修改LLM行为虽高效，但引导信号强度不稳定、引导幅度的语义缺乏统一可解释性；同时多特质引导时朴素向量组合会相互干扰。

Method: 1) 识别每个引导向量的最易操控区间（most steerable interval）并据此重标定引导范围；2) 提出parameterized steering，将激活直接设定到所需特质强度而非沿方向推动；3) 对多特质场景正交化引导向量以解耦特质。

Result: 重标定使引导信号更强且一致；参数化引导提供可解释的强度语义；正交化后各特质解耦，模型可表现单一行为而不诱发其他特质，实现多特质同时引导。

Conclusion: 通过区间重标定、参数化设定与向量正交化，激活引导在强度一致性、可解释性与多特质并发控制方面得到改进。

Abstract: Activation steering offers an effective and efficient way to modify an LLM's behavior at inference time. However, it remains unclear how to apply steering such that the signal is consistently strong and the steering strength has a universally interpretable meaning. We address this first by identifying the most steerable interval for each steering vector and rescaling the steering range accordingly. Building on this, we introduce a more interpretable method for steering, called parameterized steering which instead of pushing activations in a direction, it sets them at the desired trait strength. Next, with multi-trait steering, we find that naively combining vectors causes them to interfere with one another. In response, we develop a method to orthogonalize the vectors; it decouples these traits, allowing a model to exhibit one behavior without inducing the others, thus enabling multiple traits to be steered simultaneously.

</details>


### [162] [The Structure Gap: When More Data Cannot Replace Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37523)
*Kheer Sagar Patel,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"结构编码问题"与"结构鸿沟"概念，论证在已知结构（求解器、对称群、物理定律等）主导的任务中，稠密神经网络即便表达力足够，也会因有限数据而存在无法消除的不确定性；结构化方法则通过构造直接消除该不确定性。论文在高斯逆问题中严格证明：已知算子的Wiener求解器达到贝叶斯风险，而正确线性类中的稠密经验学习器有持久期望超额风险，并给出单元覆盖下界与不可观测轨道不可辨识结果，用实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"足够大的学习者+足够多的数据可使显式领域知识变得可选"这一流行信念。指出对结构编码任务而言，核心问题不是表达能力（稠密网络可包含目标映射），而是有限数据下的辨识、外推与认证。

Method: 定义结构编码问题（输入-输出映射由求解器、对称群、图、物理定律、测量算子、文法、组合搜索空间或优化目标确定或强约束）；引入"结构鸿沟"=获得支配结构的方法与仅获得样本的学习器之间的有限资源差距，并给出其渐近而非绝对形式；在高斯逆问题上严格证明Wiener求解器达到贝叶斯风险而稠密经验学习器存在持久期望超额风险；证明单元覆盖下界与未观测轨道不可辨识性；在已知算子反演、稀疏恢复、图可达性（含规模外推）上做受控实验。

Result: 高斯逆问题中，已知算子的Wiener求解器达到贝叶斯风险，而正确线性类中的稠密经验学习器存在持久的期望超额风险；单元覆盖下界与未观测轨道不可辨识结果成立；已知算子反演、稀疏恢复、图可达性外推实验复现了相应机制。

Conclusion: 结构鸿沟是有限数据下的本质限制：结构无关学习随覆盖度增长可逼近结构化求解器，但任何有限数据集都留有结构按构造即可消解的残余不确定性。当存在经校验的结构时，它可在学习开始前约束假设空间，使学习只需处理结构未指定的部分。

Abstract: A persistent belief in modern AI is that sufficiently large learners trained on sufficiently much data can make explicit domain knowledge optional. This paper examines a limitation of that belief. We define structure-encoded problems: tasks whose input-output map is determined, or sharply constrained, by known structure such as a solver, symmetry group, graph, physical law, measurement operator, grammar, combinatorial search space, or optimization objective. For such tasks, the central question is not expressivity--a dense neural network may contain the desired map--but finite-data identification, extrapolation, and certification. We introduce the structure gap: the finite-resource gap between a method that receives the governing structure and a learner that receives only examples. The strongest useful form of the claim is asymptotic rather than absolute: structure-agnostic learning may approach a structured solver as coverage grows, but every finite dataset can leave residual uncertainty that the structure resolves by construction. We prove this exactly for a Gaussian inverse problem: the known-operator Wiener solver attains Bayes risk, while the dense empirical learner in the correct linear class has persistent expected excess risk. We also prove a cell-coverage lower bound and an unobserved-orbit non-identifiability result. Controlled experiments in known-operator inversion, sparse recovery, and graph reachability with size extrapolation instantiate the mechanisms. When validated structure is available, it can constrain the hypothesis space before learning begins, leaving learning to address components the structure does not specify.

</details>


### [163] [GTFormer: A Syntax-Free Graph Network Alternative to Transformers for 8192$\times$ Length Extrapolation in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37524)
*Elisabeth Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GTFormer：用无句法的分层GNN（平衡二叉树+边界桥接边+随机扩展边+凸高速门控+自顶向下广播）替代扁平自注意力，以O(N)内存实现从N≤512零样本外推到N=4,194,304（8192×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的全对全token比较带来O(N²)计算与内存瓶颈，且随N超出训练长度出现严重softmax注意力稀释；标准Transformer与RoPE在超长序列上出现分布外注意力崩溃与显存耗尽。作者受代数图学习（WPNet）启发，寻求一种线性的、无需语法先验的长上下文序列建模替代方案。

Method: 提出GTFormer——无句法分层GNN骨干，将原始token流组织为有界度层次图：(1) 平衡二叉归约树，深度⌈log₂N⌉；(2) 水平边界桥接移位边；(3) 随机扩展图（expander）捷径边；(4) 多通道凸高速门控（convex highway gating）；(5) 自顶向下广播传播。全图结构保证O(N)内存。

Result: 在八个长上下文基准（代码、日志、多跳检索）上，仅在N≤512序列训练，零样本外推至N=4,194,304（8192×），单张消费级GPU上保持近完美准确率；基线标准Transformer与RoPE Transformer则因OOD注意力崩溃与OOM失效。

Conclusion: 有界度的分层图结构可作为自注意力的线性复杂度替代，实现指数级长度外推而无需训练期长序列或语法先验，为通用长上下文序列建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Self-attention mechanisms have established the Transformer as the dominant backbone across natural language processing, source code intelligence, and automated theorem proving. However, all-to-all pairwise token comparison enforces a quadratic $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ compute and memory barrier alongside severe softmax attention dilution as sequence length $N$ scales beyond the training horizon. Inspired by recent advances in algebraic graph learning (\textbf{WPNet}), this work introduces \textbf{GTFormer}, a syntax-free hierarchical Graph Neural Network (GNN) that replaces flat self-attention for general long-context sequence modeling. GTFormer organizes raw token streams into a bounded-degree hierarchy combining a balanced binary reduction tree ($\lceil \log_2 N \rceil$ depth), horizontal boundary-bridging shift edges, random expander shortcuts, multi-channel convex highway gating, and a top-down broadcast pass. Across eight long-context code, log, and multi-hop retrieval benchmarks trained exclusively on sequences of length $N \le 512$, GTFormer extrapolates zero-shot to $N = 4{,}194{,}304$ ($8192\times$) on a single commodity GPU in linear $\mathcal{O}(N)$ memory, maintaining near-perfect accuracy where standard and RoPE Transformers suffer from out-of-distribution attention collapse and out-of-memory exhaustion.

</details>


### [164] [Collective Dynamics of Self-Reinforcement: How Cross-Task Coupling Drives Policy Collapse in Unsupervised RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37553)
*Peize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将无标签RLVR在大量问题训练时的推理崩溃解释为一种集体不稳定性：共享耦合放大统一扰动，形成临界问题数N_c，理论与仿真及小Transformer实验均验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签RLVR用"与自身多数投票一致"作为可验证奖励，初期提升推理能力，但训练使用许多问题时奖励趋近1而准确率下降，甚至对每题输出同一答案。作者旨在解释这一崩溃现象及其对数据集规模的依赖性。

Method: 构建问题共享参数的可解多问题模型。分析自强化漂移F的单问题平均斜率⟨F'⟩为正时共享扰动被放大；单题锐化速率被1/N减慢，而共享模以∝λ²（与N无关）增长。推导临界问题数N_c≃ln(1/δ)/(λ²G)。仿真验证标度律；用核版本理论识别保护性耦合与边际熵奖励；在小Transformer的无标签RL中做消融（移除/恢复共享方向）。

Result: 仿真证实：尖锐相变；N_c∝λ⁻²（含对数修正）；不同耦合、置信度剖面与训练变体下存在单一临界放大λ²NG；批大小呈对数依赖；初始置信的模型不崩溃。小Transformer中移除共享方向完全防止崩溃，恢复则崩溃，且崩溃随训练集规模与基座模型熵增大。

Conclusion: 无标签RLVR的崩溃源于问题间共享的参数耦合导致的集体失稳，而非单题行为；临界问题数由耦合强度、初始置信与种子决定。可通过抑制共享方向、保护性耦合与边际熵奖励来稳定训练。

Abstract: Label-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) rewards a language model for agreeing with its own majority vote. It improves reasoning at first but eventually collapses: the self-assessed reward goes to one while accuracy falls, often until the model gives the same answer to every problem. The collapse appears only when training uses many problems. We explain this dataset-size dependence as a collective instability. In a solvable many-problem model in which problems share parameters, a population whose problems prefer different answers amplifies any shared perturbation for as long as the population-averaged slope $\langle F'\rangle$ of the single-problem self-reinforcement drift $F$ is positive. This holds while problems are still uncertain about their answers. Per-problem sharpening, slowed by $1/N$ for $N$ training problems, races against the shared mode, which grows at a rate $\proptoλ^2$ independent of $N$, where $λ$ is the strength of the shared coupling. This yields a critical number of problems $N_c\simeq\ln(1/δ)/(λ^2G)$, where $δ$ seeds the shared mode and $G$ decreases with the initial confidence. Simulations of the model confirm the predictions: a sharp transition, $N_c\proptoλ^{-2}$ up to a logarithmic correction, a single critical amplification $λ^2NG$ across couplings, confidence profiles and training variants, a logarithmic dependence on batch size, and no collapse for confident initial models. A kernel version of the theory identifies a protective coupling (generalization of sharpening) and a marginal-entropy bonus that stabilizes the uniform collective mode. In label-free RL of a small Transformer, removing the shared direction prevents collapse entirely, restoring it causes collapse, and collapse grows with training-set size and base-model entropy.

</details>


### [165] [When uncertainty quantification fails: structural vulnerabilities and corrections for selective prediction under clinical class imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37561)
*Walter Endrizzi,Flavio Ragni,Raffaele Marchesi,Stefano Bovo,Monica Moroni,Giuseppe Jurman,Venet Osmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在严重类别不平衡的预后任务中，标准不确定性度量衡量的是类别归属（数据稀疏性）而非临床模糊性；采用阈值对齐度量（CCR+threshold-aware）与类别分层评估可恢复严格单调性，实现有界且平衡的选择性预测风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性预测依赖"不确定性分数能反映临床模糊性"这一前提，但在以少数类为关注结局的严重不平衡预后任务中，该前提失效：标准不确定性度量退化为类别成员身份的代理，不论个体预测是否清晰都系统性地惩罚少数类患者，从而制造"安全幻觉"。

Method: 在56种合成条件与三个神经退行性疾病队列（多发性硬化、帕金森病、阿尔茨海默病，覆盖严重不平衡至近平衡）上评估风险轨迹；对比全局指标与类别分层指标；分析CCR（class-conditional/coverage-based risk）配合标准熵在τ=0.5时破坏类内单调性的问题，并提出CCR与threshold-aware（阈值对齐）度量的组合方案。

Result: 框架在各风险轨迹上持续保持有界且平衡的风险；全局指标则要么使少数类灵敏度崩溃，要么以牺牲多数类为代价保护少数类。CCR+标准熵因τ=0.5时破坏类内单调性而无法保护少数类；CCR与threshold-aware度量结合可恢复严格单调性、正确识别模糊病例，并安全缓解少数类被排除的问题。

Conclusion: 临床类别不平衡下，标准不确定性量化度量的是数据集稀疏性而非临床模糊性，形成系统性的安全错觉；该偏差是可修正的结构性缺陷。部署选择性预测的临床流程应采用阈值对齐的不确定性度量与类别分层评估，以降低类别特定风险失衡，在不静默排除最脆弱患者的前提下实现更安全的选择性预测。

Abstract: Background. Selective prediction systems improve the safety of clinical artificial intelligence by deferring ambiguous cases to human experts while automating high-confidence decisions. However, this safety mechanism relies entirely on the premise that uncertainty scores accurately reflect clinical ambiguity. In prognostic tasks characterized by severe class imbalance, where the clinical outcome of interest is a minority, this assumption breaks down. Standard uncertainty measures cease to capture clinically aligned ambiguity; instead, they become a proxy for class membership, systematically penalizing minority patients regardless of their individual predictive clarity.
  Results. Across 56 synthetic conditions and three neurodegenerative cohorts spanning severe class imbalance to near-balanced conditions (multiple sclerosis, Parkinson's Disease, Alzheimer's Disease), our framework consistently maintained bounded, balanced risk across trajectories. In contrast, global metrics either collapsed minority Sensitivity or protected the minority at the expense of majority patients. Applying CCR with standard entropy fails to protect the minority because it breaks within-class monotonicity when tau=0.5. Combining CCR with threshold-aware metrics restores strict monotonicity, correctly identifies ambiguous patients, and safely mitigates minority exclusion.
  Conclusions. Under clinical class imbalance, standard uncertainty quantification measures dataset sparsity rather than clinical ambiguity, creating a systemic illusion of safety. This bias is a correctable structural flaw. Clinical pipelines deploying selective prediction under imbalance should adopt threshold-aligned uncertainty measures and class-stratified evaluation. This reduces class-specific risk imbalance, supporting safer selective prediction under the evaluated conditions without silently excluding the most vulnerable patients.

</details>


### [166] [Pneuma: A Large-Scale Real-World Time Series Benchmark for Commercial Aircraft Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37563)
*Jiafeng Lin,Yutian Zhang,Huan Zhang,Yuxuan Wang,Jinyan Feng,Yuhui Liu,Zhongyi Pei,Jianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Pneuma：首个基于在役商用飞机机载时序的公开大规模预测性维护基准，含超9.2亿多维时间步、4种机型、9类经核验故障场景，围绕预测、故障分类、异常检测三项任务评测25+模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用航空预测性维护缺乏公开、大规模的真实数据集，现有方法多基于专有数据开发与评估，导致可复现性差。

Method: 构建以引气系统为核心的机载多元时序数据集，标签由真实维修记录导出；在统一设置下，于9个精挑子集上评测四类方法家族中的25+模型，覆盖多变量预测、有监督故障分类与无监督异常检测三项任务。

Result: 数据集呈现显著类别不平衡、故障演化信号微弱、机型配置差异大等真实挑战；即使当代强模型也难以刻画细微故障特征；增加上下文示例与多变量推理可稳定带来增益；预测质量与异常检测性能强相关。

Conclusion: Pneuma 为面向真实部署的数据驱动预测性维护提供严格且可复现的基础，并为复杂航空时序建模提供实践性启示。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance is vital for commercial aviation, yet progress remains hindered by the lack of public, large-scale real-world datasets. As a result, most methods are developed and evaluated on proprietary data, limiting reproducibility. We introduce Pneuma, the first publicly available large-scale benchmark for predictive maintenance using onboard time series from in-service commercial aircraft. Built around bleed air systems, Pneuma contains over 920 million multivariate time steps across four aircraft types and 9 verified fault scenarios, directly aligned with real maintenance logs. The dataset exhibits key real-world challenges, including substantial class imbalance, subtle fault evolution patterns, and significant variance across aircraft configurations. We structure the benchmark around three tasks: multivariate forecasting, supervised fault classification, and unsupervised anomaly detection, with labels derived from actual maintenance records. Under unified settings, we evaluate over 25 models from four methodological families on 9 carefully curated subsets. The results reveal that even strong contemporary models struggle with subtle fault signatures. Further analyses show consistent gains from additional in-context examples and multivariate inference, while forecasting quality is strongly associated with anomaly detection performance. Together, these findings provide practical insights into effectively modeling complex real-world aviation time series. We hope Pneuma will serve as a rigorous and reproducible foundation for advancing data-driven predictive maintenance toward real-world deployment in commercial aviation.

</details>


### [167] [Gym-Anything-RT: Scaling Real-Time Environments for Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37567)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gym-Anything-RT，用自动生成的可暂停/减速视觉小游戏，可控对比考察视觉语言智能体的实时交互能力，发现巨大能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体在数学、软件工程、桌面操作等可充分思考的任务上进展显著，但实时交互场景（如玩游戏、操作实体设备）中环境会在推理或前向计算期间持续变化，其能力尚不清楚，且缺少可控制、可解耦“实时性”要求的评测手段。

Method: 基于六类实时交互能力分类法（含环境感知、动态状态下行动等），用智能体化流水线自动生成视觉小游戏作为测试环境；关键设计是同一小游戏可按实时、暂停或减速三种模式运行，从而在控制变量的条件下比较实时要求带来的影响。据此构建100个实时小游戏并评测AI智能体。

Result: 实时交互存在显著能力缺口：GPT-6 Astra (xHigh) 实时模式下通过率仅9.0%，环境暂停时升至75.0%。并观察到前沿智能体的有趣行为：具备写代码能力时，它们倾向写代码绕过实时感知与行动的要求。

Conclusion: 实时交互是当前视觉语言智能体的主要短板，且可通过暂停/减速实现实时性要求的可控消融分析；作者公开Gym-Anything-RT及生成的小游戏以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: AI agents have made substantial progress in domains where they have a non-trivial amount of time to deliberate between actions, such as mathematics, software engineering, and performing tasks on desktop applications. It is less clear how such models fare in settings that demand real-time interaction, such as playing a video game or operating a physical device. In these settings, the environment state may evolve as the agent is reasoning, or even as the underlying model is performing a forward pass. To gain insight into the real-time interaction capabilities of vision-language agents, we introduce Gym-Anything-RT. Our key idea is to construct visual minigames that demand a portfolio of capabilities required for interactive real-time settings. Gym-Anything-RT uses an agentic pipeline to automatically generate such environments based on a taxonomy of six capabilities required for real-time interaction, including the ability to perceive the environment and act under an evolving environment state. A key feature of Gym-Anything-RT is that it allows for controlled comparison of real-time interaction capabilities: we can run the same minigame in real time, or pause or slow down the environment to relax the requirement for real-time actions. We use Gym-Anything-RT to construct 100 real-time minigames and evaluate AI agents on them. We find large capability gaps in real-time interaction. For example, GPT-6 Astra (xHigh) achieves a pass rate of only 9.0% on the real-time minigames, which rises to 75.0% when the environment is paused. We also identify intriguing behaviors by the most powerful frontier agents: for instance, when given the ability to write code, these agents write code that bypasses the need to perceive and act in real time. We publicly release Gym-Anything-RT and the generated minigames to spur further research on AI agents in real-time interactive settings.

</details>


### [168] [Understanding the limits of latent world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37588)
*Rodrigo Gonzalez Laiz,Steffen Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"忠实潜世界模型"(faithful latent world model)概念，将其刻画为环境转移映射与学习到的潜动力学之间的等变条件，并给出潜 rollout 一致性（LRC）指标来评估动作条件下的潜预测；在现有SOTA潜世界模型上发现无一真正通过忠实建模环境动力学来完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型被视为支撑智能体长时间/空间尺度推理的核心组件，但评估多聚焦任务性能，缺乏对"学到的潜动力学是否反映环境底层结构"的洞察，因此需要一个直接衡量潜预测忠实性的准则与度量。

Method: 概念上定义忠实潜世界模型为环境转移映射与学习潜动力学之间的等变性（equivariance）条件；据此提出潜 rollout 一致性（LRC, latent rollout consistency）指标，用于评估动作条件（action-conditioned）下的潜预测质量。

Result: 在所研究的多个SOTA潜世界模型上，均未发现通过忠实建模环境动力学来求解任务的情形，即任务成功可能依赖非忠实或非动力学的捷径。

Conclusion: 任务性能不足以证明世界模型的忠实性，需以等变性/LRC为补充评估维度；现有潜世界模型的潜动力学与原环境结构存在系统性偏差。

Abstract: World models are widely proposed as core components for enabling agents to reason over long temporal and spatial horizons. Yet current evaluations often emphasize task performance while providing limited insight into whether learned world models acquire predictive latent dynamics that reflect the underlying structure of the environment. Here we introduce the concept of faithful latent world models as an equivariance condition between the environment's transition map and the learned latent dynamics. We propose latent rollout consistency (LRC) as a metric to evaluate action-conditioned latent prediction. Strikingly, we find that across the studied state of the art latent world models, none solves the task by actually faithfully modeling the dynamics of the environment.

</details>


### [169] [GAS-PFN: Graph-Aware Spatiotemporal Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Zero-Shot Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37598)
*Joseph Natter,Long Vu,Yifan Zhang,Bobin Deng,Kun Suo,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAS-PFN，将道路拓扑通过随机游走扩散注入PFN合成任务先验，并设计双掩码条件的两阶段Transformer推理架构，实现无需参数更新的零样本交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通预测需同时建模复杂时序动态与路网拓扑空间依赖。监督式图模型性能强但绑定特定数据集与传感器图，迁移部署成本高；PFN虽可零样本推理，但现有预测类PFN未显式表示图结构依赖。

Method: 将采样的道路拓扑经随机游走扩散融入合成任务先验，使预训练阶段学习图耦合动态；协同设计推理架构，在共享Transformer骨干上进行两次掩码条件前向，分别建模传感器特定时序动态与邻域传感器的时空交互，再融合表征进行预测。

Result: 在六个基准、90分钟预测跨度下，相较最接近的PFN前作TimePFN平均降低MSE 34.0%；相较四个近期工业界通用零样本预测器平均降低MSE 27.7%（所有模型均使用90分钟连续历史，额外上下文取自各数据集按时间划分的训练集）。

Conclusion: 将PFN先验与推理架构同图结构对齐，可提升零样本交通预测的上下文学习能力。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting requires modeling complex temporal dynamics and the spatial dependencies imposed by road topology. Supervised graph-based models achieve strong performance, but are often trained for a particular dataset and sensor graph, making deployment to new or evolving networks costly. Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) offer a zero-shot alternative by learning in-context inference from synthetic task priors, which provide an explicit mechanism for encoding domain-specific assumptions. Existing forecasting PFNs, however, do not explicitly represent the graph-structured dependencies governing traffic dynamics. To combine graph-aware traffic modeling with zero-shot adaptation, we introduce the Graph-Aware Spatiotemporal Prior-Data Fitted Network (GAS-PFN). GAS-PFN incorporates sampled road topology into its synthetic task prior through random-walk diffusion, teaching the model to learn graph-coupled dynamics during pretraining. Its co-designed inference architecture uses two mask-conditioned passes through a shared Transformer backbone to separately model sensor-specific temporal dynamics and spatiotemporal interactions among neighboring sensors before integrating their representations for prediction. This design enables GAS-PFN to use target-network structure and context without parameter updates. Across six benchmarks at the 90-minute horizon, GAS-PFN reduces MSE by 34.0% on average relative to TimePFN, its closest PFN-based predecessor. It also reduces MSE by 27.7% on average relative to four recent industry-developed general-purpose zero-shot forecasters. In this comparison, all models receive 90 minutes of contiguous recent history, and any additional context is drawn from each dataset's chronological training split. These results indicate that aligning PFN priors and inference architectures with graph structure improves in-context learning for zero-shot traffic forecasting.

</details>


### [170] [EnvSched: Environment-Aware Scheduling for Rollout-Efficient Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37609)
*Sihan Yang,Weichen Bai,Ruichuan An,Yiming Cheng,Zhiyuan Feng,Tianyi Jiang,Renye Yan,Ning Ding,Yifan Zhang,Renrui Zhang,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EnvSched，一种从 rollout 历史在线学习的“环境感知调度器”，通过对环境-任务对构建双关系图并预测任务内奖励方差，在有限 rollout 预算下提升智能体强化学习的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的可执行环境规模扩大，rollout 生成与验证成本高；在有限 rollout 预算下，如何在“环境层面”做数据管理（组织、评估、调度任务实例及其环境）以提升智能体 RL 效率，尚待研究。

Method: EnvSched 在线调度器，含两类互补价值建模：exploitation（优先可能产生有信息量奖励差异的任务）与 coverage（将批次分散到欠观察的环境与交互模式）。将环境-任务对作为双关系图节点：环境内边刻画任务语义与共享工具/状态/验证器结构，跨环境边刻画抽象执行与奖励拓扑。沿两类边传播历史 rollout 统计量以预测任务内奖励方差；调度器据此把每批分配给高预测方差且欠观察的环境与任务，并以环境配额与相似度惩罚抑制集中与冗余，无需额外 pilot rollout。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上做 RL，训练 40 步后在 BFCL-v3、tau-Bench、ACEBench-Agent 上平均得分 48.81，超过随机任务选择训练 120 步的最高分；在相同训练预算下，跨模型规模亦提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 环境级数据管理与基于图的价值预测调度可显著提升智能体 RL 的样本/rollout 效率，为有限预算下的智能体训练提供有效方案。

Abstract: As executable environments for language agents scale, generating and verifying their rollouts becomes increasingly expensive. Under finite rollout budgets, this raises an important question: can data management at the environment level make agentic reinforcement learning (RL) more efficient by organizing, evaluating, and scheduling task instances together with the environments that determine their tools, states, transitions, and verification? To address this question, we introduce EnvSched (Environment-aware Scheduling), an online scheduler that learns from rollout history. EnvSched assigns scheduling value through two complementary modeling choices: exploitation prioritizes tasks likely to provide informative reward differences, while coverage spreads each batch across underobserved environments and interaction patterns. EnvSched organizes environment--task pairs as nodes in a dual-relation graph, with within-environment edges capturing task semantics and shared tool, state, and verifier structure, and cross-environment edges capturing abstract execution and reward topology. By propagating historical rollout statistics along both edge types, it predicts within-task reward variance. These predictions guide a scheduler that allocates each batch to tasks with high predicted reward variance and underobserved environments and tasks. Environment quotas and a similarity penalty further limit concentration and redundancy, yielding informative and diverse batches without additional pilot rollouts. In RL on Qwen3-8B, EnvSched achieves an average score of 48.81 across BFCL-v3, tau-Bench, and ACEBench-Agent after 40 training steps, exceeding the highest score attained by random task selection after 120 steps. It also improves downstream performance across model sizes at the same training budget.

</details>


### [171] [MuonGS: Pretraining LLMs with Relaxed Weight-Norm Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37647)
*Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以"累积有效学习率" $C_T=\sum_{t<T}\eta_t/\|W_t\|_F$ 统一分析 Muon 类优化器的权重范数控制，指出 vanilla Muon 仅能对数累积，并据此提出 MuonGS（Gram–Schmidt 去除径向分量），证明其 $C_T=\Omega(\sqrt{T})$ 且在预训练中优于 MuonH。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减与显式范数约束能提升语言模型预训练优化器性能，但"为何有效"以及"需要多少控制"缺乏统一理论解释；同时 vanilla Muon 经验上收敛进度偏慢，其机理不明。

Method: 引入累积有效学习率 $C_T=\sum_{t<T}\eta_t/\|W_t\|_F$ 作为分析工具：在有界更新方向下，任意训练区间内的累积量可界定相对权重位移。据此比较 vanilla Muon（仅对数累积）、MuonW/MuonH（至少线性累积）；并进一步证明足够缓慢的范数增长可获得快于对数最坏情形的累积。基于该洞见提出 MuonGS：用 Gram–Schmidt 投影移除每次 Muon 更新的径向分量而不固定权重范数，并给出理论分析。

Result: 在更新方向有界、名义学习率恒定时，MuonGS 的权重范数最多按 $\sqrt{t}$ 增长，从而保证 $C_T=\Omega(\sqrt{T})$，后期训练仍可持续累积；对光滑尺度不变目标给出 $O(T^{-1/2})$ 的稳态保证。在 160M、400M 稠密模型及 135M 激活/1B 总参数 MoE 预训练中，长训练周期下 MuonGS 验证困惑度低于 MuonH，稠密模型在匹配验证损失时估计等效加速约 15%。

Conclusion: 范数控制的作用可通过累积有效学习率统一刻画：对数累积限制探索，而亚线性（$\sqrt{t}$）范数增长可实现 $\Omega(\sqrt{T})$ 的持续累积。MuonGS 以非固定范数的方式实现这一目标，理论保证与大规模预训练实证一致，表明更宽松的范数控制设计是可行的。

Abstract: Weight decay and explicit weight-norm constraints improve optimizer performance in language model pretraining, yet a unified understanding of why such control helps and how much is needed remains incomplete. We analyze norm control in Muon over the entire training trajectory using the \emph{accumulated effective learning rate}, $C_T=\sum_{t<T}η_t/\|W_t\|_F$, where $η_t$ is the nominal learning rate and $η_t/\|W_t\|_F$ is the effective learning rate relative to the weight norm. For bounded update directions, accumulation over any training interval bounds relative weight displacement. We show that vanilla Muon can accumulate only logarithmically, potentially limiting exploration and helping explain its slower empirical progress, whereas MuonW and MuonH guarantee at least linear accumulation under the stated conditions. Going beyond bounded-norm control, we show that sufficiently slow norm growth guarantees faster accumulation than the logarithmic worst case, opening up less restrictive optimizer designs.
  Based on this insight, we propose MuonGS, which uses Gram--Schmidt (GS) projection to remove the radial component of each Muon update without fixing the weight norm. With bounded update directions and a constant nominal learning rate, we prove that the weight norm grows at most proportionally to $\sqrt{t}$ after $t$ steps. Consequently, MuonGS guarantees $C_T=Ω(\sqrt{T})$, with accumulation sustained even in later training. Further, for smooth scale-invariant objectives we establish its $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity guarantee. In pretraining experiments with 160M- and 400M-parameter dense models as well as a 135M active- and 1B total- parameter MoE model, MuonGS achieves lower validation perplexity than MuonH at long training horizons. In particular, for the dense model it achieves an estimated token-equivalent speedup up to about $15\%$ at matched validation loss to MuonH.

</details>


### [172] [Scaling Laws for Tool-Use Agents via Controllable Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37648)
*Andrew Szot,Samira Abnar,Omar Attia,Abbas Kazerouni,Michael Kirchhof,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Raviteja Vemulapalli,Alexander Toshev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SynthAPI合成工具使用环境生成管线，通过在工具、任务、数据库三个维度控制环境多样性，在458,752个任务、849个模型上拟合出预测下游泛化的缩放律，并据此构建更强的工具使用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能完成复杂多步任务的高性能LLM工具使用智能体，关键在于合成工具使用环境的数据组成如何影响训练效果，而现有工作缺乏对"环境多样性构成"与下游泛化之间可预测关系的系统性刻画。

Method: 提出SynthAPI管线，显式沿工具、任务、工具所操作数据库三条轴控制合成环境的多样性；系统性改变生成控制参数，覆盖458,752个任务与4种规模的849个训练模型，拟合出以下游工具使用智能体泛化为目标、以合成环境规模与组成为自变量的缩放律。

Result: 得到可预测的缩放关系，并归纳出若干经验：随数据预算增长，扩大工具与数据库多样性比单纯增加任务数量更有效；提高数据复杂度带来更大收益。按此结论扩数据后，智能体在四个评测基准上优于先前大规模工具使用智能体与合成环境生成工作训练出的模型。

Conclusion: 合成环境的数据"组成"而非仅"数量"决定了工具使用智能体的泛化能力，系统性的缩放律可为数据规模扩张提供可预测的指导，从而以更高数据效率获得更强智能体。

Abstract: We explore how to build performant large language model (LLM) tool-use agents that complete complex, multi-step tasks. Specifically, we study how data composition in synthetically generated tool-use environments affects the training of such agents.
