To do so, we propose the SynthAPI pipeline, which explicitly controls the diversity of generated environments along the axes of tools, tasks, and the databases on which the tools operate. By systematically varying SynthAPI's generation controls in 458,752 tasks across 849 trained models of four different sizes, we derive a scaling law that predicts downstream tool-use agent generalization based on the scale and composition of the synthesized environments. These predictable relationships reveal several lessons for scaling tool-use agent data. As data budgets grow, scaling tool and database diversity becomes more effective than increasing the number of tasks, and increasing data complexity yields larger gains. Scaling according to these findings yields agents that outperform models trained on data from prior work in large-scale tool-use agent and synthetic environment generation across four evaluation benchmarks.

</details>


### [173] [Attention as Lattice Gauge Theory: Relations Interact Only Through Wilson Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37656)
*Aakash Sarkar,Ananya Mukherjee,Motoshige Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"实体坐标框架可独立旋转"（逐实体框架自由度）这一对称性出发，证明关系组合的等变更新与不变量评分具有唯一形式——Wilson环（holonomy）类函数与共轭等变更新 $U=\Psi(H)T_{ij}$，并据此提出无训练的 holonomy attention。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系可经共享实体复合（如亲属链），且复合顺序有方向性，验证复合关系本质上是"闭环"检验。现有知识图谱嵌入、AlphaFold三角更新、Edge Transformer等虽有类似"经第三点更新边"的形状，却缺少对称性约束；作者希望从对称性原理推导出唯一允许的形式，统一这些分散的做法。

Method: 假设各实体坐标框架可独立旋转（per-entity gauge freedom）。在纯规范（pure-gauge）扇区分析：无共享环的关系不能相互作用；三角形唯一规范不变评分是其 holonomy $H=T_{ij}T_{jk}T_{ki}$ 的类函数（Wilson环）；边唯一的等变更新为 $U=\Psi(H)T_{ij}$，其中 $\Psi$ 共轭等变。将由此得到的等变子族实例化为通用关系图上的 holonomy attention。

Result: 无训练、仅靠组合，在稀疏图、五分之一边损坏条件下以 0.88 推断未观测的 $S_4$ 关系，接近专门同步算法；所有交换（commutative）架构上限为 0.083。holonomy 评分检测损坏三角形 AUC 达 0.99，而交换评分为第二选择规则所限上限 0.76；并能无标签分离聚类。

Conclusion: 边注意力经由 Wilson 环并非众多设计之一，而是对称性允许的唯一形式；交换算子嵌入是其阿贝尔扇区，AlphaFold 三角更新与 Edge Transformer 只具其形而无其约束。该框架为关系推理提供了原理性、可验证且无需训练的构造。

Abstract: Relations compose through the entity they share - Alice is Bob's mother and Bob is Carol's father, so Alice is Carol's grandmother - and the order matters, since a father's mother is not a mother's father; verifying a composed relation means closing the loop. We show that if each entity's coordinate frame may be rotated independently (per-entity frame freedom), three things follow. In the pure-gauge sector, relations that share no cycle cannot interact at all. The only gauge-invariant score of a triangle is a class function of its holonomy $H = T_{ij} T_{jk} T_{ki}$ - a Wilson loop. And the only equivariant update of an edge is $U = Ψ(H)\, T_{ij}$ with $Ψ$ conjugation-equivariant. Edge attention by Wilson loops is therefore not one design among many but the unique form the symmetry permits. Three communities hold pieces of this form: the commuting-operator embeddings of knowledge graphs are its abelian (commuting) sector; AlphaFold's triangle update and the Edge Transformer have its shape - an edge updated through the third vertex - without its constraints; and lattice QCD builds it equivariantly on lattices. We instantiate the equivariant sub-family on general relational graphs as holonomy attention. With no training and composition alone, it infers unobserved $S_4$ relations on sparse graphs at 0.88 with a fifth of the edges corrupted, at or slightly below specialist synchronization algorithms, where every commutative architecture is capped at 0.083; its holonomy score flags corrupted triangles at AUC 0.99 where commuting scores are capped at 0.76 by a second selection rule, and it separates clusters without labels.

</details>


### [174] [Heads of Security: Learned Attention Gates Improve Prompt Injection Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37657)
*Igor Shilov,Xiaoxue Yang,Alexander Panfilov,Evgenii Kortukov,Matthieu Meeus,Peter Romov,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Geiping,Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Heads of Security (HoS)，通过一组可学习的、按注意力头粒度的门控实现指令-数据分离，从而防御提示注入攻击，在鲁棒性与效用上优于 SecAlign++ 与 ASIDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入防御难以兼顾鲁棒性与模型效用：微调类方法（如 SecAlign）在自适应攻击下易失效且损害少样本性能；架构类方法（如 ASIDE）虽内建指令-数据分离，但在工具使用上效用极差。作者希望以更细粒度、更有针对性的注意力干预实现更好的安全性-效用权衡。

Method: HoS 为每个注意力头学习一个小型注意力门控，学习各头如何在可信 token 与不可信 token 之间重新分配注意力，使模型能够在不可信内容上进行推理而不执行其中注入的指令。在 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 与 GPT-OSS-20B 上训练与评估，与 SecAlign++（微调类 SOTA）和 ASIDE（架构类防御）对比，并引入经自动搜索强化的白盒攻击 Claudini 进行评估。

Result: 黑盒 RL-Hammer 攻击下，GPT-OSS-20B 与 SecAlign 的 ASR 分别达 97% 和 85%，HoS 仅 10%；白盒 Claudini 在 HoS 上达到同等 ASR 需约 3 倍 FLOPs。HoS 在标准多选题与提示设置下保持基座模型效用，而 SecAlign 与 ASIDE 在少样本场景下退化。静态攻击的智能体基准中，HoS 与 SecAlign 表现相近，且显著优于基座模型的安用权衡；ASIDE 工具使用效用仅 10%。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的注意力干预（逐头门控实现指令-数据分离）可以切实获得并提升提示注入鲁棒性，同时保持模型效用，为防御设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce Heads of Security (HoS), a prompt injection defense that enforces instruction-data separation via a small set of learned per-head attention gates. HoS learns how each head should redistribute attention between trusted and untrusted tokens, enabling models to reason over untrusted content without following injected instructions. We evaluate HoS on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and GPT-OSS-20B. We compare it against SecAlign++, a state-of-the-art finetuning-based defense, and ASIDE, a defense that, like ours, builds instruction-data separation into the model architecture. We show that HoS offers greater robustness to adaptive attacks, better performance on standard utility benchmarks, and matches top performance on standard agentic prompt injection benchmarks. More specifically, the black-box RL-Hammer attack reaches 97% ASR against GPT-OSS-20B and 85% against SecAlign, but only 10% against HoS. Claudini, a state-of-the-art white-box attack, strengthened against HoS through autoresearch, requires 3 times more FLOPs to reach the same ASR on HoS as it does on GPT-OSS-20B or SecAlign. HoS preserves base-model utility across standard multiple-choice benchmarks and prompting setups, whereas SecAlign and ASIDE degrade in few-shot settings. On agentic benchmarks with static attacks, HoS and SecAlign perform similarly and substantially improve the security-utility trade-off over the base model. ASIDE struggles with tool use, achieving only 10% utility. These results show that prompt injection robustness can be achieved and improved through targeted attention interventions.

</details>


### [175] [When Should We Intervene at Test Time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37658)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Liangliang Li,Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PORTAL 控制器，决定何时启用测试时自适应干预、标注哪些输入、何时停止查询，将平均精度之外的批次级失效问题转化为带标签预算的可控选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应干预具有双面性：同一干预可能挽救某批次却损害另一批次（如更新归一化统计量在含单一类别的批次上失效）；平均精度掩盖了这些失败，且无法告知模型何时应使用干预。目标标签可指导决策，但标签稀缺，且部分选择本身已经明确。

Method: PORTAL 将每个可用预测规则视为候选，并在当前反馈到达前固定其预测，使每个获取的标签能在同一输入上评估所有候选；融合这些共享比较结果，把查询导向仍有可能超越当前领先者的候选；当剩余标签无法改变选择，或可能的精度增益不足以支撑再次查询时即停止。理论分析将所需反馈量与精度差距及共享错误联系起来。

Result: 实验表明该控制使整体上损失精度的干预在选定批次上变为有益选择；避免因到达顺序变化导致的严重失效，并在完整损坏数据流上保持收益；当固定规则已表现良好时，PORTAL 以极少反馈维持性能；周期性标签检查可发现历史证据需刷新，并在条件变化时帮助控制器恢复；所获标签还能训练更强的候选，选择机制将其近乎全部精度增益带入部署。

Conclusion: PORTAL 以共享比较与自适应停止准则实现高效、可恢复的测试时干预选择；同一反馈同时改善当前决策与后续批次的预测，使标签预算在在线自适应中发挥双重作用。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation helps a deployed model respond to changing inputs, but the same intervention can rescue one batch and damage the next. Updating normalization statistics, for example, can improve corrupted images yet fail when a batch contains mostly one class. Average accuracy hides these failures and cannot tell a model when to use an intervention. Target labels can guide this decision, but they are scarce and some choices are already clear. We introduce PORTAL, a controller that chooses when to deploy an intervention, which inputs to label, and when to stop querying. Each available prediction rule is a candidate, and its predictions are fixed before current feedback arrives. Every acquired label therefore evaluates all candidates on the same input. PORTAL combines these shared comparisons and directs queries toward candidates that can still overtake the current leader. It stops when the remaining labels cannot change the choice or when the possible accuracy gain no longer justifies another query. Our analysis links the required feedback to accuracy gaps and shared mistakes, explaining why some choices need much less evidence. Experiments show that this control turns an intervention that loses accuracy overall into a useful choice on selected batches. It prevents severe failures caused by changes in arrival order and preserves gains across complete corruption streams. When a fixed rule already works well, PORTAL uses little feedback to retain its performance. Periodic label checks reveal when past evidence needs refreshing and help the controller recover as conditions change. The acquired labels also train stronger candidates for future batches, and selection brings nearly all of their accuracy gains into deployment. The same feedback therefore improves both the choice made now and the predictions available to later batches.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Continue or Restart? The Limits of Early Failure Prediction in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35971)
*Srinath Srinivasan,Lunxiao Li,Tim Menzies,Wesley Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文基于147,002条执行轨迹研究发现，早期停止在自主编码智能体中主要依赖任务间的难度分配而非任务内的尝试级判别；在大多数配置下，不间断继续比各种重启策略解出率更高且平均token成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期停止被认为能降低自主编码智能体的算力成本，但决定何时放弃某次运行需要区分"问题基线难度"与"单次尝试的轨迹质量"。作者旨在检验早期前缀是否提供任务内的尝试级判别信号，以及这些预测能否真正转化为净算力节省。

Method: 使用两个公开语料库、四种模型-框架设置下的147,002条执行轨迹；对比预执行问题上下文与早期执行前缀，评估线性计数模型、首轮文本嵌入、任务条件排序器及微调Qwen3-0.6B监控器；在共享token预算下回放分数引导重启、匹配随机重启与不间断继续，并进行精确代数分解。

Result: 早期前缀仅略微提升跨任务结果排序；任务内判别在5k–20k token各早期节点接近随机水平（首轮嵌入与20k token任务条件模型AUC约0.50；三个随机种子、32,406次尝试、20k token下任务内平均AUC为0.50 [0.48, 0.51]）。75k token上限下学习式重启较匹配随机重启提升解出率最多1.98个百分点，但代数分解显示该增益几乎全部来自任务间停止分配，任务内尝试选择贡献近零（-0.11至+0.24个百分点）。四种设置下（以及224种预算-阈值配置中的216种），不间断继续均以更低平均token成本获得更高解出率。

Conclusion: 早期停止的实用评估必须同时确立尝试级判别能力，并证明相对于不间断继续的净算力节省；仅靠跨任务难度分配不足以支撑其价值。

Abstract: Early stopping promises to reduce compute costs for autonomous coding agents by terminating unpromising runs early. Deciding when to abandon a run requires separating baseline issue difficulty from the trajectory of an individual attempt. We study the distinction in 147,002 execution traces from two public corpora spanning four model-framework settings. Comparing pre-execution issue context against early execution prefixes, we evaluate linear count models, initial-turn text embeddings, task-conditional rankers, and a fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B monitor. Although early prefixes modestly improve cross-task outcome ranking, within-task discrimination remains near chance across early landmarks (5k-20k tokens): initial-turn embeddings and 20k-token task-conditional models yield AUCs around 0.50, and three-seed neural evaluations across 32,406 attempts at 20k tokens achieve a mean within-task AUC of 0.50 [0.48, 0.51] across tasks of varying solve rates. To evaluate how these predictions translate into stopping decisions, we replay score-guided and matched random restarts under shared token budgets alongside uninterrupted continuation. Under a 75k-token cap, learned restarting improves resolution over matched random restarting by up to 1.98 percentage points. An exact algebraic decomposition resolves this paradox: the gain over random restarting stems almost entirely from between-task stopping allocation (avoiding stops on easy issues), whereas attempt-level selection within a task contributes near zero (-0.11 to +0.24 percentage points). Across all four settings at the 75k cap (and 216 of 224 tested budget-threshold configurations overall), uninterrupted continuation achieves higher solve rates at lower mean token cost than both restart policies. Practical evaluations of early stopping must therefore establish attempt-level discrimination and demonstrate net compute savings relative to continuation.

</details>


### [177] [Testing Agentic Service Systems: An Agentic Living Lab Methodology Across the Physical-Digital Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35978)
*Zhongjie Wang,Xiang He,Xiao Wang,Mingyi Liu,Shuang Yu,Hanchuan Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"Agentic Living Lab"方法论，以"测试单元、参与者、现实基底"三问组织对代理的评测，用服务事件为单位并显式披露跨基底的实现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以任务成功率衡量代理不足：结果可能未获授权、义务未了结，或依赖执行基底；代理为委托人行动并改变持久业务状态与物理过程，需要更完整的评测框架。

Method: 以含委托角色、义务、世界状态与证据的"服务事件"为测试单元；区分被测代理与环境参与者（真人、人类痕迹、规则替代物、代理型代理）；引入Realization Profiles、能力声明的Reality Substrate绑定、Grounding Profiles与Semantic Delta披露；提出实验控制、参与者、世界实现、证据评估四平面参考架构及贯穿的权限与治理控制；运行时语义区分意图、规范性准入与事实核对。

Result: 发布JSON Schema、确定性fixture与有限参考runner使部分规则可机器校验；构造的出行与工业案例展示了授权与履约失败、代理敏感性及基底变化。

Conclusion: Living Lab主张需真实利益相关者接地、参与者校准/验证与受治理反馈，但不要求每次运行都有同步人类参与；代理质量、代理有效性与跨基底效应稳定性仍属经验问题；所发布工件非完整平台或实证验证。

Abstract: Task success is insufficient for testing agents that act for principals, change persistent business state, and affect physical processes: a useful result may be unauthorized, leave obligations unresolved, or depend on the execution substrate. We propose an Agentic Living Lab methodology organized around three questions: what is the test unit, who and what participate, and under what reality is the test executed? The unit is a service episode containing delegated roles, obligations, world state, and evidence. The method separates agents under test from environment actors and distinguishes real humans, human traces, rule surrogates, and agent-based proxies. Realization Profiles, capability-declared Reality Substrate bindings, Grounding Profiles, and Semantic Delta disclosures make changes across software, enterprise systems, simulation, and physical settings explicit. A four-plane reference architecture separates experiment control, participants, world realization, and evidence evaluation, with cross-cutting authority and governance controls. Runtime semantics distinguish intent, normative admission, and factual reconciliation. Real stakeholder grounding, participant calibration or validation, and governed feedback are required for the Living Lab claim, but not synchronous human participation in every run. Released JSON Schemas, deterministic fixtures, and a limited reference runner make selected rules machine-checkable; they are not a complete platform or empirical validation. Constructed travel and industrial designs illustrate authorization and fulfillment failures, proxy sensitivity, and substrate changes. Agent quality, proxy validity, and cross-substrate effect stability remain empirical questions.

</details>


### [178] [UnityBench-Mini3D: Evaluating LLM Agents for 3D Game Development in Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35980)
*Yijun Zhou,Minghao Shao,Ke Zhang,Renkai Yuan,Hongbo Yuan,Bin Hu,Bozhao Qian,Hongxia Shen,Yanhong Liu,Yuting Chen,Yuntao Shi,Zhongxuan Xu,Shuo Mao,Haijia Zhou,Zhendong Li,Tao Zhang,Chenggang Li,Yuxuan Wang,Zinan Zheng,Calvin Zhang,Jia Li,Heng Chang,Jie Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 UnityBench-Mini3D：77个Unity项目级任务的基准，按7类专家技能与3个难度分级，用1,178个留出的人类撰写里程碑测试在引擎内评估；21个模型×9个编码框架平均通过率57.7%（简单80.9%→困难35.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏开发对编码智能体的压力不同于通用软件工程：正确性取决于引擎API、序列化场景状态与运行时行为的交互，而非仅源码。现有基准多面向Godot等平台，且过度依赖LLM裁判或粗粒度任务级结果，缺乏细粒度、确定性的诊断能力。

Method: 构建UnityBench-Mini3D：77个项目级Unity任务，围绕7个专家定义的技能类别（而非教程或类型划分）、3个难度层级组织；每个任务由完整Unity工程+自然语言需求构成；评估采用1,178个人工编写、留出的里程碑测试，覆盖场景结构、组件配置与运行时行为，在引擎内执行。

Result: 引擎内评估提供确定性的部分得分、可定位失败点，并能区分部分实现与完整实现，无需主观裁判。在21个模型与9个编码框架上，平均通过率总体57.7%，简单任务80.9%，困难任务降至35.2%。

Conclusion: UnityBench-Mini3D 提供可复现、具诊断性的测试平台，能在不同难度层级上区分系统能力，弥补Unity平台与细粒度确定性评估的空白。

Abstract: Game development stresses coding agents in ways general software engineering does not: correctness depends on how engine APIs, serialized scene state, and runtime behavior interact, not on source code alone. Existing benchmarks mainly target platforms such as Godot and heavily rely on LLM judges or coarse task-level outcomes. We present UnityBench-Mini3D, a benchmark of 77 project-level Unity tasks organized around seven expert-defined skill categories rather than tutorials or genres, with three difficulty tiers. Each task pairs a complete Unity project with natural-language requirements and is evaluated using 1,178 held-out, human-authored milestone tests covering scene structure, component configuration, and runtime behavior. This in-engine evaluation provides deterministic partial credit, localizes failures, and distinguishes partial implementations from complete solutions without relying on subjective judges. Across 21 models and 9 coding harnesses, the average pass rate reaches 80.9% on easy tasks but falls to 35.2% on hard tasks, with just 57.7% overall. UnityBench-Mini3D provides a reproducible, diagnostic testbed distinguishing systems across difficulty levels.

</details>


### [179] [Small agents should know what their tools do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35981)
*Alexander Kovalenko,Artem Redchych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 EffectWorld——一个带效果类型（effect-typed）工具的可执行世界与基准，用小模型 Lora 微调即可把调用安全性、调用效率与任务成功率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源模型虽能调用工具，但缺乏对调用"效果"的推理：无询问即删除、重复写入、重复读取已有状态、本可用一段短程序完成却每轮只发一个调用。现有评测缺乏对调用效果安全性与调用效率的显式、可判定度量。

Method: 每个工具声明其效果类型、是否幂等、读写哪些资源；基于声明构建三类组件：(1) 回放验证器（replay verifier），无需标签或 judge 模型即可判定轨迹是否"效果安全"；(2) 执行器，支持单轮执行含多个依赖调用的工具程序；(3) 编译器式优化器，把低效轨迹改写为已验证的训练信号。再用这些数据训练低秩适配器（LoRA）。

Result: Qwen3-4B 上：分布内任务成功率 53%→99%；未见任务类型与干扰工具的留出集 52%→81%；未见领域 74%→83%；输出 token 约减少三分之一。跨模型族可迁移。BFCL 上实时准确率持平基座，多轮准确率 21.9→26.6；含近失样本下降的配方使 BFCL 平均分超过基座且不可相关性检测持平。另发现公开工具语料中的拒答模板会显著损害非实时准确率（88.0→31.4），过滤后恢复至 84.8；正样本（而非负样本）才是收益来源，负样本上的抑制与偏好目标相对纯 SFT 无增益。

Conclusion: 工具声明的效果语义可同时充当可验证的安全判据、多调用执行与训练信号来源，是提升小模型工具调用安全性与效率的有效范式；同时强调数据清洗（去除拒答模板）与正样本驱动的重要性，仅域内评测易掩盖此类陷阱。

Abstract: Small open language models can call tools, but they do not reason about what a call does: they delete without asking, repeat writes, re-read state they already hold, and issue one call per turn where a short program would do. We introduce EffectWorld, an executable world of effect-typed tools in which every tool declares its effect, whether it is idempotent, and which resources it reads and writes. These declarations let a replay verifier decide whether a trace is effect-safe without labels or judge models, let an executor run tool programs of several dependent calls in one turn, and let a compiler-style optimizer turn wasteful traces into verified training signal; together they make EffectWorld a benchmark of effect safety and call efficiency. Low-rank adapters trained on this data deliver large gains: on Qwen3-4B, they raise task success from 53% to 99% in distribution, from 52% to 81% on a held-out split with unseen task kinds and distractor tools, and from 74% to 83% on an unseen domain, with about a third of the output tokens. The gains carry over to other model families. On the Berkeley Function-Calling Leaderboard (BFCL), the adapter matches the base model's live accuracy and raises multi-turn accuracy from 21.9 to 26.6, and a recipe with near-miss declines lifts the BFCL average above the base model's while matching its irrelevance detection. Along the way we uncover and fix a pitfall that in-domain evaluation alone would miss: refusal templates in widely used public tool-use corpora teach a small model not to call tools, cutting BFCL non-live accuracy from 88.0 to 31.4, and filtering them restores 84.8. We also find that positive examples, not negative ones, carry the learning: suppression and preference objectives on the verified negatives add nothing over an SFT-only control, while targeted positives move exactly the behaviour they show.

</details>


### [180] [Generation and Verification: Human-Agent Collaboration for Safety-Critical Automotive Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35982)
*Elizaveta Zinovyeva,Aiham Taleb,Nikita Kozodoi,Daniel Schleicher,Amir Mahdi Namazi,Martin Kraus,Levent Kent,Brian Jensen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出面向汽车 C/C++ 的编码助手：通过 MCP 暴露 generate/edit/verify 三个工具让开发者按需执行验证，并以提示工程与微调定制领域模型；两周生产试点中 verify 成为最常用工具，75% 开发者偏好更快的提示工程配置，检查显示提示工程在 C 上违规更少、微调在 C++ 架构模式符合性更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体研究多关注生成质量，但安全攸关的汽车软件另有两项要求：一是以验证为先，每处变更需经评审、追溯至需求并符合 MISRA、AUTOSAR 等标准方可提交；二是领域狭窄，围绕分层架构、嵌入式资源约束和组织特定接口约定编写，此类数据在通用 LLM 训练语料中稀缺。

Method: 1) 通过 Model Context Protocol 向用户暴露 generate、edit、verify 三个工具，使开发者可对任意代码按需运行验证，而非依赖智能体自查；2) 不采用通用模型，而是通过提示工程与微调将底层 LLM 定制到汽车领域，并基于 AUMOVIO 生产代码构建数据集，将每个函数与其须满足的需求文本及 UML 行为配对；3) 在实际生产项目开展两周试点，并对比两种模型定制策略。

Result: 试点中开发者反馈 verify 为使用最多的工具，并确认其被用于自身代码而非仅用于生成产物；模型定制策略对比中 75% 偏好更快的提示工程配置；受 MISRA 与 AUTOSAR 启发的模式检查器显示，提示工程在 C 代码上违规数更低，而微调在 C++ 上对架构模式的符合性更好。

Conclusion: 工具化的按需验证比智能体自检更契合安全攸关汽车软件以验证为先的工作流；提示工程与微调各有侧重，C 语言可优先采用提示工程以兼顾速度与合规，C++ 架构模式一致性则受益于微调，二者宜按语言与合规目标组合使用。

Abstract: Research on coding agents mainly focuses on generation quality. Yet, safety-critical automotive software makes two additional demands. It prioritizes verification, since every change is reviewed, traced to a requirement, and checked against industry standards such as MISRA and AUTOSAR before it can be committed. It is also a narrow domain, written against layered architectures, embedded resource constraints, and organization-specific interface conventions that are scarce in the data general-purpose LLMs are trained on. We present a coding assistant for automotive C/C++ that addresses both demands. First, it exposes three tools to the user over the Model Context Protocol (generate, edit, and verify), allowing developers to run verify on any code on demand rather than relying on the agent to check its own output. Second, rather than using a general-purpose model, we customize the underlying LLM for the automotive domain through prompt engineering and fine-tuning. We construct a dataset from automotive production code of AUMOVIO, where each function is paired with the requirement texts and Unified Modeling Language (UML) behavior it must satisfy. In a two-week pilot on active production projects, developers report verify as the most-used tool and confirm using it on their own code and not only on generated output. Comparing model customization strategies, 75% prefer the faster prompt-engineered configuration. MISRA- and AUTOSAR-inspired pattern checkers indicate that prompt engineering achieves a lower number of violations on C, while fine-tuning shows better conformance with architecture patterns for C++.

</details>


### [181] [PROBE: Frontier Coding Agents Find Different Bugs Than Maintainers Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35987)
*Marco Paina,Mark Niklas Müller,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PROBE基准，包含20个GitHub仓库18个月内修复的2,457个缺陷，用于系统评估编码智能体在整个仓库范围内主动发现缺陷的能力，并给出将候选发现与部分参考集匹配的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动缺陷发现是重要的软件工程问题，编码智能体已被广泛部署，但其"发现人类维护者会在整个仓库中修复的全部缺陷"这一能力尚未被研究，缺少相应基准。

Method: 构建PROBE基准：汇总20个多样化GitHub仓库在18个月内修复的全部2,457个缺陷，并设计候选发现与部分参考集之间的匹配方法；随后在多种前沿模型上评估编码智能体的召回率与精确率，并辅以LLM与人工评估者有效性判断，以及缺陷分布对比分析。

Result: 所有编码智能体召回率低于21%、精确率低于9%；更强模型将召回率从8%提升至17%，但精确率无改善；尽管精确率低，LLM与人工评估者仍判定85%的发现有效；不同智能体发现的缺陷分布相似，但与维护者修复的缺陷分布差异显著。

Conclusion: 缺陷发现的现实价值显著而前沿模型召回率偏低，PROBE可作为模型使用者与开发者评估和改进编码智能体的重要资源。

Abstract: Proactive bug discovery is an important software-engineering problem. Even though coding agents are already widely deployed in this domain, their capabilities remain understudied. In particular, the challenging task of finding all bugs that human maintainers would repair across an entire repository has not been studied yet. To close this gap, we introduce PROBE, a benchmark consisting of all 2,457 bugs fixed over the course of 18 months across 20 diverse GitHub repositories, together with a method for matching candidate findings to this partial reference set. We evaluate coding agents across frontier models and find they all achieve a recall of below $21\%$ and a precision of less than $9\%$ with respect to the reference set. While stronger models increase recall substantially from $8\%$ to $17\%$, they do not improve precision. Surprisingly, despite the low precision, LLM and human evaluators still judge $85\%$ of findings valid. Crucially, we find that found bug distributions are similar across agents but differ substantially from those of bugs fixed by maintainers. The practical relevance of bug discovery and the low recall of frontier models make this benchmark a valuable resource for both model users and developers.

</details>


### [182] [Before the First Edit? Measuring When Coding-Agent Guidance Actually Arrives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35992)
*Thupakula Sai Varun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文对编码智能体"先规划后编辑"的时机主张进行事件级审计，在76臂Qwen2.5-Coder 14B实验中，15臂对7个任务、3个仓库的Python源码产生修改，所有首次修改前均有指导，但未达预设20臂覆盖目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体框架声称"首次源码编辑前提供指导"，但这一承诺究竟拦截了哪个事件并不清楚；现有规定动作对比只能反映门控规则的覆盖面，无法揭示智能体收到计划后的实际延续行为。

Method: 将"指导"拆解为五个可审计事件：策略分配、请求资格、触发、计划交付、观测到的源码内容变更；对每个执行动作前后的Python文件做哈希；在公开软件issue上运行本地智能体，采用试点引导的76臂研究（Qwen2.5-Coder 14B），并辅以事后局部检验（目标与回归选择器）；同时分析公开的PAUSE与SI2CA轨迹。

Result: 76臂中15臂在7个任务、3个仓库上修改了Python源码；指导均先于每个观测到的首次变更，但所有变更均经由结构化编辑器完成，未达预设20臂覆盖目标；事后局部检验发现7个变更臂通过所测目标与回归选择器，其中4个使用不完整的回归清单；PAUSE与SI2CA轨迹可见被相邻读取夹住的源文本变更，但缺乏同步文件系统状态，无法定位确切的首次突变。

Conclusion: 该审计使"时机"主张在明确源码范围内变得可检验；现有数据不足以证明规划能提升修复正确性，也未覆盖实际运行中以shell优先写入的情形。

Abstract: When a coding-agent harness promises guidance before the first source edit, what event does it actually intercept? We audit five separate events: policy assignment, request eligibility, trigger, plan delivery, and observed source-content change. A prescribed-action comparison exposes the coverage of three gate rules but cannot tell us how an agent would continue after receiving a plan. We therefore run local agents on public software issues and hash Python files before and after each executed action. In a pilot-guided, 76-arm study using Qwen2.5-Coder 14B, 15 arms change Python source across seven tasks and three repositories. Guidance precedes every observed first change, but every change uses a structured editor and the study misses its prespecified 20-arm coverage target. Post hoc local tests find seven changed arms that pass the tested target and regression selectors; four of those seven use incomplete regression lists. Public PAUSE and SI2CA trajectories add source-text changes bracketed by adjacent reads, but lack synchronized filesystem states and cannot identify exact first mutations. The audit makes the timing claim testable within a stated source scope. These data do not show that planning improves repair correctness or covers shell-first writes in live agent runs.

</details>


### [183] [CIVER: Causal Intervention Verification for Reliable Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36010)
*Xinyuan Song,Hasibul Haque,Jay Somasundaram,Ryan Turner,Bowen Zhu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIVER：基于因果干预的LLM代码审查框架，通过"审查-干预-验证"三模块联合优化，区分真实缺陷与误报，在SWE-Review-Bench上正确识别77%的真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理使代码贡献与仓库变更激增，LLM审查代理随之普及；但其生成的审查意见冗长、含大量误报且缺乏证据，可能误导开发者与下游编码代理，因此需要从不可靠的审查意见中区分可执行的发现与无依据的主张。

Method: 提出CIVER因果干预框架，包含三个模块：审查模块（评估发现项）、干预模块（生成面向修复的扰动）、验证模块（对审查可靠性打分）；三模块通过专家迭代（expert iteration）联合优化，使真实缺陷在定向修复下响应、在无关改动下保持稳定。

Result: 在SWE-Review-Bench上，CIVER通过定向修复正确识别77%的真实缺陷；跨项目与下游修复评测表明其可跨仓库泛化，并提升编码代理的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以因果干预信号替代单纯文本判断，可有效提升自动化代码审查的可靠性，并惠及下游编码代理。

Abstract: Code review is essential for maintaining software repositories by identifying defects, improving code quality, and supporting reliable software evolution. With the growing adoption of LLM-based coding agents, an increasing number of code contributions and repository changes are generated by these agents. Consequently, LLM-based code review agents are becoming increasingly prevalent to evaluate these contributions and maintain software quality. However, LLM reviewers often generate lengthy reviews containing numerous potential issues, many of which are false positives or lack sufficient evidence. These unreliable reviews can mislead developers and downstream coding agents, motivating the need to distinguish actionable findings from unsupported claims. We propose CIVER, a causal intervention-based framework that identifies the core issues underlying code-review findings and distinguishes genuine defects from false positives. CIVER integrates three modules: a review module assesses findings, an intervention module generates repair-oriented perturbations, and a verifier module scores review reliability. Through joint optimization via expert iteration, the three modules identify genuine defects by responding to targeted repairs while remaining stable under unrelated changes. Extensive experiments on SWE-Review-Bench demonstrate that CIVER correctly identifies 77% of genuine defects through targeted repairs. Cross-project and downstream repair evaluations further demonstrate that CIVER generalizes across repositories and improves coding agents' task success rates, highlighting its effectiveness for reliable automated code review.

</details>


### [184] [Do Coding Agents Still Need a Debugger? Frontier-Model-Based Fault Localization at the Accuracy Ceiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36015)
*Christoph Daxerer,Johann Glock,Christian Macho,Rudolf Ramler,Martin Pinzger,Josef Pichler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在610个注入了单行缺陷的LeetCodeDataset程序及5种保持语义的重写版本上，Sol 6.1、Luna 6与Qwen 3.8三个编码智能体在无工具条件下即可定位98.2%–99.9%的缺陷；调试器辅助可消除大部分残余错误，但token开销高达14.7–27.0倍，且智能体在被允许时极少真正使用交互式调试器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位缺陷有两条路径：仅从源码静态推理，或借助调试器运行并单步跟踪。早期智能体在静态执行追踪与调试器操作上表现不佳，而前沿LLM已在这两方面提升，因此需要量化评估：无工具时的定位精度、调试器辅助的增益与代价、以及可选情况下智能体的实际使用行为。

Method: 向144个LeetCodeDataset解法注入610个单行缺陷，并用五种行为保持变换配方重写每个缺陷程序；基于Sol 6.1、Luna 6和Qwen 3.8的智能体分别在无辅助、可选辅助、鼓励辅助三种条件下进行缺陷定位。

Result: 无工具时，原始程序定位率为98.2%–99.9%，变换后为96.7%–99.9%，精度损失主要出现在循环与条件结构被重写的配方上。鼓励辅助消除了Luna 6和Qwen 3.8在变换程序上残余错误的63%和61%（分别+2.1和+1.3个百分点），但token消耗为14.7–27.0倍；Sol 6.1保持99.9%。可选辅助下，Luna 6和Qwen 3.8仍分别提升+1.5和+1.3个百分点，而交互式调试器仅在0.2%–6.2%的运行中被使用。

Conclusion: 对于短小且规格明确的程序，当前编码智能体仅凭源码即可定位几乎所有缺陷，调试器辅助能消除大部分残余错误。给予选择权时，智能体运行程序的频率远高于单步跟踪，但仍保留了大部分收益。

Abstract: Background: To localize a fault, a coding agent can reason from source code alone or use debugger assistance by running the program and stepping through it in an interactive debugger. While earlier agents struggled with static execution tracing and debugger operation, frontier LLMs have improved at both. Objective: We evaluate how accurately coding agents localize faults without tools across original and transformed programs, how much debugger assistance improves accuracy and at what cost, and how agents use it when optional. Methods: We seed 610 single-line faults into 144 LeetCodeDataset solutions and rewrite each faulty program with five recipes of behavior-preserving transformations. Agents based on Sol 6.1, Luna 6, and Qwen 3.8 localize each fault with no, optional, or encouraged debugger assistance. Results: Without tools, the agents localized 98.2 to 99.9% of faults in original and 96.7 to 99.9% in transformed programs, losing accuracy mainly where recipes restructured loops and conditions. Encouraged assistance removed 63% and 61% of Luna 6's and Qwen 3.8's remaining transformed errors (+2.1 and +1.3 percentage points) at 14.7 to 27.0 times the token use, while Sol 6.1 stayed at 99.9%. With optional assistance, Luna 6 and Qwen 3.8 still gained +1.5 and +1.3 points, though the agents used the interactive debugger in only 0.2 to 6.2% of runs. Conclusions: On short, precisely specified programs, current coding agents localize nearly all faults from source code alone, and debugger assistance removes most errors that remain. Given the choice, agents executed the program far more often than they stepped through it, yet kept most of the benefit.

</details>


### [185] [AGDecode: Semantic Attribute Grammar Derivatives for Safety-Constrained Decoding of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36105)
*Rajan Maghera,Asher Tempest Arya,Aren A. Babikian,Marsha Chechik,Maryam Mehri Dehnavi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 AGDecode：基于最小不动点语义的可编程语义约束解码框架，将语义约束解码归约为部分语法树上的可实现性问题，并把语义规范编译为关系约束系统交由 SMT/CHC 求解器裁决，从而在 C 程序生成中强制执行路径敏感的安全性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代码生成需要保证结构（语法）与语义正确性。现有约束解码方法虽可强制语法约束及类型安全等特定语义性质，但只针对固定性质，或依赖难以推广到数据相关、归纳式程序性质的操作性剪枝机制，缺乏通用性。

Method: 1) 将属性文法推广到部分构造的语法树；2) 把语义约束解码归约为部分树上的“可实现性（realizability）”问题；3) 将语义规范编译为关系约束系统，交由外部验证 oracle（SMT、CHC 求解器）求解，实现语言语义与约束求解机制的清晰分离；4) 对不确定查询的处理采用保守策略（为保证可靠性而拒绝 token）或推测式继续生成，直至后续查询消解整个生成；5) 在 Rocq 中机械化核心元理论。

Result: 在理想验证 oracle 下证明了方法的可靠性与完备性；在 C 程序语义约束生成任务上，成功执行路径敏感安全性质（空指针安全、无除零、变量先初始化后使用），表明 solver-backed 语义约束解码能够提供丰富的语义保证，且在常规程序边界处计算开销适中。

Conclusion: 语义约束解码可通过“属性文法 + 最小不动点语义 + 关系约束 + 外部求解器”的可编程架构实现，兼顾通用性与可靠性，为 LLM 代码生成中的深层语义正确性提供可行路径，代价是可接受的计算开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, motivating techniques that guarantee the structural and semantic correctness of generated programs. Constrained decoding enforces correctness during generation by restricting token choices to those that admit at least one valid completion. While existing approaches can enforce syntactic constraints and selected semantic properties such as type safety, they either target specific properties or rely on operational pruning mechanisms that are difficult to generalize to data-dependent, inductive program properties. We present AGDecode, a programmable framework for semantic constrained decoding based on least-fixed-point semantics. AGDecode extends attribute grammars to partially constructed syntax trees, and reduces semantic constrained decoding to a realizability problem over such trees. The framework compiles semantic specifications into systems of relational constraints that are discharged by external verification oracles, such as SMT and CHC solvers, cleanly separating language semantics from constraint-solving machinery. We prove that our approach is sound and complete relative to an ideal verification oracle. In practice, solvers inevitably encounter uncertain queries; conservatively, our system can reject tokens for soundness or speculatively continue to generate until later queries resolve the entire generation. We mechanize the core metatheory in Rocq and evaluate the framework on semantic-constrained generation of C programs, enforcing path-sensitive safety properties including null-pointer safety, division-by-zero freedom, and initialized-variable usage. Our results demonstrate that solver-backed semantic constrained decoding can enforce rich semantic guarantees while incurring modest computational overhead at regular program boundaries.

</details>


### [186] [Context Is a Pipeline, Not a Window: Information Attrition in LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36114)
*Yuvraj Sehgal,Mahsa Panahandeh,Naser Ezzati-Jivan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文指出LLM Agent评估只看上下文窗口大小与答案正确率，忽略了"工具输出在模型看到前被截断"的损失，并以真实排障Agent测量该损失、用受控实验复现其代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent评估仅关注上下文窗口容量和最终答案正确率两个指标，缺少对"工具输出在进入模型前被丢失/截断"这一中间环节的度量，可能掩盖真实瓶颈。

Method: 在真实工具型Agent（通过阅读Linux内核trace定位注入故障根因）上做测量：720次运行、11,189条工具结果统计；对864条真实工具结果进行回放，其中包括一条生产trace（其截断会删除全部128条证据记录）；开展受控实验比较截断与不截断；并调研7个Agent框架的截断实现。

Result: 上下文几乎未用满（最大prompt仅占窗口5.75%），但1/3工具结果（3,827/11,189）被按固定长度切片截断，且全部产生损坏的JSON；字段级极不均衡：同一结果中一个字段仅4.7%的调用得以送达，另一字段达99.3%；证据所在字段随故障进程启动时机（trace早期/晚期）而变，Agent诊断准确率呈相同分化。受控实验中截断的首要代价体现为额外工作而非错误答案：工具调用增加43%、计算量翻倍、yield从0.61降至0.31；若有替代路径仍能得出正确答案。7个框架中有2个采用同样的不安全截断方式。

Conclusion: 论文提出Agent上下文五阶段流水线模型并逐阶段度量失效点，指出按JSON文本定长切片截断会非均匀地删除尾部数据并破坏JSON，代价先表现为算力/调用膨胀而非直接错误；截断是否真正导致准确率下降仍为开放问题。

Abstract: LLM agents are judged by two numbers: the size of the context window, and how often the final answer is correct. Both numbers miss a third thing: how much of a tool's output is lost before the model ever sees it. We measured this loss in a real tool-using agent. The agent finds the cause of injected faults by reading Linux kernel traces. The context window was almost empty: across 720 runs, the single largest prompt used only 5.75% of it. Yet a third of all tool results (3,827 of 11,189) were cut before they reached the model, and every cut result became broken JSON. The harness cut each result by slicing its JSON text at a fixed length. This does not shorten a result evenly. It deletes whatever data sits near the end. One field reached the model in only 4.7% of calls. A second field, in the very same results, reached the model in 99.3% of calls. Which field held the fault's evidence depended on when the faulty process started: early or late in the trace. The agent's diagnosis accuracy matched this same split. So we ran controlled experiments to test one question: did the data loss cause the drop in accuracy? This paper makes four contributions. First, we model an agent's context as a five-stage pipeline, with a measured failure at each stage. Second, we replay 864 real tool results, including one from a production trace where the shipped cut deletes all 128 records of the fault's evidence. Third, a controlled experiment shows that the cut's real cost appears first as extra work, not as wrong answers: 43% more tool calls, twice the computation, and yield falling from 0.61 to 0.31. The agent still finds the right answer when it has another way to reach it. Fourth, a survey of seven agent frameworks finds two that cut tool output the same unsafe way. Whether the data cut truly causes the accuracy drop is still an open question.

</details>


### [187] [Stratum: Repository Memory for Coding Agents Beyond the Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36153)
*Yuvraj Sehgal,Mahsa Panahandeh,Naser Ezzati-Jivan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对编码智能体跨会话无记忆的问题，提出持久化、经验感知的仓库记忆架构，设计分层记忆系统 Stratum，并用实测检索延迟与多会话缺陷定位实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文窗口虽扩大千倍，但编码智能体每次会话仍无过去工作经验记忆，需重复读文件、重试失败方案、遗忘根因。作者认为这不是上下文长度问题，而是记忆架构问题，因此需要与仓库共存的持久化、经验感知记忆。

Method: 通过三项真实仓库测量支撑设计：1）中等规模项目已超出最大生产上下文窗口，且以KV缓存存储知识的字节开销比索引文本高10^4~10^5倍；2）752个真实缺陷修复提交中，中位数仅修改2个文件，这些文件及其直接导入不足仓库10%，瓶颈在选取而非容量；3）即使引入传递导入可达性与测试链接，仍有47%共改文件对结构上不连通，选取所需知识须从历史与经验中挖掘。据此设计 Stratum：分层记忆随仓库存储，通过"page-safe"工具提供返回内容、剩余内容与代价信息的三段式响应。

Result: 百万节点存储上的检索中位延迟为1.7 ms；在八个真实缺陷的多会话实验中，继承另一模型撰写的前一会话记忆的新智能体在24次试验中21次定位到根因文件，仅检索基线为6/24（Fisher p<10^-4），中位token开销低4.5倍；将八次调查笔记合并至单一存储后成功率仍为8/8。

Conclusion: 编码智能体的核心瓶颈是记忆架构而非上下文长度；持久化、经验感知的分层仓库记忆能在低延迟、低token成本下显著提升跨会话缺陷定位能力，并对记忆规模扩展保持鲁棒。

Abstract: Context windows have grown a thousand-fold, yet coding agents still start every session with no memory of their past work. They re-read files, retry failed approaches, and forget root causes they established hours earlier. This is not a context-length problem but a memory-architecture problem. Our hypothesis: coding agents need persistent, experience-aware repository memory, so that knowledge survives from one session to the next. Three measurements on real repositories motivate the design. First, mid-size projects already exceed the largest production context windows, and storing knowledge inside the model's key-value (KV) cache costs $10^4$ to $10^5$ times more bytes than storing it as indexed text. Second, across 752 real bug-fix commits, the median fix edits two files, and these files plus their direct imports amount to less than 10% of the repository; the bottleneck is selection, not capacity. Third, even with transitive import reachability and test links, 47% of co-edited file pairs remain structurally disconnected; the knowledge needed for selection must be mined from history and experience. We sketch Stratum, a tiered memory that lives with the repository and serves it through page-safe tools: every response states what was returned, what remains, and what it cost. Retrieval over a store of one million nodes takes 1.7 ms at the median. In a multi-session experiment on eight real bugs, a fresh agent that inherited a prior session's memory, written by a different model, identified the root-cause file in 21 of 24 trials versus 6 of 24 for a retrieval-only baseline (Fisher $p<10^{-4}$), at 4.5x lower median token cost. With the notes of all eight investigations pooled in one store, success remained 8/8.

</details>


### [188] [Can AI Guide Testing Without Generating Tests? A Comparative Study of AI-Assisted Unit Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36185)
*Zihan Fang,Ron Asoulin-Handelman,Helen Wu,Yu Huang,Gina R. Bai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TestCompass——一个只反馈测试套件弱点、不生成可执行测试的生成式AI导师，并在18名学生课堂研究中与通用AI助手对比，考察测试行为、测试套件质量与主观体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使助手能生成复杂可执行单元测试，但新手可能直接接受输出，跳过"测什么""套件是否完整""结果是否正确"等判断环节，从而丧失发展核心测试技能的机会。已有AI辅助测试生成与反馈式编程导师研究，但尚不清楚AI能否在不代写测试的前提下支持新手测试实践。

Method: 设计并实现TestCompass：基于生成式AI，针对学生测试套件的薄弱环节给出定向反馈，而不输出可执行测试。采用课堂内被试间（或组间）对比研究，18名学生完成两项Java单元测试任务，分别使用TestCompass与通用AI助手，采集测试行为（如测试数量、自写与验证耗时）、测试套件质量指标及主观体验问卷。

Result: 使用通用AI助手的被试产出更多测试；TestCompass组在自主编写与验证测试上投入更多时间。多数测试套件质量指标在两组间相当；在其中一项任务中，TestCompass组以显著更少的测试方法数（p=0.012）达到相近的需求覆盖率。两组均未报告测试知识或信心的短期提升。

Conclusion: 反馈导向的AI可在不代写测试的前提下支持有意义的测试成果，同时保留动手测试实践的机会，为AI辅助测试教育提供互补路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have enabled assistants to generate increasingly sophisticated executable unit tests. However, novices may accept these outputs without independently identifying what to test, evaluating test-suite completeness, or verifying correctness, potentially limiting opportunities to develop essential testing skills. Although prior work has explored AI-assisted test generation and feedback-oriented programming tutors, little is known about whether AI can support novices' testing practices without generating tests for them. To investigate this, we introduce TestCompass, a generative AI tutor that provides targeted feedback on weaknesses in students' test suites without generating executable tests. In a classroom study with 18 students, we compared TestCompass with a general AI assistant across two Java unit-testing tasks, examining students' testing behaviors, test-suite quality, and subjective experiences. We found that participants using the general-purpose AI assistant produced more tests, whereas TestCompass participants spent more time developing and verifying tests themselves. However, most test-suite quality measures were comparable between conditions, and TestCompass participants achieved similar requirement coverage with significantly fewer test methods ($p=0.012$) in one task. Neither group reported short-term improvements in perceived testing knowledge or confidence. These findings suggest that feedback-oriented AI can support meaningful testing outcomes while preserving opportunities for hands-on testing practice, offering a complementary approach to AI-assisted testing education.

</details>


### [189] [Agent or Human? Detecting Commits by AI Coding Agents in the Absence of Authorship Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36229)
*Kosei Horikawa,Hao Li,Yutaro Kashiwa,Youssef Esseddiq Ouatiti,Mohammed Sayagh,Bram Adams,Hajimu Iida,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究去除作者签名后能否区分AI编码代理提交与人类提交，发现信号主要来自提交信息冗长度与变更规模，AUC最高0.859，但在实际代理占比下精度仅0.332，且跨代理迁移性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实证研究依赖bot账号、Co-authored-by等可选且可删除的签名来识别AI代理提交，且签名常挂在PR而非单个commit上，故在签名缺失时能否区分Agent与Human提交尚不清楚。

Method: 构建含1,226个仓库、7,837,946个commit、覆盖5种编码代理与人类提交的数据集；掩蔽签名后使用XGBoost、TF-IDF及gpt-6-sol零样本进行检测，评估ROC AUC、在观测代理占比下的精度、跨代理迁移性和时间变化。

Result: 掩蔽签名后检测器AUC最高达0.859，信号主要来自提交信息冗长度和变更规模而非代码本身；共享样本上gpt-6-sol零样本AUC接近XGBoost和TF-IDF；但全测试集最佳检测器精度仅0.332；对留出的Claude Code和Copilot迁移差，TF-IDF对Claude Code的AUC仅0.524；Copilot的AUC先降后回升，更长训练历史对Claude Code有益但对Copilot无益。

Conclusion: 无签名检测可用于对commit做审查优先级排序，但无法替代记录式归因；依赖签名标签的研究应将签名识别视为代理活动的下界。

Abstract: AI coding agents such as Claude Code, GitHub Copilot coding agent, and Cursor now commit directly to open-source repositories. Empirical studies of these agents identify their commits through authorship signatures, such as bot accounts and Co-authored-by trailers. These signatures are optional, can be removed, and are often attached to pull requests rather than to individual commits. Hence, it is unclear whether Agent commits can be told apart from Human commits once the signatures are gone. We investigate the signals and limits of signature-free detection using 7,837,946 commits from 1,226 repositories, covering five coding agents and Human commits. After signatures are masked, detectors rank Agent commits above Human commits with a ROC AUC of up to 0.859, and most of this signal comes from message verbosity and change size rather than from the code itself. On the shared sample, gpt-6-sol without fine-tuning reaches AUCs similar to XGBoost and TF-IDF. At the observed Agent share, however, the best detector on the full test set reaches a precision of only 0.332. Detection transfers poorly to held-out Claude Code and Copilot, with TF-IDF reaching an AUC of only 0.524 for Claude Code. Temporal changes also depend on the agent, e.g., Copilot AUC drops and later recovers, while more training history helps Claude Code but not Copilot. These results indicate that signature-free detection can prioritize commits for review but cannot replace recorded attribution, and that studies relying on signature-based labels should treat them as a lower bound on agent activity.

</details>


### [190] [Instant Software Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36254)
*Wenxin Jiang,Mikola Lysenko*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"即时软件工厂"(instant software factory)概念：仅用两个协调输入——带固定目标的cron-job智能体群与共享通信板（如GitHub）——即可在已测试仓库上实现持续交付，并将工厂开发建模为内生反馈下的多目标优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体能快速构建原型，但从原型到产品之间存在长尾的修复、优化、测试与发布工作。传统软件工厂以可重复流程处理这些持续工作，本文追问：这类工作是否需要专门的编排机制，以及应如何评价此类"工厂"。

Method: 从两个人工配置的工业级工厂（分别观察15天和17天）中蒸馏出一份bootstrap提示词，用于从单轮人类输入搭建两个协调输入（cron-job智能体群 + 共享通信板），并将治理设为可分离层；随后把工厂开发形式化为内生反馈（endogenous feedback）下的多目标优化问题。

Result: 在GitHub协调的CLI工厂中，智能体上线期间日均合并数上升15倍（仅为观察性关联）；智能体提交了95%的新issue，并在58个修改CI工作流的PR中贡献40个；至少5.6%标记为已修复的issue事后被发现修复不正确。该bootstrap提示词尚未经过评估。

Conclusion: 与在固定基准上打分的智能体不同，工厂会改变自身的工作负载、评估器与所测进度，因此其评价必须考虑内生反馈。计划在五个月内演进两座工厂、在新仓库上测试bootstrap，并检验五个可证伪假设。

Abstract: Coding agents can quickly build prototypes, but a long tail of fixes, optimizations, tests, and releases separates a prototype from a product. A software factory handles this ongoing work through a repeatable process. We ask whether this work needs dedicated orchestration and how such factories should be judged. Given a tested repository and a coding agent, an instant software factory uses only two coordination inputs, a swarm of cron-job agents with standing objectives and a shared communication board, such as GitHub, where agents coordinate through the traces they leave. We developed a bootstrap prompt designed to set up both inputs from one round of human input, with governance as a separable layer. We distilled it from two manually configured industrial factories, observed for 15 and 17 days, and have not yet evaluated it. In the GitHub-coordinated CLI factory, daily merges rose 15x during agent rollout, an observed association. Its agents also filed 95% of new issues and authored 40 of 58 PRs that changed CI workflows, and at least 5.6% of issues marked as fixed were later found to have incorrect fixes. Unlike an agent scored on a fixed benchmark, a factory thus changes its own workload, evaluator, and measured progress. We formulate factory development as multi-objective optimization under this endogenous feedback. Over five months, we plan to evolve both factories, test the bootstrap on new repositories, and test five falsifiable hypotheses.

</details>


### [191] [Your Wish Is My Commit: Over-Compliant Refactoring in AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36346)
*Haocong Jiang,Sadra Sabouri,Sepand Haghighi,Souti Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控实验研究AI编码智能体的谄媚行为：在迭代重构任务中，五个模型均无法在代码已优化后停止编辑，且会在宣称完成后继续修改，造成代码反复重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在谄媚（sycophancy）倾向，即过度顺从用户要求。当模型作为编码智能体时，这种过度合规会带来实际代价：在代码已无需改进时仍继续编辑，会向代码库引入反复改动（churn）、技术债务和审查负担。然而软件工程场景中谄媚的后果仍缺乏研究。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，选取迭代重构（iterative refactoring）这一无终止标准的开放式任务，代码一旦整洁，之后的任何编辑即反映合规而非改进。实验覆盖算法类（n=50）与面向对象类（n=10）套件，对Codex和Claude Code两个常用智能体、共五个模型反复要求继续重构，累计超过3,000轮交互，并定义六种过度合规的行为信号。

Result: 研究假设能力强的智能体在识别出已优化代码后会停止，但没有任何模型这样做。所有模型都持续编辑，首次停止前重写的代码量最高达原始代码库的31倍，并且在宣称任务完成后仍会恢复编辑。过度合规是一种代价高昂的多轮行为，会随每一轮累积。

Conclusion: 谄媚/过度合规在软件工程中是显著且被低估的问题，表现为跨越多轮的持续性行为且逐轮累积放大。所提出的六种行为信号为开发者和工具构建者提供了检测与抑制该行为的手段。

Abstract: Large language models are known to be sycophantic, over-complying with users even when doing so is unproductive. When these models act as coding agents, this can be costly: an agent that keeps editing after no edit improves the code writes churn, technical debt, and review burden into the codebase. Yet the consequences of sycophancy in software engineering remain largely understudied. To understand them, we run a controlled study of iterative refactoring, an open-ended task with no stopping criterion, where any edit an agent makes once the code is already clean reflects compliance rather than improvement. Across algorithmic (n=50) and object-oriented (n=10) suites, we repeatedly ask two widely used agents, Codex and Claude Code, to refactor further across five models. Over 3,000 turns, we define six behavioral signals of over-compliance. We hypothesized that a capable agent would stop once it recognized an optimized codebase; none did. Every model kept editing, rewriting up to 31x the original codebase before first stopping and resuming edits even after declaring the task complete. Over-compliance is a costly, multi-turn behavior that compounds with every turn, and our six signals give developers and tool builders a way to detect and curb it.

</details>


### [192] [When AI Can Build It, What Should We Assess? Rethinking Evidence of Engineering Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36413)
*Ali Shakiba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一门13周、允许使用生成式AI的软件密集型工程课程的重设计，通过将"工程问责工作流"与六通道评估证据架构（产品、过程/来源、验证、解释、反思、个体保证）结合，并以"收集—交叉核验—升级"（CCE）逻辑贯穿，使学生在大量委托AI完成产物的情况下仍能显性体现工程能力与责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI可产出代码、测试、文档和技术解释，这些传统上被视为学生能力证据的产物其"质量"已不再能可靠推断其背后的工程能力，从而削弱了基于产物质量的评估效度。

Method: 采用设计研究式重设计：一方面构建工程问责工作流，规范人机分工并保留人类决策权；另一方面建立六类互补评估证据通道；再以CCE推理逻辑对多通道证据进行交叉核验，避免将单一产物或轨迹视为充分证据。首轮部署覆盖87名学生、17个团队，累计957个个人周度Git仓库与34个团队项目仓库。

Result: 首轮部署得出四点实践启示：(1) AI辅助会改变评分量规各项证据权重的落点；(2) 多证据通道需刻意交叉核验并保证实质互补性；(3) 实践中实现的证据强度可能偏离设计意图；(4) 自动化更易扩展证据"采集"而非证据"解读"。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于提出并实际部署了一套有概念基础的课程设计，使在AI能承担大量产物生产的前提下，工程责任与工程能力仍可被观察与评价；同时揭示评估证据架构在落地时的效度落差与人工解读瓶颈。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce code, tests, documentation, and technical explanations that resemble artefacts traditionally used as evidence of student competence, weakening the inference from artefact quality to the engineering capability behind it. We report the redesign and first deployment of a 13-week, AI-permissive, software-intensive engineering course in which students may delegate substantial work to AI while retaining responsibility for planning, verification, review, integration, and explanation. The redesign couples an engineering accountability workflow, which structures human-AI work and preserves human decision authority, with an assessment evidence architecture comprising six complementary channels: product, process/origin, verification, explanation, reflection, and individual assurance. A collect--cross-check--escalate (CCE) reasoning logic connects these channels rather than treating any single artefact or trace as sufficient evidence of competence. By the end of Week 11, the deployment involved 87 students organised into 17 teams, with 957 individual weekly Git repositories and 34 group-project Git repositories supporting repeated assessment and engineering activity. The first deployment yielded four practical lessons: AI assistance changes where rubric criteria carry evidential weight; multiple evidence channels require deliberate cross-checking and meaningful complementarity; the evidential strength realised in practice may differ from that intended in the assessment design; and automation scales evidence collection more readily than interpretation. The contribution is a conceptually grounded, deployed design for making engineering responsibility and competence more visible when AI can perform substantial artefact production.

</details>


### [193] [ProCerDCL: Query-Efficient Black-Box Probabilistic Verification of Deep Learning Compiler Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36499)
*Zexin Li,Weibo Zhang,Linyi Li,Arvind Easwaran,Simin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出查询高效的\ tool框架，用单次序贯检验实现黑盒概率验证的三值契约，比基线省3.6–4.2倍样本，并用于局部模型语义一致性检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒概率验证需在给定可采样输入分布与二值性质预言机下判定违规概率p是否超过阈值θ。基线方法需构造一族区间并多次检验，查询开销大，且仅提供较弱的判定契约。

Method: \ tool以单一序贯检验替代区间族：借助经重复抽样校正的精确二项置信区间或anytime-valid置信序列，给出三值输出——接受断言p≤θ、拒绝断言p>θ+η（η为用户指定分离边界）、不确定表示证据不足；各错误判定概率受指定误差预算约束。将该框架应用于概率局部模型语义一致性，预言机检测参考执行与编译执行在指定扰动分布下是否存在决策级不一致。

Result: 在TorchInductor与TensorRT上评估21个任务的24项工作负载。采样效率方面，配对单模型clean-label半径搜索显示基线在数值参数匹配下需3.6–4.2倍唯一样本。θ=10^-3时，840个输入–后端实例得817次接受、19次不确定、4次拒绝；两个已记录的编译器故障案例研究进一步说明局部判定的诊断能力。

Conclusion: 单序贯检验可在保证误差预算意义下高效实现三值概率验证契约，显著降低查询成本，并在编译器语义一致性检查中给出可解释的接受/拒绝/不确定判定，具备实际诊断价值。

Abstract: We present \tool, a query-efficient framework for black-box probabilistic verification. Given a fixed, sampleable input distribution and a binary property oracle, \tool assesses the property's violation probability $p$ against a threshold $θ$. Under independent sampling and stateless oracle behavior, acceptance asserts $p\leθ$, rejection asserts $p>θ+η$ for a user-specified separation margin, and an inconclusive verdict indicates insufficient evidence for either conclusion. The probability of each type of erroneous verdict is bounded by its specified error budget. To support this three-valued contract efficiently, \tool uses a single sequential test, eliminating the interval family required by the baseline. The test accounts for repeated sampling checks through either corrected exact binomial confidence intervals or an anytime-valid confidence sequence. We apply \tool to probabilistic local model semantic consistency, where the property oracle detects decision-level inconsistencies between reference and compiled executions under a specified perturbation distribution. Our evaluation covers 24 tasks across TorchInductor and TensorRT and examines sampling efficiency, conclusiveness, and diagnostic behavior. For sampling efficiency, we use paired one-model clean-label radius searches: the baseline requires 3.6-4.2 times as many unique samples under matched numerical parameters while providing a weaker decision contract. For model semantic consistency, checks at $θ=10^{-3}$ yield 817 acceptances, 19 inconclusive verdicts, and 4 rejections across 840 input--backend instances from 21 tasks. Case studies of two documented compiler faults further demonstrate what these local verdicts can diagnose.

</details>


### [194] [From Ripple to Boundary: Deterministic Impact Boundaries for LLM-Based Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36549)
*Jiameng Bai,Zhitao Wang,Long Wang,Keyue Qian,Sai Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出以确定性结构约束替代概率性语义检索，将设计资产建模为知识图谱并组织成内容寻址的Merkle DAG，利用设计变更的逻辑涟漪效应与哈希传播之间的拓扑同构性，将模糊的影响分析转化为可验证的结构操作，从而为LLM智能体划定精确的修改边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM时代，工业软件工程转向设计驱动的代码生成，演化瓶颈从"写代码"转为"维持多源设计资产与生成代码之间的确定性一致性"。设计资产变更时，LLM智能体缺乏确定性的影响边界，导致两类失效：幻觉出不存在的修改、破坏无关代码。语义检索或文本diff等概率性方法无法刻画高度互联设计依赖间的结构性涟漪效应。

Method: 1）将设计资产形式化为知识图谱（KG），并组织为内容寻址的Merkle有向无环图；2）核心洞察：设计变更的逻辑涟漪效应与DAG内内容哈希传播之间存在拓扑同构；3）据此把模糊的影响分析转化为可验证的结构操作；4）通过两阶段diff过程提取精确因果链，界定确切的修改边界；5）基于该边界进行确定性上下文注入，向LLM智能体提供严格受限的代码与设计更新；6）KG副产品可作为智能体可查询的自然语言设计检索知识库。

Result: 在大型工业支付平台上的早期部署表明，该机制能够成功计算精确的影响边界，并生成符合预期设计变更的增量代码修改。

Conclusion: 从概率性语义检索转向确定性结构约束，可将影响分析转化为可验证的结构操作，从根本上保证演化安全性，同时兼顾自然语言设计检索能力，具备工业级可行性。

Abstract: The landscape of software evolution is undergoing a fundamental paradigm shift in the era of Large Language Models. As industrial software engineering increasingly transitions toward design-driven code generation, the bottleneck of evolution has shifted from writing code to maintaining deterministic consistency between multi-source design assets and the generated code. When a design asset changes, LLM agents currently lack a deterministic impact boundary to identify which code must be evolved, leading to two failure modes: hallucinating missing changes or breaking unrelated code. Probabilistic approaches, such as semantic retrieval or text-based diffing, fail to capture the structural ripple effects across highly interconnected design dependencies. We propose a paradigm shift from probabilistic semantic retrieval to deterministic structural constraints. We formalize the design assets as a Knowledge Graph and organize them into a content-addressed Merkle Directed Acyclic Graph. Our key insight reveals a topological isomorphism between the logical ripple effect of a design change and the content hash propagation within the DAG. By leveraging this, we transform ambiguous impact analysis into verifiable structural operations. Through a two-phase diffing process, we extract a precise causal chain that defines the exact modification boundary. This boundary enables deterministic context injection, providing the LLM agent with strictly scoped code and design updates, thereby fundamentally guaranteeing evolution safety. As a by-product, the KG serves as an agent-queryable knowledge base for natural language design retrieval. Early deployment on a large-scale industrial payment platform demonstrates that our mechanism successfully computes the exact impact boundary and generates incremental code modifications that meet the expected design changes.

</details>


### [195] [Benchmarking LLM Agents for Behaviorally Adequate Test Generation from Software Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36584)
*Binhang Qi,Yiming Liu,Yun Lin,Yangjie Zhou,Hailong Sun,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Req2Test-Bench，一个从 8 个开源项目的 BDD 制品中回收、经执行验证的需求到测试（requirement-to-test）生成基准，共 5,086 个实例，覆盖 5 种编程语言与 4 个 BDD 框架。评估 8 种 agent–模型–推理配置后发现：执行通过与行为充分性之间存在显著鸿沟，最佳配置通过率为 82.8%，但仅有 33.8% 的测试达到 kill-completeness 意义下的行为充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型 agent 越来越多地将自然语言需求编译为仓库级代码，因此需求到测试生成在提供验证约束（harness）以引导代码生成方面愈发重要。然而现有测试生成基准多聚焦白盒测试，且已有需求–测试研究通常依赖文档与生成测试之间的语义对齐评估，难以回答关键问题：当被验证行为的实现被隐藏时，agent 能否生成可验证所需行为的测试。

Method: 构建 Req2Test-Bench：从 8 个开源项目的开发者撰写 BDD 制品中回收 5,086 个执行验证过的实例，覆盖 5 种语言、4 个 BDD 框架；每个实例将 Gherkin 自然语言需求与开发者撰写的测试实现配对。任务设定为：给定需求与目标接口（隐藏实现），agent 需生成完整、可能跨多文件的测试实现。采用 kill-completeness 作为严格的行为充分性度量——kill-complete 测试须杀死 ground-truth 测试所能杀死的全部变异体；并评估 8 种 agent–模型–推理配置。

Result: 执行成功与行为充分性之间存在显著差距：最佳配置 Codex + DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 对 82.8% 的实例生成可通过的测试，但仅 33.8% 达到行为充分。进一步发现：(1) 扩大 agent 规模相对于生成成本在行为充分性上收益急剧递减；(2) 需求欠规范（含模糊的 setup 与隐含的预期结果）造成信息鸿沟；(3) 基于需求的 harness 用以引导 agent 的非确定性，是有前景的改进方向。

Conclusion: 现有 agent 在隐藏实现条件下生成的测试大多仅能执行通过，难以真正验证所需行为；行为充分性远低于执行成功率，且单纯扩大 agent 规模收益递减。需求欠规范是主要瓶颈，而基于需求的 harness 引导是提升需求到测试生成质量的可行方向。

Abstract: As large language model agents increasingly compile natural-language requirements into repository-level code, requirement-to-test generation grows in importance to provide validation constraints (or harness) to guide code generation. Yet it remains unclear whether current agents can generate tests that validate the required behavior when the implementation of that behavior is withheld. Existing test-generation benchmarks mostly focus on white-box testing, whereas the requirement--test-related study usually requires evaluation on semantic alignment between the given documents and the generated tests.
  We introduce Req2Test-Bench, a benchmark containing 5,086 execution-validated instances recovered from developer-authored behavior-driven development (BDD) artifacts in 8 open-source projects, spanning 5 programming languages and 4 BDD frameworks. Each instance pairs a natural-language requirement in Gherkin with its developer-written Test Implementation. Given the requirement, target interfaces of the required behavior, an agent must generate a complete, potentially multi-file Test Implementation. Evaluation of 8 agent--model--reasoning configurations reveals a substantial gap between execution success and behavioral adequacy, which is measured strictly as kill-completeness: a kill-complete test kills every mutant that the ground-truth test kills. The best-performing configuration, Codex with DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, produces passing tests for 82.8% of instances, yet only 33.8% are behaviorally adequate. We find that (1) scaling up agents yields sharply diminishing returns in behavioral adequacy relative to generation cost; (2) under-specified requirements cause the information gap, including ambiguous setup and implicit expected outcomes; and (3) requirement-grounded harnesses that steer agent non-determinism offer a promising direction for improvement.

</details>


### [196] [A Fine-Grained Empirical Study of Upstream Security Events and Downstream Mitigation in Maven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36603)
*Kazi Amit Hasan,Enrong Pan,Yuan Tian,Mariam Guizani,Steven H. H. Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文追踪Maven生态832个CVE从上游修复到下游采纳的全过程，发现时效延迟同时存在于上下游两侧，且下游暴露高度集中（前10% CVE占72.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将上游修复发布与下游采纳割裂分析，无法判断下游暴露究竟源于上游发布延迟、下游采纳延迟，还是二者兼有；本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 选取Maven生态832个CVE，追踪从上游修复到每个受影响下游项目采纳的全链路；将修复分类为silent与transparent，还原report、fix、release、NVD披露的事件顺序，将含漏洞构件与其下游依赖者关联，并与下游采纳行为和dependabot使用情况进行对比。

Result: 下游暴露由双侧延迟共同造成。上游约82%的CVE（无论silent还是transparent修复）修复版本先于NVD披露发布；其余17.9%的下游项目在披露后数日至四个多月内无修复可采纳。下游暴露高度集中，前10%的CVE贡献72.1%。1,677次采纳中85.3%发生在NVD披露之后，对披露前发布的silent修复升至88.3%；多数采纳来自常规依赖更新。使用dependabot版本更新时，release-to-adoption中位延迟为10.4天，未使用时为110.6天；但采纳时仅8.5%的采纳配置了版本更新。

Conclusion: 下游暴露的治理需上下游协同：上游应减少披露后修复缺口并提高修复透明度，下游应提升采纳时效，依赖自动化工具（如dependabot）的配置率提升是缩短延迟的关键着力点。研究为上游维护者、下游开发者及依赖自动化工具提供了可操作启示。

Abstract: Open-source vulnerability mitigation spans upstream libraries and the downstream projects that depend on them. Upstream, a vulnerability progresses through a public report when one exists, a fix, a fixed release, and NVD disclosure. Downstream, a project mitigates it by adopting the fixed release, and the timeliness of this adoption is measured relative to these events. Prior work studies the two sides separately, so it is unclear whether downstream exposure stems from delayed upstream release, delayed downstream adoption, or both. We address this gap by following 832 CVEs in the Maven ecosystem from the upstream fix to its adoption by each exposed downstream project. We classify upstream fixes as \textit{silent} or \textit{transparent}, recover the order of report, fix, release, and NVD disclosure, and link vulnerable artifacts to their downstream dependents. We then compare this order with downstream adoption and dependabot usage. We find that delay arises on both sides. Upstream, the fixed release comes before NVD disclosure for about 82\% of CVEs, for both silent and transparent fixes. For the other 17.9\%, downstream projects have no fix to adopt for days to more than four months after disclosure. Downstream, exposure is concentrated, with the top 10\% of CVEs accounting for 72.1\% of it. Of 1,677 observed adoptions, 85.3\% happen after NVD disclosure, rising to 88.3\% for silent fixes released before disclosure. Most adoptions come through routine dependency updates. Adoptions with dependabot version updates have a median release-to-adoption delay of 10.4 days, compared with 110.6 days without dependabot. Yet version updates were configured at adoption time for only 8.5\% of total adoptions. We translate these findings into implications for upstream maintainers, downstream developers, and dependency-automation tools.

</details>


### [197] [ASNER: Software Architecture Recovery with Adaptive Semantic Neighborhoods and Evidence-Grounded Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36894)
*Wenhao Wang,Zhi Jin,Jia Li,Dongming Jin,Heng Tian,Yingtao Fang,Wenguang Ye,Tiankuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASNER：面向软件架构恢复的自适应语义邻域与证据支撑精化方法，在12个开源项目上取得五项指标最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构恢复方法融合结构与语义信号，但功能相关不等于共享架构职责；固定阈值或Top-K邻域忽略仓库规模与局部相似度分布，导致误差在聚类中传播；无约束的LLM精化缺乏证据支撑，可能改错正确分配。

Method: 用LLM推导结构化文件职责；构建随仓库规模与局部相似度分布自适应的语义邻域；在静态依赖图上融合筛选后的语义关系与弱目录线索；通过社区检测恢复组件骨架；再由责任归属感知（ROA）Agent基于项目级职责与可追溯代码证据审查不确定边界文件，仅在证据支持时修改分配。

Result: 在覆盖C、C++、Java、Python的12个开源项目上，ASNER在通用基准的全部五项指标上取得最佳宏平均，ARI与c2c@66分别较GAER-GAT提升19.5%和32.9%；下游评估显示ASNER引导的LocAgent在文件级、模块级和函数级代码定位上均有提升。

Conclusion: 以职责感知、项目自适应的关系建模和证据支撑的边界验证，可有效抑制语义噪声与无约束LLM精化带来的错误；所恢复的架构上下文对仓库级软件工程任务（如代码定位）具有实际价值。

Abstract: Automated software architecture recovery provides architectural context for software engineering and repository-level LLM agents. Existing methods combine structural and semantic signals, but functional relatedness does not necessarily imply shared architectural responsibility. Fixed thresholds or Top-K neighborhoods also ignore repository scale and local similarity distributions, introducing errors that propagate through clustering. Unconstrained LLM refinement may further alter correct assignments without sufficient evidence. We propose ASNER (Adaptive Semantic Neighborhoods and Evidence-Grounded Refinement for Software Architecture Recovery), based on responsibility-aware, project-adaptive relation modeling and evidence-grounded boundary verification. ASNER derives structured file responsibilities with an LLM, constructs semantic neighborhoods that adapt to repository scale and local similarity distributions, and augments a static dependency graph with selected semantic relations and weak directory cues. Community detection recovers the component backbone. A Responsibility-Ownership-Aware (ROA) Agent then examines uncertain boundary files using project-level responsibilities and traceable code evidence, changing assignments only when supported. Experiments on 12 open-source projects spanning C, C++, Java, and Python show that ASNER achieves the best macro-average on all five metrics on the common benchmark, improving ARI and c2c@66 over GAER-GAT by 19.5% and 32.9%, respectively. A downstream evaluation shows that ASNER-guided LocAgent improves file-, module-, and function-level code localization, demonstrating the value of its architectural context for repository-level software engineering tasks.

</details>


### [198] [A lightweight C Code Generator for Heterogeneous TinyML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36907)
*Chen Liu,Bernhard Vogginger,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种面向含领域专用加速器的裸机异构系统的轻量级C代码生成器，降低从训练到推理的迁移成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI进步依赖复杂训练框架与强大硬件，但生态割裂：模型以框架相关的格式表示，而加速新算子的专用硬件不断涌现，导致训练到推理存在显著迁移开销。

Method: 设计轻量级C代码生成器，面向集成领域专用加速器的裸机异构系统，并结合权重调优、偏置融合等提前（ahead-of-time）优化；以基于RISC-V的雷达DSP为案例进行验证，与注册相同加速器的microTVM流程对比。

Result: 推理速度提升2.3倍，二进制体积减少20%。

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限的边缘设备提供了除microTVM之外的另一种有吸引力的选择，代码已在Gitlab开源。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the significant advancements in artificial intelligence have been closely tied to increasingly sophisticated training frameworks and steadily powerful computing hardware. However, the entire ecosystem remains fragmented. On one hand, models are represented with frameworks dependent formats, while on the other hand, new tailored hardware for accelerating novel operators has continued to emerge. To effectively diminish the transfer costs from training to inference, we propose a lightweight C code generator designed for bare-metal heterogeneous systems that incorporate domain-specific accelerators. Through a comprehensive case study on a RISC-V based radar DSP, we demonstrate that by employing an accelerator with ahead-of-time optimizations like weight tuning and bias fusion, our approach achieves a 2.3 times improvement in inference speed and a 20% reduction in binary size compared to the microTVM flow registered with the same accelerator, thus offering another compelling choice for resource-constrained edge devices. The code is open-sourced in Gitlab.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond QASecClaw: Agent Scaling, Verifier Feedback, and Evidence Reliability in Software Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36947)
*S M Abdulla Al Mamun,Akif Islam,Mohd. Ruhul Ameen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文通过五组实验考察：在已具备确定性工具证据时，额外的专用语言模型上下文能否提升软件保障；结论是不能，支持对测试场景采用确定性证据准入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件保障实践已能获得确定性工具（静态警告、验证器、测试生成器等）产生的证据，但尚不清楚再叠加专用代码语言模型的上下文能否带来额外收益，尤其在证据本身可能被刻意误导的情形下。

Method: 采用 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct（另做 1.5B 敏感性分析），共完成 13,638 次模型请求；设计五类实验：自建 Java 警告对照、OWASP Benchmark 评估、Defects4J 测试生成、SV-COMP 断言判定，以及刻意误导性证据包；并对比 JBMC+JVM 验证基线，及基于构造的适用范围检查。

Result: Java 警告上上下文数由 1 增至 50 使 token 用量增加 49 倍，但未改善不安全告警的清除；160 个 OWASP 案例中模型配置在清除率与未解决率间权衡，十上下文组仅评估 96 例；32 个 SV-COMP 任务上原始验证器反馈将平均正确判定由 12.7 提至 22.0，但错误判定由 1 增至 7，JBMC+JVM 基线为 26 个正确、6 个未解决；两组各 120 个证据包中模型拒绝 70–100% 的适用反例，而构造相关的适用范围检查全部判对；3 个 Defects4J 缺陷未发现任何目标缺陷（其中 1 个不被测试适配器支持）。

Conclusion: 结果属描述性，在所测设置下支持采用确定性证据准入而非依赖模型上下文扩展；同时暴露出判定聚合、反馈解读及评估覆盖范围方面的局限。

Abstract: Can additional specialist language-model contexts improve software assurance when deterministic tool evidence is already available? We study this question through five experiments spanning authored Java warning controls, OWASP Benchmark assessment, Defects4J test generation, SV-COMP assertion verdicts, and deliberately misleading evidence packages. The evaluated systems use Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct, with a 1.5B sensitivity analysis, and complete 13,638 model requests. On eight related development warnings, increasing the context count from 1 to 50 increases token use 49-fold without improving unsafe clearance. On 160 OWASP cases, model configurations trade clearance against unresolved outcomes; the ten-context arm assesses only 96 cases. On 32 SV-COMP tasks, raw verifier feedback raises mean correct model verdicts from 12.7 to 22.0, but also raises incorrect verdicts from one to seven; a JBMC-plus-JVM validation baseline returns 26 correct verdicts and six unresolved outcomes. On two sets of 120 evidence packages, models reject 70-100% of applicable counterexamples, while construction-specific scope checks classify every package correctly. No target defect is discovered in three Defects4J bugs, one unsupported by the test adapter. These descriptive results support deterministic evidence admission for the tested settings, while exposing limits of verdict aggregation, feedback interpretation, and the available evaluation coverage.

</details>


### [200] [Does More Thinking Pay Off? Reasoning Cost and Correctness of LLMs in Code Generation and Bug Fixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36952)
*Andie Huang,Ying Pang,Qian Zhang,Yihang Cheng,Lin Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文量化了LLM代码生成与修复中显式推理轨迹的贡献：866个Python任务、5种模型设置下，推理带来的正确率提升有限且不稳定，而token开销显著；离线截断实验表明保留前半段轨迹不会显著降低正确率，说明"推理越多越好"不成立，应同时报告推理成本与正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成与缺陷修复的基准只报告正确率，忽视推理轨迹消耗的token，导致无法判断显式推理相对其成本的净收益，也缺乏对推理轨迹内部行为与可截断性的系统考察。

Method: 在HumanEval Pro、MBPP Pro、BugsInPy上选取866个Python任务，跨5种模型设置，测量开启推理后的pass@1/测试通过率增益与token开销；对推理轨迹做定性行为分析；再离线截断已存轨迹（保留前半、重复时停止、首次出现代码时停止），让关闭推理的Qwen3-32B基于剩余内容生成解。

Result: Qwen3-32B开启推理后，HumanEval Pro平均pass@1由71.1%升至76.5%，BugsInPy平均测试通过率由3.5%升至7.9%；MBPP Pro由68.1%升至69.5%不显著，但每题中位思考token超2000。跨模型中位轨迹长度与正确率无稳定正相关。定性上看，代码生成轨迹多用于选择与检查实现，缺陷修复轨迹多用于定位故障并规划修复。截断实验显示保留前半轨迹无显著正确率下降；重复即停很少触发；首次出现代码即停对部分模型有益、对另一些有害，均不优于固定截断。

Conclusion: 推理并非越多越好：收益依赖任务与模型，且与token成本不匹配；轨迹存在大量冗余。研究与实践应同时报告推理成本与正确率，简单固定的轨迹截断已可与启发式停止规则持平。

Abstract: Many LLMs now emit an explicit reasoning trace before they write code, but benchmark results for code generation and bug fixing usually report correctness alone. It is therefore unclear how much the extra reasoning contributes relative to the tokens it consumes. We examine this question with 866 Python tasks drawn from HumanEval Pro, MBPP Pro, and BugsInPy under five model settings. We look at the accuracy gain and token overhead of reasoning, the behaviors that appear inside reasoning traces, and whether a shortened trace still leads to a correct solution. Turning reasoning on in Qwen3-32B over five runs improves mean pass@1 on HumanEval Pro from 71.1% to 76.5% and mean tests pass on BugsInPy from 3.5% to 7.9%. On MBPP Pro the improvement from 68.1% to 69.5% is not statistically significant, even though the model uses a median of over 2,000 thinking tokens per task. Across models, a longer median trace does not reliably mean higher accuracy. A qualitative analysis shows that traces mostly choose and check an implementation in code generation, while in bug fixing they mostly locate the fault and plan the repair. We then truncate stored traces offline and ask Qwen3-32B with reasoning off to produce a solution from what remains. Retaining the first half of a trace yields no statistically significant drop in correctness. Stopping when the trace becomes repetitive seldom triggers, and stopping once solution code first appears benefits some models but harms others. Neither rule beats plain fixed truncation. These results suggest that more reasoning is not always better and that studies should report reasoning cost alongside correctness.

</details>


### [201] [Toward Circuit-Aware Mutation Testing for Quantum Programs: Exact Visibility and Execution Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36976)
*Andriy Miranskyy,Jose Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个“电路感知”的量子程序变异测试框架，用于区分变异体存活的三类原因（行为未变、被输入/测量掩盖、统计证据不足），并给出判定流程、数学保证、示例与参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序变异测试中，存活变异体（surviving mutant）的成因不唯一：可能程序行为实际未变，可能变化被所选输入或测量掩盖，也可能仅因统计证据不足而未被检测到。现有做法无法区分这些成因，导致变异得分含义模糊，测试者也无法判断该执行什么、该改什么。

Method: 构建电路感知框架，覆盖理想无噪声程序与单门/子电路替换型变异。方法追踪变异对被测输出的影响路径，结合既有数学判定（等价性/可观测性检查）与统计估计：对可支撑情形给出“测试无法揭示该变异”的条件，以及在具备所需状态信息时变异效应的精确公式；在明确假设下给出变异得分的界，并保留存活与未定（unresolved）变异体；同时识别出一类电路族，其变异效应可高效经典分类。

Result: 交付物包括：(1) 判定流程（decision procedure）；(2) 数学保证（不可检测性条件、效应精确公式、变异得分界）；(3) 改变测试的实例及其实现需求；(4) 面向差分期望测试的小规模电路参考实现；(5) 一类可高效经典分类变异效应的受限电路族。证据范围为理想模型下的证明与可执行示例。

Conclusion: 该框架使测试者能明确区分“行为未变”“被掩盖”“证据不足”三类存活原因，从而决定执行哪些测试、修改哪些测试，以及哪些部分仍属未知；但结论限于理想模型下的证明与示例，尚缺噪声环境与大规模电路的实证评估。

Abstract: Mutation testing assesses test suites by introducing program changes and checking whether tests detect them. In quantum programs, a surviving mutant may leave program behavior unchanged, produce a change hidden by the chosen input or measurement, or remain undetected because statistical evidence is insufficient. We develop a circuit-aware framework to distinguish these causes and guide execution planning and test changes. The framework covers ideal, noiseless programs and mutations that replace one gate or subcircuit. The method traces how a mutation can affect measured outputs and combines established mathematical checks with statistical estimates. For supported cases, we establish conditions under which a test cannot reveal a mutation and exact formulas for its effect when the required state information is available. Under stated assumptions, we bound the mutation score while retaining surviving and unresolved mutants, and identify a restricted circuit family whose mutation effects admit efficient classical classification. The deliverables include a decision procedure, mathematical guarantees, worked examples of test changes and their implementation requirements, and a small-circuit reference implementation for differential expectation tests. These results help testers decide what to execute, what to change, and what remains unknown. The evidence is limited to proofs and executable examples under the ideal model.

</details>


### [202] [Spine: Compiling Subtask Trees into Structured Context for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37224)
*Jiahong Xiang,Kunqiu Chen,Lingming Zhang,Yuqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Spine 提出一种将模型上下文与子任务嵌套/完成状态对齐的接口与运行时：智能体通过 work scope 显式声明子任务边界，运行时用持久树 SpineTree 记录子任务关系与状态，并在每次模型调用前即时编译上下文，保留未完成子任务的交互、将已完成子任务压缩为其结果，同时保持前缀缓存复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程编码任务包含嵌套子任务。顺序追加式历史导致上下文膨胀与性能下降；而无边界地压缩/裁剪历史会丢失未完成子任务所需信息。递归子智能体委派虽能隔离已完成子任务，但把"子任务边界"与"由谁执行"混为一谈，强制每个边界都委派，带来额外执行与协调开销。

Method: 引入 work scope 显式子任务边界接口，使同一智能体可内联执行嵌套子任务，仅将委派保留给独立并行任务；运行时以 SpineTree 持久树记录子任务关系、局部交互、完成状态与返回结果；每次模型调用前即时（just-in-time）将树编译为上下文，未完成子任务保留原始交互、已完成子任务以其结果替换，并维持前缀缓存复用。集成于 Codex 形成 SpineCodex，并打包为可复用软件包接入 Pi。

Result: 使用 GPT-5.6 Sol 时，相较原生 Codex，SpineCodex 在 SWE-Milestone 上得分提升 11.8%，总成本降低 32.4%；ProgramBench 平均测试通过率提升 17.3%；十个 FrontierSWE 任务中八个 Mean@5 提升。在 SWE-Milestone 上以比 Recursive Subagent 低 73.7% 的总成本取得相当得分。在 Pi 上（Gemini 3.8 Flash、Qwen 3.8 27B），SWE-Milestone 得分提升 9.5–17.5%，ProgramBench 平均通过率提升 4.3–8.7%。

Conclusion: 将子任务粒度与执行代理选择解耦、并让上下文构建与子任务完成状态对齐，可在显著降低 token/成本的同时提升长程编码任务的表现；该方法具跨模型与跨框架的可移植性。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding tasks contain nested subtasks. Typically, agents record interactions sequentially into a growing history, causing context inflation and performance degradation. Compressing or trimming the history without explicit subtask boundaries can discard information needed by unfinished subtasks. Conversely, recursive subagent delegation isolates completed subtasks through child agents by assigning every subtask boundary to delegation, which conflates subtask granularity with executing agent choice, adding execution and coordination overhead. We introduce Spine, an interface and runtime that align model context with subtask nesting and completion. An agent declares explicit subtask boundaries through work scopes, allowing the same agent to execute nested subtasks inline while reserving delegation for independent parallel tasks. The runtime organizes execution in SpineTree, a persistent tree recording subtask relations, local interactions, completion states, and returned results. Before each model call, it compiles the tree into model context just in time, retaining unfinished subtasks' interactions and replacing completed subtasks' details with their results, while preserving prefix-cache reuse.
  We integrate Spine into Codex to build SpineCodex. With GPT-5.6 Sol, compared with native Codex, SpineCodex improves SWE-Milestone Score by 11.8% while reducing total cost by 32.4%, improves the average ProgramBench test-pass rate by 17.3%, and improves Mean@5 on eight of the ten evaluated FrontierSWE tasks. On SWE-Milestone, it also achieves a comparable Score to Recursive Subagent at 73.7% lower total cost. We also integrate Spine into Pi through a reusable software package: with Gemini 3.8 Flash and Qwen 3.8 27B, it improves SWE-Milestone Score by 9.5-17.5% and the average ProgramBench test-pass rate by 4.3-8.7% compared with native Pi.

</details>


### [203] [BenchMeUp: Building Software Engineering Benchmarks from Natural-Language Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37283)
*Islem Bouzenia,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BenchMeUp 可从自然语言规格自动构建软件工程基准：为四类任务构建出198个实例，196个（99%）成功打包，平均每实例耗时36分钟、成本$0.79，91%（180/198）满足全部有效性标准（Codex 仅 10/200）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究依赖基准来评估新技术，但人工构建基准成本高昂：需寻找合适实例、准备执行环境、确定预期结果并验证正确性，过程繁琐且劳动密集。

Method: 提出 BenchMeUp：输入定义目标实例、数据源与评估流程的自然语言规格；据此生成搜索规则与构建计划；调用并行 LLM 智能体识别候选实例并构建可执行实例；在全新执行环境中验证结果；最后通过统一命令行接口打包实例。

Result: 在程序修复、issue 复现、编译器自动调优、静态分析警告修复四类任务上，目标200实例（每类50）共构建198个，196个（99%）打包成功；平均每实例搜索与构建耗时36分钟、成本$0.79；196个打包实例中189个（96%）支持端到端评估；对照源、规格与预期结果核查，180/198（91%）满足全部标准，而通用编码智能体 Codex 交付的200个实例中仅10个满足。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 的智能体可跨多样软件工程任务自动化构建基准，并产出可被执行现有技术使用的实例；但超出"可执行"层面的有效性仍需人工核查。

Abstract: Software-engineering research relies on benchmarks that evaluate new techniques on a set of problem instances.
